Puer, that is, the appearance. Have similar meanings.

[2]

Dabao, a silver ingot weighing fifty taels.

[3]

Let the animals go to eat green grass.

Four

Xiangzi lay in a small shop in Haidian for three days. His body was hot and cold, his mind was dazed, and there were purple blisters on his gums. He just wanted to drink water and not eat anything. After three days of hunger, his anger subsided and his body became as soft as sugar. I'm afraid it was during these three days that people heard about his relationship with the three camels from talking in his sleep or talking nonsense. When he woke up, he was already "Camel Xiangzi".

Ever since he came to the city, he had been "Xiangzi", as if he had no surname at all; now, "Camel" is placed above "Xiangzi", and no one cares about his surname. He doesn't care whether he has a surname or not. However, three animals were exchanged for only a few dollars, and he was given a nickname. He felt that it was not a good deal.

As soon as he could struggle to stand up, he wanted to go out and take a look. He didn't expect that his legs would be so tired. When he walked to the door of the shop, he sat on the ground as soon as he was weak. He sat there in a daze for a long time, with cold sweat on his head. After enduring it for a while, he opened his eyes, his stomach rang for a while, and he felt a little hungry. He stood up very slowly and found a wonton picker. I asked for a bowl of wontons, but he still sat on the floor. I took a sip of the soup and felt nauseous. I held it in my mouth for a long time and reluctantly swallowed it; I didn't want to drink anymore. However, after a while, the hot soup ran like a thread all the way to his abdomen, causing him to burp twice. He knew he had a life again.

With some food in his stomach, he took care of himself. I have lost a lot of weight, and my torn pants are extremely dirty. He was too lazy to move, but he wanted to regain his cleanliness immediately, and he refused to go into the city with such a look on his face. However, you have to spend money to be clean and tidy. It costs money to shave your head, change clothes, and buy shoes and socks. The thirty-five yuan in my hand shouldn't move at all. Even if it doesn't move at all, it's still far from the amount needed to buy a car! However, he pitied himself. Although I was taken away by the soldiers for only a few days, when I think about it now, everything seems like a nightmare. This nightmare made him much older, as if he suddenly gained several years in one breath. Looking at his big hands and feet, it was obviously his own, but it seemed as if he had suddenly found them somewhere. He was very sad. He didn't dare to think about the grievances and dangers in the past. Although he didn't think about them, they still existed, just like when it was cloudy, he knew it was dark even if he didn't look at the sky. He feels that his body is particularly lovely and he should not suffer too much from himself. He stood up, knowing that his body was still very soft, but he couldn't wait to dress up, as if he could instantly become stronger by shaving his head and changing clothes.

After dressing up, I only spent a total of two yuan and twenty cents. A pair of natural-colored coarse cloth trousers and coat that resembles lining costs one yuan, blue cloth shoes cost eight cents, socks made of thread shawl cost one fifty cents, and a straw hat costs twenty-five cents. The old thing he took off was replaced with two packs of matches. Holding two packs of matches, he walked along the avenue to Xizhimen. He had not gone far before he felt weak and tired. But he bit the bullet. He couldn't ride in a car, and he couldn't ride in a car from any aspect: How could a countryman take ten miles and eight miles as a guide, and besides, he was pulling a cart. Not to mention, it is a joke to be caught by such a small illness based on his own stature and strength; unless he falls down and cannot get up again, he will roll on the ground and roll into the city, and he will never give in! If he couldn't walk into the city today, he thought, Xiangzi would be finished. He only trusts his body, no matter what illness he has!

Unsteadily, he let go. Not far from Haidian, Venus appeared in front of his eyes. Holding on to a willow tree, he calmed down for a long time. He was spinning and fussing for a while, but he still refused to sit down. The rotation of heaven and earth slowly calmed down, and his heart seemed to fall into his own heart from a long distance. Wiping the sweat on his head, he took another step. He had already shaved his head and put on new clothes and new shoes. He thought that he was worthy of himself; then, his legs had to fulfill their responsibilities, let's go! Angrily, he walked to the gate. Seeing the hustle and bustle of the people and horses, hearing the complex and harsh sounds, smelling the smell of dryness, and stepping on the soft and dirty dust, Xiangzi wanted to climb down and kiss the gray and smelly ground, the lovely ground, the place where foreign money grows! He has no parents, brothers, or relatives. His only friend is this ancient city. This city gave him everything. Even when he was hungry here, he was more lovely than in the countryside. There was something to see and hear, and there was light and sound everywhere. As long as he worked hard, there was countless money here, and he could eat and wear all kinds of good things. Here, you can get meat soup and preserved water if you beg for food. In the countryside, you can only get stick noodles. When he arrived at the west side of Gaoliang Bridge, he sat on the river bank and shed a few tears!

The sun is setting in the west, and the old willows on the river are leaning crookedly, with a little golden light hanging on their tips. There isn't much water in the river, but there is a lot of green algae growing there, like a greasy green belt, long, narrow, dark green, and emitting a slightly fishy tidal smell. The wheat on the north bank of the river has sprouted awns, is short and dry, and a layer of dust has fallen on the leaves. The green leaves of the lotus pond in Henan float weakly on the water, and small bubbles appear from time to time on the left and right sides of the leaves. On the bridge to the east, people and cars passed by, especially in the setting sun, and they seemed to feel uneasy as dusk approached. These are very interesting and cute in Xiangzi's eyes and ears. Only such a small river can be regarded as a river; such trees, wheat, lotus leaves, and bridges can be regarded as trees, wheat, lotus leaves, and bridges. Because they all belong to Peking.

Sitting there, he was not busy anymore. Everything in front of him was familiar and lovely, and he seemed happy to just sit and die. After resting for a long time, Xiangzi went to Qiaotou and ate a bowl of old tofu: vinegar, soy sauce, pepper oil, minced chives. The hot snow-white tofu was scalded, and it gave off a slightly fragrant smell. After taking a bite, the tofu burned his body; he added two small spoons of chili oil himself. After finishing a bowl, his waistband was soaked with sweat. With half-closed eyes, he handed the bowl out: "One more bowl!"

Standing up, he felt like a human again. The sun was still at its lowest point in the west, and the river water was slightly reddish by the sunset. He was so happy that he wanted to shout out. He touched the smooth scar on his face, touched the money in his bag, and glanced at the sunshine on the turret. He forced himself to forget about his illness and everything else. As if he had some wish, he decided to walk into the city.

The city gate was crowded with all kinds of cars and people. No one dared to move quickly, but everyone wanted to pass quickly. The sound of whips, shouts, curses, horns, bells, and laughter were all buzzed together by the door - like a loudspeaker. It seemed that everyone had a voice, and they all buzzed. Xiangzi thrust his big feet one step east, one step west, and moved his hands left and right, squeezing into the city like a big, thin fish leaping with the waves. He saw Xinjiekou at a glance. The road was so wide and straight. His eyes shone as brightly as the reflection on the roofs to the east. He nodded.

His belongings were still at Xi'anmen Street and the depot, so naturally he wanted to go there. Because he had no family, he always lived in a car factory, although he didn't always drive cars there. Renhe's boss Liu Siye is almost seventy years old; old people are not honest. When he was young, he served as a soldier, set up a casino, bought and sold people, and let the king of hell off the hook. Fourth Master Liu has all the qualifications and abilities required for this kind of business - strength, mentality, means, communication, business name, etc. In the early Qing Dynasty, I fought in groups, robbed good women, and knelt on iron ropes. Kneeling on the iron rope, Liu Si did not frown or say spare his life. The lawsuit taught him to stand up, which is called "font size". After being released from prison, he happened to enter the Republic of China. The patrol force became more and more powerful. Mr. Liu realized that the heroes on the ground had become a thing of the past. Even if Huang Tianba was reincarnated, he would not have much chance. He opened a car factory. Coming from a humble background, he knew how to deal with the poor, when to tighten up and where to loosen up. He had a genius for mobilization. None of the coachmen dared to play tricks on him. He glares and laughs with him, which can make people confused, as if they are in heaven and hell, and have no choice but to listen to his manipulations. So far, he has more than 60 cars, and even the worst ones are 70% to 80% new. He doesn’t keep any broken cars. When it comes to renting a car, his car is bigger than others, but during the third quarter, he can rent it for two days longer than others. The man and the factory had a place to live, and the bachelor who pulled his cart could live there for free - but he had to pay his share in the cart. If he couldn't pay the bills and was tired of him, he would take off his bedclothes and throw the person out the door like a broken kettle. If you have an urgent matter or illness, you only need to tell him, and he will be unequivocal and enthusiastically help in every situation. This is called "Zihao".

Mr. Liu Si is a tiger. He is almost seventy, and his back is not bent. He can still walk ten or twenty miles with his legs. Two big round eyes, a big nose, a square mouth, a pair of big tiger teeth, and a mouth like a tiger. He was almost as tall as Xiangzi, with a shaved head and no beard. He calls himself a tiger, but unfortunately he has no son, only a thirty-seven or eighteen-year-old tiger girl - anyone who knows Fourth Master Liu must also know the tiger girl. She also looks tough, so she scares men. She is good at helping her father, but no one dares to marry her. She is just like a man in everything. She even scolds people with the ease of a man, sometimes with more tricks. Fourth Master Liu fights outside and Huniu fights inside. Father and daughter manage Renhe and the depot like an iron tube. Renhe Factory has become an authority in the world of foreign cars. The methods of the Liu family and their daughters are often mentioned in the mouths of car drivers and car owners, just like the quotations from scholars.

Before buying his own car, Xiangzi drove Renhe Factory's car. He left his savings to Liu Siye for storage. After collecting enough money, he wanted to come over and buy the new car.

"Master Liu, look at my car!" Xiangzi pulled the new car to Renhe Factory. The old man glanced at the car and nodded: "Don't leave!"

"I still have to live here. If I pay more, I can get a monthly subscription before I go to the residential building!" Xiangzi said proudly.

"Okay!" Fourth Master Liu nodded again.

So, Xiangzi found a monthly salary and went to Zhuoshan; when he got out of work, he went to work as a guest, so he lived in Renhechang.

According to other coachmen, it is a rare thing to be able to live in Renhe Factory instead of pulling Liu Siye's cart. Therefore, some people even speculated that Xiangzi must be related to Old Man Liu; some even said that Old Man Liu probably fell in love with Xiangzi and wanted to find a "little man" for Huniu to recruit a son-in-law. Although there is a bit of jealousy in this conjecture, if this is really the case, once Mr. Liu dies in the future, Renhe Factory will definitely belong to Xiangzi. This taught them to only dare to make wild guesses, but not to dare to say anything in front of Xiangzi that would not be listened to. In fact, Old Man Liu had another reason for his preferential treatment of Xiangzi. Xiangzi is a person who can maintain old habits in a new environment. If he becomes a soldier, he will never put on that tiger skin and immediately stop pretending to be stupid and bully others. In the car factory, he was not idle. As soon as he stopped sweating, he would find something to do. He cleans the car, pumps the gas, dries the raincoat, and greases the car... He doesn't need anyone's orders, he does it himself, and he does it happily, as if it's a great entertainment. There were about twenty rickshaw drivers living near the factory. After collecting the carts, everyone was either sitting and chatting, or sleeping with their heads covered. Xiangzi, Xiangzi was the only one whose hands were not idle. When they first came here, everyone thought he was being courteous to Fourth Master Liu and trying to curry favor with others; after a few days, they saw that he had no intention of trying to please others, he was so sincere and natural, and they had nothing to say. Old Man Liu never praised him or looked at him twice; the old man knew it well. He knew that Xiangzi was a good man, and even if he didn't pull his cart, he would still like Xiangzi to be in the factory. With Xiangzi here, let alone the other courtyards and entrances, they are always kept clean. Huniu liked this silly big man more. Xiangzi always listened carefully to what she said and did not argue with her. The other coachmen always spoke sideways because they had suffered so much. She was not afraid of them at all, but she did not want to pay any attention to them. Therefore, all her words were left to Xiangzi. When Xiangzi went to ask for a monthly subscription, the Liu family seemed to have lost a friend. When he came back, even the old man's curses seemed more cheerful and charitable.

Xiangzi took two packs of matches and entered Renhe Factory. It's not dark yet, and the Liu family's father and daughter are having dinner. Seeing him come in, Huniu put down her chopsticks: "Xiangzi! Did you let the wolf pick it up, or did you go to Africa to dig gold mines?" "Huh!" Xiangzi didn't say anything.

Fourth Master Liu's big round eyes wandered around Xiangzi and said nothing. Xiangzi, wearing a new straw hat, sat opposite them.

"If you haven't eaten yet, let's eat together!" Huniu seemed to be entertaining a good friend.

Xiangzi didn't move, and suddenly felt an indescribable affection in his heart. He has always regarded Renhe Factory as his home: when he rents a house on a monthly basis, the owner often changes; when he rents a place to sit, the seats change from time to time; this is the only place where he is always allowed to live, and there are always people chatting with him. Now that he had just escaped with his life, he came back to an acquaintance and allowed him to eat. He almost wondered if they were trying to deceive him, but he almost shed tears.

"I just ate two bowls of old tofu!" He showed a little courtesy.

"What did you do?" Mr. Liu's big round eyes were still staring at Xiangzi. "Where's the car?" "Car?" Xiangzi spat.

"Come here and have a bowl of rice first! The poison won't kill you! What does it matter with two bowls of old tofu?!" Huniu pulled him over, as if an old sister-in-law loves her uncle. Xiangzi didn't go to carry the bowl, but took out the money first: "Fourth Master, give it to me first, thirty yuan." He put some change in his pocket.

Mr. Liu asked with the tip of his eyebrows, "Where did it come from?"

While Xiangzi was eating, he told the story of being dragged away by the soldiers.

"Humph, you stupid boy!" After hearing this, Fourth Master Liu shook his head. "If you bring them to the city and sell them to soup pots, they are worth more than ten yuan a head; in winter, when all the camel hair is available, three of them will cost sixty yuan!"

Xiangzi had already regretted it, and when he heard this, he felt even sadder. However, then I thought about it, it was a bit unethical to sell three live animals to the soup pot to be knifed; he and the camel both escaped, so they both deserved to live. Without saying anything, he calmed down.

The tiger girl pulled the guy away, and Mr. Liu raised his head as if he thought of something. Suddenly he smiled, revealing two tiger teeth that were getting stronger with age: "Idiot, you said you were sick in Haidian? Why didn't you come back all the way from Huangcun Avenue?"

"I came back around Xishan Mountain. I was afraid that I would be caught up by others on the main road. What if the people in the village wanted to take it too seriously and treat me as a deserter!"

Fourth Master Liu smiled and rolled his eyes inwardly. He was afraid that Xiangzi's words were evil. If the thirty yuan was stolen, he would not be able to store the stolen goods on behalf of others. When he was young, he had done all kinds of illegal things; now, he considered himself to be a reformer, so he had to be careful, and he knew how to be careful. There was only such a gap in Xiangzi's narrative, but Xiangzi explained it without any fuss, and the old man was relieved.

"What should we do?" the old man said, pointing to the money.

"Listen to you!"

"Buy another car?" The old man showed his fangs again, as if to say: "Buy a car yourself and live in my place for free?!"

"Not enough! You have to buy new ones!" Xiangzi didn't look at Mr. Liu's teeth, but only looked at his own heart.

"Lend it to you? One cent for profit, and two and a half for others!"

Xiangzi shook his head.

"You might as well give me a profit if you follow the car shop's instructions!"

"I don't want to print the money either," Xiangzi said in a daze, "I'll save it slowly, and when I've counted enough, I'll buy the spot with cash!"

The old man looked at Xiangzi, as if he were reading some strange word, "Damn it," but he couldn't get angry. After a while, he picked up the money and said, "Thirty? Don't be careless!"

"That's right!" Xiangzi stood up and said, "Go to bed. I'll give you a pack of matches!" He placed a pack of matches on the table, then paused for a moment: "Don't tell others about the camel!"

five

It is true that Old Man Liu did not promote Xiangzi, but the story of the camel quickly spread from Haidian to the city. In the past, although everyone could not find anything wrong with Xiangzi, due to his stubbornness, they thought he was not very gregarious and awkward. Since "Camel Xiangzi" spread, although Xiangzi is still dull and unfriendly, everyone has looked at him differently. Some people said that he lost a gold watch, some said that he got three hundred yuan in vain. The one who believed that he knew the most accurately nodded and said that he brought back thirty camels from the Western Mountains! Although the opinions are different, the conclusion is the same - Xiangzi made evil fortune! As for people who make evil money, no matter how "not a good friend" this guy is, everyone should respect him as usual. Since it is so difficult to make money by working hard, everyone hopes to make some illicit wealth. Since evil fortune is so rare, those with some luck must be different and have great fortune and fortune. Therefore, Xiangzi's silence and aloofness turned into a noble man who spoke late. He should be like this, and they should be chasing him to win over them. "Come on, Xiangzi! Tell me, tell me how you got rich?" Xiangzi heard this every day. He said nothing. It wasn't until he got desperate and his scar turned a little red that he said, "Fa Cai, where the hell is my car?"

Yes, it's true. Where did his car go? Everyone started thinking. But worrying about others is worse than enjoying them, so everyone forgot about Xiangzi's car and thought about his good luck. After a while, everyone saw that Xiangzi was still pulling carts and had not changed his profession or bought a house or land, so they became a little indifferent to him. When mentioning Xiangzi Xiangzi, they no longer asked why he was called "Camel", as if he should be called that at all.

Xiangzi himself did not forget this matter lightly. He wished he could buy a new car right away, and the more anxious he became, the more he thought about the original one. He worked hard all day long, but as he worked, he remembered what happened. When he thought about it, he felt blocked in his heart. He couldn't help but think, what if he wanted to be strong? The world was not fair just because he wanted to be strong, so why should he take away his car for nothing? Even if we get another one right away, how can we know that we won’t encounter that kind of thing again? The past seemed to him like a nightmare, and he could hardly hope for the future. Sometimes he almost felt a little envious when he saw other people drinking, eating, smoking and running around in the local brothels. Since it's useless to be strong, why not be happy in front of you? They are right. Even if he doesn't go to the local house first, he should drink two cups of wine and feel at ease. Cigarettes and alcohol seem to have a special attraction for him now. He feels that these two things are not expensive, but they will be enough to comfort him, allowing him to still move forward and forget the pain of the past.

However, he still didn't dare to touch them. He must be able to save one more if he can, otherwise he cannot buy his own car early. Even if he buys it today and loses it tomorrow, he still has to buy it. This is his wish, his hope, even his religion. Without pulling his own car, his life seemed to be in vain. He never thought of becoming an official, getting rich, or buying property. His ability is only to pull a car, and his most reliable hope is to buy a car; he cannot be worthy of himself unless he buys a car. He thought about it all day long and calculated his money; if he forgot about it, he would forget about himself and feel that he was just a runaway beast without any development or human taste. No matter how good the car is, as long as it is rented, he will never pull it with ease, as if he is carrying a stone on his back. Even if he rents a car, he will not be lazy. He will always clean it up and never bump into it; but this is just some caution, not a kind of happiness. Yes, packing your car is like counting your money, which is true happiness. He still had to stop eating, smoking, and drinking, and it was hard to even drink tea after wrapping it. In the teahouse, a respectable coachman like him, after running for a while, makes a point of drinking a bag of tea leaves worth ten cents, plus two bags of sugar, in order to replenish the energy and dissipate the fire. When he ran and sweat dripped down his "ears and lips" and his chest felt a little hot, he really wanted to do this; this was definitely not a habit or style, but a real need to suppress it with these two bowls of tea. Just thinking about it, he still drank the powder that was a penny a packet. Sometimes he really wanted to scold himself, why he was suffering like this; but, as a coachman, if he wanted to have two dollars left in the month, how could he not do this? He was cruel. Let’s talk about it after buying the car, let’s talk about it after buying the car! Having a car is worth everything!

He is so stubborn about spending money, and he is even less relaxed about making money. Without a monthly subscription, he would work all day long, leaving early and coming back late. He would not take the car away until he had paid a certain amount of money, regardless of time or legs; sometimes he would work hard all day and all night. In the past, he would not steal other people's business, especially the old, weak and disabled soldiers. He used his body and his car to compete with them for seats, but could he still have their share? Now, he doesn't care much about this. He only sees money, and one more is one more. No matter the bitterness or sweetness of the transaction, no matter who he is competing for business with; he only cares about the business and nothing else, like a hungry wild beast. Pulling up and running, he felt a little better, feeling that only by being stubborn could he have any hope of buying a car. The reputation of Camel Xiangzi who came and went is far less than that of Xiangzi alone. Many times, he would grab a deal and run away, followed by a lot of curses. He didn't reply, lowered his head and ran away, saying in his heart: "If I hadn't bought a car, I would never be so shameless!" He seemed to beg everyone's forgiveness with these words, but he refused to say it to everyone so directly. At the bus stop or in a teahouse, he saw people staring at him. He wanted to explain to everyone, but when he saw how indifferent they were, and because he did not drink, gamble, play chess, or chat with them on weekdays, he could only keep his words in his stomach and had no way of speaking out. Embarrassment gradually turned to shame, and his anger grew; they glared at him, and he glared at them. Thinking of how much respect everyone had for him when he escaped from the mountain, he felt even more sad that he was being looked down upon like this. Holding a pot of tea alone, if he was in a teahouse, or counting the coins he had just earned, if he was at the entrance of a car, he would use all his strength to suppress his anger. He didn't want to fight, although he wasn't afraid of one. We are not afraid of fighting, but we should think about fighting Xiangzi. None of them is his opponent, and it is not very bright for us to fight one of them. Barely suppressing his anger, he couldn't think of any other way. He could only endure it for a while and wait until he bought the car. With his own car, he doesn't have to worry about renting a car every day. He can naturally be generous and no longer offend others by robbing business. With this thought in mind, he glanced at everyone as if to say: Let's wait and see!

As for him personally, he shouldn't be so desperate. After escaping back to the city, he didn't wait for his illness to recover before he pulled up the car. Although he didn't give in at all, he often felt tired. He didn't dare to rest when he was tired. He always thought that running a few more times and sweating would make him feel less tired. Regarding food, he didn't dare to pout, but he also didn't dare to eat more good things. He saw that he had lost a lot of weight, but his body was still as tall and his muscles and bones were still as strong. He was relieved. He always thinks that because he is taller than others, he must be able to suffer more than others. It seems that he has never thought that because he is larger and suffers more, he should need more nourishment. Lady Tiger has told him several times: "If you do this, it's your own fault if you vomit blood!"

He understood very well that this was a good thing, but because things were not going as he wanted and his body was not well maintained, he was a little angry. He nodded slightly and said, "If I didn't run like this, how long would it take for me to buy a car?"

If someone else was so sharp-eyed, Huniu would have to scold him for at least half a day; to Xiangzi, she was extremely polite and protective. She just curled her lips: "You have to stop here to buy a car. You are made of iron! You should have a good three days of rest!" Seeing that Xiangzi couldn't listen to this: "Okay, you have your old ideas, don't blame me if you die!"

Mr. Liu also looked down on Xiangzi a little: Xiangzi's desperate efforts, leaving early and returning late, were of course not good for his car. Although there is no time limit for renting a car for a whole day, you can take it out and pick it up whenever you like. If everyone is as stubborn as Xiangzi, a car will break down at least half a year early. No matter how strong it is, it can't stand up to a nail! Besides, Xiangzi only cares about running to his death and doesn't always have time to help clean the car, which is another loss. The old man felt a little unhappy. He didn't say anything. It was a general rule to work all day with no time limit. Helping to pack the car was a friendship, not an obligation. With his character, he couldn't express himself to Xiangzi. He could only show a look of dissatisfaction from the corners of his eyes, and closed his mouth tightly. Sometimes he wanted to kick Xiangzi out; looking at his daughter, he didn't dare to do this. He had no intention of treating Xiangzi as a potential son-in-law, but since his daughter liked this stupid boy, it was not easy for him to be meddlesome. He only has one girl, and seeing that there is no hope of getting married, he can no longer drive her away as his friend. To be honest, Huniu was so useful that he really didn't want her to get married. He felt that this selfishness was a bit strange and could not be tolerated by her, so he was somewhat afraid of her. The old man was fearless all his life, but in his old age he became afraid of his own daughter. He came up with some reasoning out of embarrassment: as long as he is afraid of individuals, it is proof that he is not completely lawless. With this fact, perhaps he would not suffer bad retribution when he was about to die. Well, he admitted that he should be afraid of his daughter, so he refused to drive Xiangzi away. This certainly does not mean that he can let his daughter do whatever he wants and even marry Xiangzi. no. He could see that his daughter might not have that intention, but Xiangzi did not dare to flatter her.

Then, he just needs to be careful, and he won't be unhappy if he recruits his daughter first. Xiangzi didn't pay attention to the old man's demeanor. He didn't pay attention to these idle dishes. If he is willing to leave Renhe Factory, it is definitely not for gambling, but because he hopes to get a monthly subscription. He was already a little tired of showing up. Firstly, he was looked down upon by everyone because he was snatching deals, and secondly, because his daily income was not fixed. It would be more today, less tomorrow, and he couldn't decide how long it would take to get enough money to buy a car. He is willing to have an accurate idea in his heart, even if there is only a small amount left, as long as he can rely on the accurate number of deaths every month, he will feel hopeful and relieved. He is a person who is willing to be a carrot and a pit.

He got a monthly subscription. Humph, it’s as unpleasant as breaking up the seats! This time it was at Yang's house. Mr. Yang is from Shanghai, Mrs. Yang is from Tianjin, and Mrs. Yang is from Suzhou. One gentleman and two wives gave birth to an unknown number of children with their southern accents. On the first day of work, Xiangzi almost fainted. Early in the morning, the eldest wife took the car to the market to buy groceries. When I came back, I sent the young masters and ladies to school separately. Some were in junior high school, some were in elementary school, and some were in kindergarten. The schools were different, the ages were different, and the looks were different, but they were all equally annoying, especially when sitting in the car. The most honest ones had more hands than monkeys. Send the children away and Mr. Yang goes to the Yamen. Send her to the Yamen, come back quickly, and take the second wife to Dong'an market or visit relatives and friends. Come back and take the students home for lunch. After eating, send them away. After sending the students back, Xiangzi thought it was time to eat, but the eldest lady spoke in a Tianjin accent and asked him to fetch water. Someone brings sweet water to Yang's house, and the bitter water for washing clothes is left to the coachman to pick up. This job was outside of the conditions. In order to deal with the matter, Xiangzi did not dare to argue and filled the tank without saying a word. He put down the bucket and was about to carry the rice bowl when the second wife asked him to buy something for her. The eldest wife and the second wife had always been at odds with each other, but in terms of housekeeping, they had the same political views. One of them was not allowing the servants to take a break, and the other was that they refused to watch the servants eat. Xiangzi didn't know this, so he just thought it was because the house happened to be so busy the day before, so he didn't say anything and bought a few sesame cakes with his own money. He loves money as much as his life, but in order to maintain his career, he has to be ruthless.

After returning from shopping, the eldest lady asked him to clean the yard. The husband, wife, and second wife of Yang Zhai all dress up beautifully when they go out, but the whole house and courtyard look like a big garbage dump. Xiangzi felt sick looking at the yard, so he just went to clean it and forgot that the coachman was not also responsible for the chores. The yard was clean and tidy, so the second wife asked him to clean the house as well. Xiangzi didn't object. What surprised him was how the two ladies could be so dirty in the house despite their appearance and beauty! After tidying up the house, the second wife handed over the little mud ghost who had just turned one year old to him. There was nothing he could do. He is good at all things that involve working hard, but he has never held a child. He held the young master with both hands. Don't use force, for fear of slipping, but use force, and he would be afraid of injuring his muscles and bones. He was sweating. He wanted to give this treasure to Zhang Ma, a big-footed woman from Jiangbei. When I found her, I was scolded by her when I met her. The servants in the Yang House have always been replaced every three to five days. The husband and wife always thought that the servants were just domestic slaves who had to kill the poor to be worthy of the wages. There is only this Zhang Ma, who has been with them for five or six years. The only reason is that she will scold her whenever she dares to speak out, no matter whether it is the husband or the wife, if she is annoyed, she will be beaten. With the ferocity of Mr. Yang's Hai-style curses, the majesty of Mrs. Yang's Tianjin accent, and the fluentness of the second wife's Suzhou tune, they had always been invincible; but when they encountered Zhang Ma's fierceness, they began to feel a sense of reciprocity, a hero meeting a hero, so they appreciated her quite a bit and took her into their own army.

Xiangzi was born in the northern countryside, and he was most taboo about swearing in the streets. But he didn't dare to hit Zhang Ma, because a good man doesn't fight with women, and he doesn't want to fight back. He just glared at her. Zhang Ma stopped speaking, as if she sensed some danger. At this time, the eldest lady called Xiangzi to pick up the student. He quickly sent the clay doll back to the second wife. The second wife thought he was deliberately looking down on her, so she cursed him out loud. The eldest wife originally didn't want Xiangzi to carry the child for the second wife, but when she heard the second wife scolding him, she also scolded him in a smooth voice, and it was him who was scolding him; Xiangzi became a shield for being scolded. He hurriedly pulled up the car and walked out, seeming to have forgotten even his anger, because he had never seen anything like this before, and when he suddenly encountered it, he felt a little dizzy.

The children were brought back in batches, and the courtyard was even busier than the market. The scoldings of three women and the cries of a group of children were as chaotic as Dashilar during a performance, and the chaos was inexplicable. Fortunately, he still had to pick up Mr. Yang, so he hurriedly ran out again. The people shouting and yelling on the street seemed to be more bearable than the chaos in the house.

It wasn't until twelve o'clock that Xiangzi found time to sigh. Not only did he feel tired on his body, but there was also a constant buzzing in his head; everyone in the Yang family, young and old, had indeed fallen asleep, but there still seemed to be shouts and curses from the gentlemen and ladies in his ears, like three different phonographs spinning in his mind, making him panic. He didn't want to think about anything anymore, he wanted to sleep. As soon as he entered his small room, his heart felt cold and he was no longer sleepy. A concierge with two doors separated by a wooden board. Zhang Ma lives on one side and he lives on the other. There were no lights in the house. There was a small window about two feet wide on the wall facing the street, which happened to be under a street lamp, giving the house a little light. The room was damp and smelly. The soil on the floor was as thick as a copper plate. There were planks placed against the wall and nothing else. He touched the bed board and knew that if he put his head down, he would have to put his feet on the wall, and if he put his feet flat, he would have to sit up halfway. He won't sleep like a gold ingot. After thinking for a long time, he pulled the plank diagonally so that the two ends faced the rhinoceros horns, so he could lay his head flat and let his legs hang down for the rest of the night.

He brought the bedding in from the doorway, laid it out carelessly, and lay down. He was not used to having his legs hanging in the air, so he couldn't sleep. I closed my eyes and comforted myself: Go to sleep, I have to get up early tomorrow! You have suffered all kinds of sins, why can't you just tolerate this! Regardless of whether he doesn’t eat well or drink well and works too hard, he may often play cards, treat guests, or have dinner[1]

. Why did we come out, Xiangzi? Not for money? As long as you make more money, you will have to endure anything! With this thought, he felt much more comfortable. After smelling the room, it was no longer as smelly as before, and he slowly fell asleep. I vaguely thought there were bed bugs, but I didn't bother to get them.

After two days, Xiangzi's heart felt cold to the core. But on the fourth day, a female guest came, and Zhang Ma was busy setting up the card table. His heart felt as if a spring breeze suddenly came over the frozen lake. The ladies played cards and handed the children over to the servants. Since Zhang Ma had to serve the cigarette and tea towel handle, those little monkeys were naturally under the control of Xiangzi. He hated these monkeys, but he took a sneak peek into the room and saw that the eldest lady was in charge of the boss's money and seemed to be very serious about it. He thought in his heart: Even though this lady is so powerful, she may not be confused. She knows how to get an extra thirty-fifty cents for the servants at this time. He was particularly patient with the monkeys. For the sake of the leader's money, he had to treat these monkey cubs as young masters and young ladies.

The card game was over and his wife asked him to send the guest home. The two female passengers were anxious to leave at the same time, so they had to hire another car. Xiangzi called for a car, and the eldest lady lifted up her robe and dragged her belt with her money all over her body, preparing to pay the guest's fare. The guest gave a few words of humility, and the eldest lady shouted as if she was desperate: "What's wrong with you, old girl! You're here, and you don't have the fare yet! Old girl! Get on board!" Only then did she take out a dime.

Xiangzi could see it clearly. When he handed over the dime, his wife's hand was trembling a little.

After seeing off the guests and helping Zhang's mother pack the card table and other things, Xiangzi glanced at his wife. The wife asked Zhang’s mother to get some boiling water. When Zhang’s mother left the house, she took out a dime and said, “Here, don’t touch me with your eyesight!”

Xiangzi's face suddenly turned purple, and he straightened his back as if his head was about to hit the roof beam. He grabbed the dime and threw it on his wife's fat face: "Give me four days' wages!" "What about you?" After the wife said this, she looked at Xiangzi again, fell silent, and gave him four days' wages. As soon as he unpacked his clothes and walked out of the street, he heard people yelling at him in the courtyard.

On an early autumn night, with a small breeze blowing in the shadows of stars and leaves, Xiangzi raised his head, looked at the distant Milky Way, and sighed. It was such a cool day and his chest was so wide, but he felt as if there wasn't enough air and his chest was very tight. He wanted to sit down and cry. With his own physique, his own forbearance, and his own strength, he would make people treat him like a pig or a dog, and he would not be able to maintain a situation. Not only did he resent the Yang family, but he also vaguely felt a sense of hopelessness, fearing that he would never get any better in his life. Pulling the bedroll, he walked slower and slower, as if he was no longer a Xiangzi who could run ten miles with his legs.

When he reached the street, there were fewer pedestrians, but the street lights were very bright. He felt even more empty and confused, and he didn't know where to go. Where? Naturally, he returned to Renhe Factory. I felt a little sad again. Those who do business and work hard are not afraid of not having business, but they are afraid of having a caregiver[1]

But the business is not concluded, just like a customer coming into a hotel or barber shop, taking a look at it, and then walking out. Xiangzi knew that it was common for him to quit his job, so if he didn't stay here, he would have a place to stay. However, he kept things humbly and humiliated himself just to buy a car, but it still happened in three and a half days. He was just like those old guys who were used to visiting the house, and he felt sad. He almost felt ashamed to enter Renhe Factory again, and made a joke to everyone: "Look, Luotuo Xiangzi dared to show off his love in three and a half days.[2]

Yeah, hum! "

If you don't work for people and factories, where will you go? In order not to think about this matter anymore, he walked straight to Xi'anmen Street. In front of Renhe Factory are three shop rooms, one of which is used as a locker room. Only the coachmen are allowed to come in to pay accounts or negotiate matters. They are not allowed to go back and forth casually, because the east and west rooms are the bedrooms of the Liu family's father and daughter. There is a car door next to the west room, two green-painted doors, with a thick iron bar bent above them, and a very bright electric lamp without a cover hanging on it. Under the lamp, there are four words painted in gold on an iron plate - "Renhe Car Factory". Coachmen use this door when leaving and collecting their carriages and coming and going at any time. The dark green paint on the door and the gold characters on it were all illuminated by the bright white electric lamp. The cars that came in and out were all beautiful cars, all with black and yellow paint, all shiny and shiny, with snow-white cushion covers. Even the coachmen felt a little proud, as if they all regarded themselves as nobles among coachmen. Enter through the gate and turn to the west of the front, you will find a large square yard with an old Sophora japonica tree in the middle. The east and west rooms are all open-faced and are used for storing cars; the south room and several small cabins in the small courtyard behind the south room are all the coachman's quarters.

It was about eleven o'clock when Xiangzi saw the extremely bright and strangely lonely lamp in Renhe Factory. There were no lights in the closet and the east room, but the west room was still on. He knew that Tiger Girl was not asleep yet. He wanted to sneak in quietly and not let the tiger girl see him; just because she thought highly of him, he didn't want her to be the first to see his failure.

As soon as he pulled the car under her window, Huniu came out of the car door:

"Hey, Xiangzi? How-" She was about to ask, but when she saw Xiangzi's dejected look, he pulled the bedroll in the car and swallowed her words.

No matter what he was afraid of, the shame and frustration in Xiangzi's heart condensed into a ball, and he immediately stood still and stayed there. Speechless, he looked at the tiger girl stupidly. She was also strange today. I don't know whether it was because of the electric light or the powder on her face. Her face was much whiter than usual. The whiter face concealed a lot of her fierceness. There is indeed some rouge on her lips, so Hu Niu also brings out some charm. When Xiangzi saw this, he felt very strange and became even more flustered. Because he had never treated her as a woman, but when he suddenly saw these red lips, he suddenly felt a little embarrassed. She wore a light green silk jacket on the upper body, and a pair of green crepe pants with fat legs on the lower body. The green coat shimmered softly and slightly sadly under the electric light. Because it was short, a little bit of the white trouser waistband was exposed, making the green color more obvious and sober. The fat black pants underneath were slightly blown by the breeze, like some sinister air, trying to get rid of the bright light and become one with the dark night. Xiangzi didn't dare to look any further, and lowered his head blankly. There was still a small green coat with light in his heart. With the financial resources of the Liu family, she could wear silk and satin every day, but when dealing with the coachmen all day long, she always wore plain clothes and trousers. Even if there were some colors, they would not be eye-catching on the cloth. Xiangzi seemed to see something very new, familiar yet new, so he felt a little confused.

He was worried at first, but after encountering this new and strange living thing under the strong light, he had no idea. He refused to move, but he hoped that the tiger girl would quickly enter the house, or order him to do something. He simply couldn't stand this kind of torture, a kind of torture that felt like nothing and was very sad.

"Hi!" She took a step forward and said in a low voice: "Don't be stunned! Go, put the car down, and come back quickly. I have something to tell you. See you at the house."

He was used to doing things for her, so he had no choice but to obey. But today she was different from the past, and he wanted to think about it. I stood there thinking about it, and felt so stiff and panicked. He had no idea and pulled the car in. Look at the south room, there is no light, everyone is probably asleep; or there are people who have confiscated cars. After putting the car away, he returned to her door. Suddenly, his heart beat fast.

"Come in, I have something to tell you!" She stuck her head out and said with a half smile and half annoyance. He walked in slowly.

There are a few unripe white pears on the table, the skin is still green. A wine flask and three white porcelain wine cups. A large plate with half a sauced chicken and some smoked liver, sauced tripe and other delicacies.

"Look," Lady Tiger pointed to a chair for him, watched him sit down, and then said, "Look, I'm having a treat today, and you should have some too!" As she said that, she poured him a glass of wine. The spiciness of Baigan wine, mixed with the flavor of smoked meat, is particularly thick and heavy. "Drink and eat this chicken. I've already eaten it, no need to let it go! I just made a prediction with the dominoes, so I know you'll come back. Will it work?"

"I don't drink!" Xiangzi looked at the wine cup in trance.

"Get out of here if you don't want to drink. What's wrong with being so kind and ungrateful? You stupid camel! I can't kill you! I can even drink four taels. If you don't believe it, look at it!" She picked up the wine cup, drank more than half of it, closed her eyes, and let out a sigh. Holding up the cup: "You drink! Or I'll grab your ears and drink it from you!"

Xiangzi was full of resentment and had no place to vent it. When faced with such teasing, he really wanted to stare at her. But he knew that Tiger Girl had always been nice to him, and she was so straightforward with everyone, so he shouldn't offend her. Since I don't want to offend her, on second thought I might as well complain to her about my grievances. I have never been very talkative, but today it seems that I have thousands of words that I am holding back in my heart, and I would not be happy if I had to talk about them. Thinking about it this way, he felt that the tiger girl was not teasing him, but frankly loved and protected him. He took the wine cup and drank it dry. A spicy breath went down slowly, accurately, and forcefully. He stretched his neck, straightened his chest, and burped two inconvenient burps.

Hu Niu laughed. He managed to keep the drink down, and when he heard the laughter, he quickly looked towards the east side.

"No one," she stopped laughing, but still had a smile on her face. "The old man went to celebrate my aunt's birthday, so there will be a delay of two or three days. My aunt lives in Nanyuan." As he said this, he filled the cup again.

Hearing this, his mind took a turn, and he felt that something seemed wrong somewhere. At the same time, he was reluctant to go out; her face was so close to his, her clothes were so clean and smooth, and her lips were so red, which all made him feel a new kind of excitement. She was still so old and ugly, but she had a little more vitality than usual, as if she suddenly became another person, still her, but with something extra. He didn't dare to think about this new thing in detail, and he didn't dare to accept it casually, but he couldn't bear to refuse it. He blushed. As if to strengthen his courage, he took another sip of wine. He wanted to complain to her just now, but he forgot about it now. Blushing, he couldn't help but look at her a few more times. The more he looked, the more confused he became. She showed more and more something that he didn't understand, and more and more hot power was transmitted to her. Gradually, she became an abstract thing. He warned himself that he needed to be careful; but he also had to be bold. He drank three cups of wine in a row and forgot what it meant to be careful. Looking at her vaguely, he felt very happy and bold for some reason; he was extremely brave and wanted to seize a new experience and happiness immediately. Normally, he was a little afraid of her; now, there was nothing scary about her. Instead, he became majestic and powerful, as if he could hold her in his hands like a cat. The lights went out in the house. It was dark. From time to time, one or two stars pierce the Milky Way, or streak into the darkness, with red or white light tails, light or stiff, falling straight or sweeping, sometimes moving and trembling, giving the sky some turbulence of light and heat, and giving the darkness some flickering explosions. Sometimes one or two stars, sometimes several stars, fly down at the same time, making the silent autumn sky tremble slightly and making thousands of stars temporarily confused. Sometimes a single giant star pierces the corner of the sky, its tail of light is extremely long, radiating star flowers; red and gradually yellowing; in the final advance, it suddenly lights up the corner of the sky white like a craziness, as if it has pierced through ten thousand layers of darkness, letting in and staying some milky white light. After the remaining light dissipated, the darkness seemed to shake a few times, then closed up again, and the quiet and lazy stars returned to their original positions, smiling in the autumn wind. There are some Qiuying flying on the ground who are looking for lovers, and they are also playing star-like games.

The next day, Xiangzi got up very early, picked up the car and went out. There was a little pain in his head and throat. This was because it was the first time he drank alcohol, but he didn't pay attention to it. Sitting at the entrance of a small alley, the morning breeze blew his head. He knew that this headache would soon pass. But there was something else on his mind that made him depressed and panicked, and there was no way to excuse it for the moment. What happened last night made him confused, ashamed, sad, and felt a little dangerous.

He didn't understand what happened to the tiger girl. She was no longer a virgin, Xiangzi didn't know until what time it was. He has always respected her, and has never heard of any irregularities in her. Although she is very casual and straightforward with everyone, no one talks about her behind their back. Even if there are some bad words about her among the coachmen, they only say that she is great and nothing else. So, why was there the scene last night?

This was obviously confusing, and Xiangzi also doubted what happened last night. She knew that he was not in the car factory, how could she wait for him wholeheartedly? If it could be any one... Xiangzi lowered his head. He comes from the countryside, and although he has never thought about getting married, he has a plan in mind: if he has his own car, his life becomes more comfortable, and he is willing to get married, he will definitely go to the countryside to marry a young, strong girl who can endure hardships and can do laundry and work. Among the young men of his age, no one would sneak into the "White House"[3] even if someone is supervising them.

? Xiangzi always refused to be easy-going. Firstly, he thought he was a strong man and could not spend money on women. Secondly, he saw with his own eyes those fools who spent money unnecessarily - some were only eighteen or nineteen years old - in the toilet with their heads against the wall and still unable to urinate. Finally, he must behave himself in order to be worthy of his future wife, because once he wants to marry, he must marry a pure and innocent girl, so he must behave like that. But now, now... Thinking of Hu Niu, if you look at her as a friend, she is really good; if you look at her as a woman, she is ugly, old, powerful, and shameless! Even when he thought about the soldiers who snatched his car and almost killed him, he was not as hateful and repulsive as when he thought about her! She had destroyed all the coolness he had brought from the country, and now he was a pussy-stealer!

Besides, what if this matter gets louder and Liu Si finds out? Does Liu Si know that his daughter is a loser? If he didn't know, wouldn't Xiangzi bear the blame alone? If they had known about it for a long time and were unwilling to control their daughter, then who are their father and daughter? If he mixes with such people, what is he himself? Even if both father and daughter were willing, he couldn't have her; no matter whether Old Man Liu had sixty cars, six hundred cars, or six thousand cars! He had to leave Renhe and the factory immediately and make a clean break with them. Xiangzi has Xiangzi's ability. He can buy a car and marry a wife based on his own ability. This is how he is upright! Thinking of this, he raised his head and felt that he was a good man. He had nothing to fear and nothing to worry about. As long as he worked hard, he would definitely succeed.

I offered my seat twice, but couldn't get it. That awkwardness suddenly returned. I don't want to think about it anymore, but my heart is filled with panic. This matter seems completely different from other things. Even if there is a solution, it is not easy to forget easily. Not only did there seem to be something stuck to my body, but there also seemed to be a black spot in my heart that could never be washed away. No matter how resentful or disgusted she was, she always seemed to capture his heart. The less he wanted to think about it, the more suddenly she jumped out of his heart. A naked woman, she gave him all the ugliness and beauty all at once, like buying a pile of junk. There were also a few small shiny colored objects among the scraps of metal, which made people unable to bear to refuse. He has never been so close to anyone. Although it is sudden and it is a deception, after all, such a relationship cannot be forgotten casually. If he just wants to put it aside, it will naturally linger in his heart, as if it has taken root. This was not only an experience for him, but also an indescribable disturbance that left him wondering what to do. He had nothing to do with her, himself, the present and the future. He was like a bug caught in a cobweb, and it was too late to struggle.

In a daze, he pulled in a few deals. Even when he was running, he did not forget this matter in his mind. He did not think of it clearly and from beginning to end, but he always thought of a meaning, a taste, or some feelings, all vague but kind. He really wanted to go drinking alone and drink without knowing anyone else. Maybe he would be happy and he could no longer suffer this torture! But he didn't dare to drink. He couldn't ruin himself over this. He thought about buying a car again. But he couldn't concentrate on thinking, there was always something blocking his mind; before he thought about the car, this thing had sneaked out and occupied his heart, like a dark cloud covering the sun, interrupting the light. In the evening, when he was about to collect the car, he felt even sadder. He had to go back to the depot, but he was really afraid of going back. What if I meet her? He pulled the empty cart around the street. Two or three times he was not far from the depot, then turned back and walked elsewhere, like a child who was truant for the first time and did not dare to enter the house. The strange thing is that the more he wants to avoid her, the more he wants to meet her. The darker it gets, the more powerful this thought becomes. A kind of boldness and confusion that he was willing to try even though he knew it was wrong tightly grabbed his heart. When he was a child, he used a pole to poke a hornet's nest like this. He was afraid, but his heart was beating to try it, as if there was some evil force urging him. In a daze, he felt a force that was more powerful than himself. It wanted to roll him into a ball and throw him into a ball of fire; he could not stop his progress.

He circled back to Xi'an Gate again. This time he didn't want to hesitate anymore and wanted to go straight to the court to find her. She is no longer anyone, she is just a woman. His whole body felt warm. As soon as he reached the door, a man in his forties walked under the light. He seemed to recognize this man's appearance and attitude, but he didn't dare to say hello. Almost instinctively, he said: "Car?" The man was stunned for a moment: "Xiangzi?" "Yes," Xiangzi smiled. "Mr. Cao?"

Mr. Cao smiled and nodded. "I said Xiangzi, if you were not at the door, why don't you come to my place? The person I am using now is too lazy. He never cares about cleaning the car, although he can run very quickly[4]

of. Are you coming? "

"Can you come, sir?" Xiangzi seemed to have forgotten how to smile, and kept wiping his face with a small towel. "Sir, when will I start work?"

"What about that?" Mr. Cao thought for a while, "The day after tomorrow."

"Yes, sir!" Xiangzi also thought for a moment: "Sir, shall I take you back?" "No. Didn't I go to Shanghai for a while? After I came back, I no longer lived in my old place. Now I live on Beichang Street; I go out for a walk at night. See you the day after tomorrow." Mr. Cao told Xiangzi the house number, and then added: "I still use my own car."

Xiangzi was so happy that he wanted to fly. All the worries of the past few days were suddenly wiped away, like a white stone road washed by heavy rain. Mr. Cao was his old master. Although they didn't live together for many years, they had a good relationship. Mr. Cao was a very kind man, and his family had a small population, with only a wife and a little boy. He pulled the car and ran straight to Renhe Factory. The lights in Tiger Girl's house are still on. When Xiangzi saw the light on, he was stunned.

After standing there for a long time, he decided to go in and see her, telling her that he had found another monthly subscription, that he would hand over the car for the two days, that he would use his savings, and that he would be done with it from now on - naturally it was not easy to say this clearly, but she would always understand.

He went in and put the car away first, then came back and boldly called Miss Liu. "Come in!"

He opened the door and she was lying on the bed, wearing ordinary clothes and bare feet. Still leaning forward, she said, "How is it? Why did you get the benefit?"

Xiangzi's face was as red as the eggs given away when giving birth. After being stunned for a long time, he said slowly: "I have found a job again and will start work the day after tomorrow. I have my own car..."

She took over the words: "You don't know what to do!" She sat up and pointed at him with a half smile and half annoyance: "Here is your food and your clothes. Why don't you have to go and sweat? The old man can't control me. I can't stay a widow for the rest of my life! The old man really Damn it, I have two bodies, we can get two or three cars, and we can earn 80 cents a day. Isn’t that better than you running around all day long? What’s wrong with me? Except that I’m a little older than you, but I’m not much older!”

"I'm willing to pull the cart!" Xiangzi couldn't find any other excuse.

"You're an authentic Wowotou head! Sit down first, I won't bite you!" After she finished speaking, she smiled, revealing a pair of tiger teeth.

Xiangzi sat down with his veins jumping. "Where's my little money?"

"It's in the old man's hands. If you can't throw it away, don't be afraid. Don't ask for it from him. Do you know his temper? It's enough to buy a car. If you ask for it again, a boy can't lose it to you. If you want it now, it would be weird if he doesn't curse you to death! He treats you well! If you can't throw it away, I'll pay for you both! You country head! Don't let me hurt you!"

Xiangzi said nothing again, lowered his head and dug for a long time, took out two days' rent of the car and put it on the table: "It's for two days." He suddenly thought: "I'll hand over the car today, and I'll take a day off tomorrow."

The tiger girl came over, grabbed the money in her hand, and stuffed it into his pocket: "These two days, I even gave away the car and the passengers for free! You kid is lucky! Just don't be ungrateful!" After that, she turned around and locked the door.
