Xiangzi went to Cao's house.

He felt a little ashamed of Tiger Girl. But since the matter was due to her temptation, and he didn't want to covet her money, he thought that there would be nothing terribly wrong in breaking up with her from now on. What he was worried about was that Fourth Master Liu was holding his little money. Go and ask for it right away, I'm afraid the old man is too worried. She never went to see their father and daughter again. Maybe the tiger girl got angry and said some bad words to the old man, and "exploded" the little money. He should continue to ask the old man to save money. He would have to run into her as soon as he arrived at Renhe Factory, which was embarrassing. He couldn't think of a proper solution, and the more he had no solution, the more uneasy he became.

He really wanted to ask Mr. Cao for an idea, but how should he say it? The part about Tiger Girl cannot be told to anyone. Thinking of this, he really regretted it. What happened was that he began to understand that it couldn't be cut off in two ways. This kind of thing can never be cleaned away, like a black scar on the flesh. He lost his car for no reason, and this entanglement came again for no reason. He felt that his life was probably over, and no matter how hard he tried to be strong, it was all in vain. After thinking about it, he realized this: in the end, he would probably have to be willing to ask for the tiger girl; not for her, but also for those cars? "The bastard eats two pieces of fried meat"! He couldn't bear it, but when the time came, he might have to do it! He had no choice but to keep going, doing the good things and waiting for the bad ones; he no longer dared to be as confident as before. His stature, strength, and ambition are all irrelevant; his life is his own, but he can teach others to take care of it; he can teach some bastard to take care of it.

Logically speaking, he should be very happy, because Cao Zhai is the cutest among the houses he has ever been in. The wages at Cao's house are not more than elsewhere, and there is not a lot of change except for the three-section reward. However, Mr. Cao and Mrs. Cao are very kind and treat everyone as individuals. Xiangzi is willing to make more money and work hard to make money, but he also wants to have a place like a house and enough food to eat. Everything in Cao's house is clean, even the servants' rooms are clean; the food in Cao's house is not bitter, and they never give smelly food to their servants. He had a spacious room, could eat three meals in a leisurely manner, and the owner was very polite. Xiangzi, even Xiangzi, was not willing to focus on money. Besides, the food and accommodation are suitable, the work is not tiring, and taking good care of your body is not a disadvantage. He would never eat so well if he paid for his own food. Now that there are ready-made vegetables and rice, and the food will not go down his spine, why doesn't he eat to the fullest? Food is also bought with money, and he calculates this account very clearly. Besides, although the Cao family doesn't play cards, doesn't treat guests often, and doesn't have much change, they can still get a dime or two from doing any temporary work. For example, if his wife asks him to buy pills for his child, she will give him an extra cent and ask him to take a car, even though she knows that he can run faster than anyone else. This little money is nothing, but it makes him feel a kind of humanity, a kind of consideration, which makes people feel happy. Xiangzi had met quite a few masters, and nine out of ten of them would pay them one day late, just one day later, which meant that they were using people for nothing, and the servants were nothing more than cats and dogs, or worse than cats and dogs. The Cao family is an exception, so he likes to be here. He tidied the yard and watered the flowers without waiting for their orders, and every time they saw him doing these things, they would always say something nice. Taking advantage of this season, they found some old things and taught him how to change them for matches. Although they were still usable, he kept them by himself. Here, he felt something human.

In Xiangzi's eyes, Fourth Master Liu can be regarded as Huang Tianba. Although he is powerful, when it comes to saving face and calling for name, he will never be black-faced. The decent person in his heart, apart from Huang Tianba, is the Saint Kong. He couldn't understand what kind of person Saint Kong was, but it was said that he knew a lot of Chinese characters, which was quite reasonable. Among the houses he had mixed with, there were both literary and military men; among the martial men, there was not even one who could catch up with Fourth Master Liu; among the literary men, although there were gentlemen who taught in universities and some who worked as errands in yamen, they certainly knew a lot of people, but he did not meet anyone who was reasonable. Even if the husband is reasonable, the ladies will be difficult to serve. Only Mr. Cao is literate and reasonable, and Mrs. Cao is also well-behaved and popular. Therefore, Mr. Cao must be Sage Kong. If Xiangzi cannot remember what Sage Kong looks like, then he must be like Mr. Cao, whether Sage Kong likes it or not.

In fact, Mr. Cao is not very clever. He is just a middle-level person who sometimes teaches and sometimes does other things. He regarded himself as a "socialist" and an aestheticist, who was slightly influenced by William Morris. He does not have profound opinions in politics or art; but he has one advantage: the little bit he believes in can be implemented in small events in life. He seems to have realized that he does not have amazing talents and can do some earth-shattering things, so he arranges his work and family according to his own ideals. Although he does not contribute to society, he at least wants to be consistent with his words and deeds and not be a hypocrite. Therefore, he pays great attention to small things, as if to say that as long as the small family is arranged beautifully, society can be casual. This sometimes makes him feel ashamed, and sometimes makes him feel proud. It seems that he can clearly see that his family is a small oasis in the desert, which can only provide some water and food that come here, and has no greater significance.

Xiangzi happened to come to this small oasis; after walking in the desert for so many days, he thought it was a miracle. He had never met anyone like Mr. Cao, so he regarded this man as a sage. This may be because he has little experience, or maybe there are not many people like this in the world. When he pulled Mr. Cao out, he was very happy because Mr. Cao's clothes were so elegant, his person was so lively and generous, and he was so neat and tall, and he was very happy, as if he was the only one worthy of pulling Mr. Cao. At home, everything is so clean and always so quiet, making him feel comfortable and settled. When he was in the countryside, he often saw old people sitting silently in winter or under the autumn moon, holding bamboo pipes in their mouths. Although he was still young and could not imitate these old people, he liked to see them sitting quietly like this. He must have guessed what it was like. Although he was in the city now, the tranquility of Cao's house reminded him of the countryside. He really wanted to smoke a pipe and try to get a taste of it.

Unfortunately, that woman and that little money made him unable to feel at ease; his heart was like a green leaf, wrapped in silk by a bug, preparing to make a cocoon. Because of this matter, he himself couldn't let go; when it came to others, even Mr. Cao, he was always in a daze, and his answers were not what he asked. This made him very sad. Cao Zhai went to bed very early and would be fine by around nine o'clock in the evening. He sat alone in the house or courtyard, thinking over and over again, thinking about these two things. He even thought of getting married right away, which would definitely put an end to Huniu's thoughts. But how can you support your family by pulling a cart? He knew the poor people in the courtyard. The men pulled carts and the women worked as sewing machines. The children picked up coal cores. In summer, they picked up watermelon rinds on the mounds and gnawed them. In winter, they all went to work at the porridge factory. Xiangzi couldn't bear this. Besides, if he gets married, Old Man Liu will definitely not get back the little money in his hand; how could Huniu be willing to let him go so easily? He couldn't give up that little money, it was earned with his life!

His own car was purchased early last fall. It's been more than a year, and now he has nothing, only more than thirty yuan that he can't get out, and some worries! The more he thought about it, the more unhappy he became.

It’s been more than ten days since the Mid-Autumn Festival, and the weather is slowly getting cooler. He planned to add two more clothes. Money again! You can’t buy clothes but still have money left over at the same time. You can’t even hope to buy a car! Even if I get a monthly subscription, what will happen to me in this life?

One night, Mr. Cao came back late from Dongcheng. Xiangzi was careful and walked across the street from Tiananmen Square. There were no people on the open road, the slight cool breeze, and the quiet lights made him run with more energy. The depression in his heart for many days was temporarily forgotten. Listening to his own footsteps and the soft sound of the bow of the car, he forgot everything. After unbuttoning his buttons, he felt the cool breeze blowing on his chest. He felt happy, as if he could just run like this until he ran to an unknown place. He might as well run to death. He ran faster and faster, and there was a car in front of him. He "drove" one and passed Tiananmen in a while. His feet seemed to be like two springs, bouncing up almost as soon as he touched the ground; the wheels behind him spun so fast that no traces could be seen, and the leather wheels seemed to have left the ground, and even the people and the car seemed to be blown up by the strong wind. Mr. Cao was struck by the cool breeze and was probably half asleep, otherwise he would have stopped Xiangzi from running away like this. Xiangzi ran away, thinking vaguely that he could sleep well today without having to think about anything anymore, even though he was sweating all over.

Not far from Beichang Street, the northern half of the road was darkened by the locust trees outside the red wall. Just as Xiangzi was about to take a step back, his feet hit something taller. The feet have arrived, and so have the wheels. Xiangzi fell out. Click, the handlebar is broken. "What's wrong?" Mr. Cao said as he spoke. Xiangzi got up on the spot without making a sound. Mr. Cao also sat up briskly. "What's wrong?"

There was a pile of newly unloaded stones to repair the road, but there was no red light.

"Did you fall?" Xiangzi asked.

"No. I'll walk back while you pull the cart." Mr. Cao was still calm and touched the rocks to see if there was anything that had fallen.

Xiangzi touched the broken section of the handlebar: "It's not broken much, sir, you can still sit on it and you can pull it!" As he said that, he pulled the car out of the stone. "Sit down, sir!"

Mr. Cao didn't want to sit down anymore, but he heard Xiangzi's words sounding like crying, so he had no choice but to go up.

Arriving under the electric light at the entrance of Beichang Street, Mr. Cao saw a piece of skin wiped off with his right hand. "Xiangzi, stop!"

When Xiangzi turned around, his face was covered with blood.

Mr. Cao was scared and couldn't think of what to say, "Hurry, hurry -"

Xiangzi was confused and thought he was being taught to run fast. He grabbed his waist and ran home in a hurry.

After putting down the car, he saw blood on Mr. Cao's hands and hurriedly ran to the courtyard to ask his wife for medicine.

"Leave me alone, look at yourself first!" Mr. Cao ran in. Xiangzi looked at himself and began to feel pain. His knees and right elbow were all broken. He thought the sweat on his face was sweat, but it turned out to be blood. Regardless of what he was doing or thinking, he sat on the stone steps of the doorway and stared blankly at the broken handlebars of the car. The brand-new black car had its head folded, revealing two bare pieces of white wood residue. It was very inconsistent and ugly, like a beautiful paper figure that had not been put on its feet and had two naked stalks sticking out of it. Xiangzi stared blankly at the two pieces of white wood. "Xiangzi!" Gao Ma, the Cao family's maid, shouted loudly, "Xiangzi! Where are you?"

He sat still, staring intently at the broken handlebar. The two pieces of white wood seemed to have penetrated into his heart.

"What a fool you are! You don't say a word, you hide here; look, you scared me! Sir, call you!" Gao Ma's words always mix things with emotions, which are obviously complicated and touching. She is a widow in her thirties, clean, cheerful, quick and careful in her work. Elsewhere, some people think she is too open-minded, has too many ideas, and is often a bit arrogant. The Cao family likes to use clean and bright people, but they don’t pay much attention to those small festivals[1]

, so she has been following them for two or three years. Even when the Cao family goes to other places, they always take her with them. "Mr. call you!" she said again. When Xiangzi stood up, she saw the blood on his face clearly: "I'm scared to death, my mother! What's wrong? If you don't move, you've got tetanus! Let's go! There's medicine there, sir!"

Xiangzi walked in front, Gao Ma was nagging behind, and they entered the study together. Mrs. Cao was also here, wrapping her husband's hands with medicine. When she saw Xiangzi coming in, she also said "yo".

"Madam, he fell so hard this time." Gao Ma was afraid that his wife wouldn't be able to tell, so she poured cold water into the basin, and was even more busy talking: "I knew it earlier. He didn't care about his life as soon as he started running. Sooner or later, something will happen. As expected! Why don't you wash him quickly? After washing, put some medicine on him, ugh!"

Xiangzi held his right elbow and did not move. The study was so clean and elegant, and it looked very unseemly to have a big man like him with blood on his face. Everyone seemed to feel that something was wrong, even Gao Ma was speechless.

"Sir!" Xiangzi lowered his head and said in a low but powerful voice: "Sir, find someone else! You can keep this month's salary to clean up the car: the handlebar is broken and the left light has a broken piece of glass; everything else is fine."

"Wash it first, apply some medicine, and then talk about anything else." Mr. Cao said, looking at his hand, while his wife was slowly wrapping gauze on it.

"Wash up first!" Gao Ma also thought of talking again. "Sir didn't say anything. Don't knock me down first!"

Xiangzi still didn't move. "No need to wash it, it'll be fine in a while! A monthly renter fell down someone or hit a car. He's too shameless to..." His words weren't enough to express what he meant, but his emotions had already been vented, and he just had to cry loudly. Resigning and giving up the salary is almost equivalent to committing suicide in Xiangzi's opinion. But responsibility and face seemed to be more important than life at this time, because it was not someone else who fell, but Mr. Cao. If he throws that Mrs. Yang, she deserves it! To Mrs. Yang, he could show off his arrogance on the streets, because she didn't treat him the same way as others, and he couldn't be polite; money was everything, and there was no dignity to speak of, so there was no such thing as rules. Mr. Cao is not that kind of person at all. He had to sacrifice money to save his face. He didn't care who he hated, he only hated his own life. He almost thought: after leaving Cao's house, he would never pull a cart again; even if his own life was worthless, he could still fight for it; what about other people's lives? What should I do if I really want to fall to death? He had never thought of this before, because this time he injured Mr. Cao, so he realized this. Okay, I don’t have to pay for it, I’ll change my career from now on, and I won’t do this thing that costs people’s lives anymore. Pulling carts was his ideal career, and to give it up would be to give up hope. He felt that he would have to live his whole life in a fool's lurch, and he didn't even have to think about becoming a good cart puller because he had grown so big in vain! When we were dispersing the seats outside, he once "copied" him unceremoniously[2]

Buying and selling is ridiculed by everyone, but he can forgive himself for being so shameless because he wants to be strong and want to buy a car. If you get into trouble for asking for a monthly payment, what can you say? This is going to be known. Xiangzi threw someone and broke the car. Who is pulling the chartered car? What the hell! Xiangzi has no way out! He couldn't wait for Mr. Cao to quit him, so he had to get out!

"Xiangzi," Mr. Cao's hands were already wrapped, "wash up! Don't say anything about resigning first. It's not your fault. You should put a red light when you put stones. Forget it, wash up and apply some medicine."

"Yes, sir," Gao Ma remembered what she was saying again, "Xiangzi can't get rid of him. Originally, he threw sir like this! But since sir said it's not your fault, you can't be awkward anymore! Look at him like this, he's not very strong. Damn, he's still like a child, he's really anxious! Madam, please tell him not to worry!" Gao Ma's words were like a gramophone record, speaking in a circle, including everyone, without any trace of succession.

"Wash up quickly, I'm afraid!" Mrs. Cao only said this.

Xiangzi was very confused. Finally, when he heard his wife say that she was afraid of blood, he seemed to have found something that could comfort her. I took out the washbasin and washed a few at the door of the study. Gao Ma was waiting for him inside the door with a medicine bottle.

"What about the arms and legs?" Gao Ma smeared his face. Xiangzi shook his head, "It doesn't matter!"

Mr. and Mrs. Cao went to rest. Gao Ma took the medicine bottle and followed Xiangzi out. When she got to his house, she put down the medicine bottle and stood at the door: "You can apply it yourself later. I said, don't be so obsessed with this matter. Back then, when my old man was alive, I often quit my job. Firstly, I was burdened outside, so he didn't want to be strong and made me angry. Secondly, he was young and arrogant, and if he didn't agree with me, he would leave! Work hard to make money, not a slave; you have Your dirty money, my clay figurine also has a local character; the old lady can't take care of me! Now I'm much better. Once the old man is dead, I have nothing to worry about, and my temper is better. But here - I have been working here for three years; but the change on September 9th is too little, but they are pretty good to people. What we sell is for money; we can't pawn it off. But it has to be said that it is good to take a long-term view: working part-time for three days and six months a year is not a big deal; if you meet a kind boss and can't stand it, the change will be less, but you can still have a little money by living on the job. You're still a little brother, it's easy to get angry. It's not necessary to be too angry. It's better to stay here for a while than to fly around. I'm not talking to them at all. It's good to be together!" She took a breath and said, "Don't be stupid, I'm honest."

Xiangzi's right elbow hurt so much that he couldn't sleep in the middle of the night. After a lot of calculation, he felt that Gao Ma's words were reasonable. Nothing is fake, only money is real. Save money to buy a car; you can’t eat when the car is on fire! Thinking of this, I felt a little peaceful sleepiness.

Mr. Cao packed up the car and did not deduct Xiangzi's wages. Mrs. Cao gave him two pills of "Three Huang Bao Wax", but he didn't take them either. He didn't mention resigning again. Although I felt embarrassed for several days, Gao Ma's words won the final victory. After some days, his life got on the same track again. He gradually forgot about this incident, and all hope sprouted again. When he was sitting alone in the room, his eyes were shining, thinking about how to save money and how to buy a car; he couldn't help mumbling, as if he had a heart attack. His arithmetic is not very clever, but he often chants "six six thirty six" in his heart and mouth; this has little to do with the number of his money, but just by repeating it like this, his heart seems to be fuller, as if he has an account.

He admired Gao Ma quite a bit and felt that this woman had more mentality and ability than ordinary men. Her words were taken from the root. He didn't dare to catch up with her for a chat, but if he met her in the courtyard or at the door, if she had time to say a few words, he would be willing to listen to her. Every time she said something, it was always enough for him to think about it for a long time, so whenever he met her, he would smile stupidly, letting her know that he admired her words, and she felt a little proud, even if she didn't have time, she had to say a few words.

However, as for how to deal with the money, he didn't dare to just follow her idea. Her idea, he thought, wasn't really a bad idea; but it was a bit risky. He was very willing to listen to her, learned a lot of tricks, and was obviously generous; in practice, he still had the same old idea - not to let go of money easily.

Yes, Gao Ma did have a solution: ever since she became a widow, she had given out the little money she could have left in the month, one dollar was a lump sum, two dollars was a lump sum, and it was given to servants, second- and third-class patrolmen, and those doing small businesses, and the interest was at least three cents. These people are often anxious for a dollar, and even if someone lends them a dollar and counts it as two dollars, they still have to reach out and take it. Otherwise, money will not teach them to see; the money they see is poisonous, and it will drain their blood when they take it, but they still have to hold on. They have the courage to pick up anything that can give them a breath of relief; life is about talking about it after taking a breath, and then talk about tomorrow tomorrow. Gao Ma had suffered from this poison when her husband was alive. Her husband came to her drunk and had to spend one dollar. If he didn't, he would get drunk and make a fuss outside the house. She had no choice but to borrow the money immediately no matter how high the interest was. She had learned this method from this experience, not as a revenge, but as a legitimate, almost emergency act of charity. If there are those who are in urgent need of money and those who are willing to lend it, Zhou Yu will give him a yellow cover and he will be willing to accept it!

In terms of purpose, she believed that there was nothing she couldn't do, so in terms of methods, she had to be more powerful. She couldn't use money to float the boat; she said what she said. This requires vision, means, caution, and fierceness, and even if it is good, you will put the eagle down[1]

. She takes no less pains than the bank manager because she requires more caution. Capital comes in different sizes, and doctrines are the same, because this is a capitalist society. It is like a very fine and very large sieve, sifting money from the top bit by bit, the lower the money, the less; at the same time, it also sifts ism downwards, but there are more on the upper and lower sides, because ism is not as afraid of small sieve holes as money. It is intangible and can slip through any tiny hole. Everyone says Gao Ma is great, and she admits it herself; her strength is tempered by hardship and suffering. She gritted her teeth when she thought of the hardships she suffered in the past, how even her husband was so ruthless and unreasonable. She can be kind or vicious, and she knows she can't live in this world otherwise.

She also advised Xiangzi to put the money out, out of good intentions. If he was willing, she could help him: "Tell you, Xiangzi, if you put it in your pocket, a piece will always be a piece! If you put it out, the money will fall! That's right, what are our eyes for? Let the money go after you have seen it accurately, and you can't let the bald-tailed eagle go. As a patrol If the policeman doesn't give you any benefits, or he doesn't return his money, go to his inspector! In a word, he has to put aside his errands. Dare you find out about the payment period. If you don't pay back, Xin Xin will give it to you one to ten. We have to have a clue. OK, let him go and take the blame. Is that okay? You listen to me. "

Xiangzi didn't need to say anything, his air was enough to show that he admired Gao Ma's words. When he thought about it alone, he felt that the money in his own hands was safer than anything else. Yes, it’s a dead end if you do this, and you won’t lose money; but it’s also true that you can’t lose it. He gently took out the few dollars he had left over the past two or three months - all of which were foreign currency - and fiddled with them piece by piece, fearing to make any noise. The foreign currency was so white, thick and eye-catching that he felt that he must not let go of it unless he used it to buy a car. Everyone has their own way, and he can't just follow Gao Ma.

It turns out that in a family named Fang, the entire family, including the servants, had a savings coupon at the post office. Mrs. Fang also advised Xiangzi: "You can set up a zhezi for just one dollar. Why don't you make one? As the saying goes, you will always miss the day but don't look forward to it until you have no time; the young and young will not take advantage of their youth and strength to have a few left, three hundred and sixty-six dollars a year. It can’t be sunny every day for ten days. It’s easy, reliable, and profitable. How can it be convenient if you can use it when you’re saddled? Go and ask for a list. If you don’t know how to write it, I’ll fill it in for you. I’m so kind!

Xiangzi knew that she meant well, and knew that the cook Wang Liu and the nanny Zi Qinma had tricks. He really wanted to give it a try. But one day Miss Fang asked him to put in ten dollars. He took a closer look at the small booklet. There were words on it and a small red seal; in total, huh, it was probably as heavy as a dozen toilet paper. After handing the money in, they drew a few words on the fold and stamped it with a small seal. He felt that this was not a scam, it had to be a scam; just put in the white and colorful cash and just rely on others to paint it three times or five times and it would be over. Xiangzi would not be fooled by this. He suspected that the Fang family had something to do with the post office business - he always thought that the post office was a business with semicolons everywhere, and the brand size was probably very old, at least similar to Ruifuxiang and Hongji - so he was so enthusiastic about promoting business. Even if this is not the case, having cash in hand is better than having small discounts, much better! The money on the booklet is just a few words!

As for the bank number, he only knew that it was a place for "seating". If the patrol police did not stop the parking of cars there, he would definitely be able to get "business" done. As for what was going on inside, he couldn't guess. Yes, there must be a lot of money here; but why come here just for fun[2]

He didn't understand money; he couldn't relate to it anyway, so he couldn't worry about it. There were many things in the city that he didn't understand, and listening to his friends' discussions in the teahouse made him even more confused, because each person had an explanation, and none of them understood what they were talking about. He didn't want to listen anymore, and he didn't want to think too much. He knew that if he wanted to rob, it would be better to rob a bank. Since he didn't want to be a bandit, he could just take his own money and don't worry about anything else. He thought this was the most sophisticated way.

Gao Ma knew that he was eager to buy a car, so she gave him another idea: "Xiangzi, I know you won't let go of the money, so it's a good idea to buy your own car early! If I were a man, if I could also pull a car, I would have to pull my own car; I pull and sing by myself, and I don't ask for anything. Man! If you could do this, I wouldn't change it if you were given a job as a county magistrate! Pulling a cart is hard work, but if I were a man and had the strength, I would not work as a patrolman. I am always standing on the street in winter and summer, earning only two dollars a month, no extra money, no freedom; if I grow a beard, I will not change it. That’s right, there’s really no improvement at all. I mean, by the way, if you want to buy a car quickly, I’ll give you a good idea: buy one for ten or at most twenty people, and it’ll cost you two yuan per person a month. If you do it for a while, it won’t be more than forty yuan right away. ? You have some savings, just go ahead and get a car. Once you get the car, it's not profitable and it's a decent thing. If you really want to hire me, I won't do anything wrong!"

This really made Xiangzi's heart beat faster! If you really have to scrape together thirty or forty yuan, plus the more than thirty yuan in Mr. Liu's hand and the few yuan he has now, wouldn't it be around eighty? Although it’s not enough to buy a 100% new car, you can still do it with an 80% new car! Besides, this way, he could ask Fourth Master Liu to get the money back, instead of letting it go like this forever. If it's 80% new, just let it be 80% new. Hold on to it and replace it when you have more.

But where can we find twenty people like this? Even if you can get it together, it's just a matter of face. If you can wait for the money, why don't you just invite me over? Why don't they invite you over tomorrow? Get up, in these poor years, there are often times when there is a fuss! A good man doesn't ask for help; simply, if he can afford a car, buy it; he doesn't ask for help!

Seeing that Xiangzi didn't move, Gao Ma really wanted to tease him, but when she thought about his sincerity, she felt embarrassed and said, "You're really good! 'Herding pigs in a small alley - go straight'; that's fine!"

Xiangzi didn't say anything. When Gao Ma left, he nodded to himself, seeming to admit that his ability to kill was worthy of admiration, and he was very happy in his heart.

It was already early winter, and at night the alleys were selling sugar-roasted chestnuts. In addition to groundnuts, there was also the sad "chamber pot vomiting". The chamber pot was a stuffed gourd jar with a tile on the handle. Xiangzi bought a large one. The first business was that the chamber pot seller couldn't find any money. Xiangzi felt happy. He looked at the small green chamber pot. It was very interesting. It was green and pouted. "No need for change, I'll do this!" Putting down the stuffy gourd jar, he sent the little green chamber pot inside: "Where is the young master not sleeping? I give you a good thing!"

Everyone was watching Xiaowen - the little boy of the Cao family - taking a bath, and they couldn't help but laugh when they saw this thing. The Cao couple didn't say anything. They probably felt that although this thing was stupid, Xiangzi's kindness should be accepted, so they all smiled and expressed their gratitude to him. Gao Ma's mouth will not be idle: "Look, it's true, Xiangzi! Such a big man can come up with such a brilliant idea; how unpleasant it is!"

Xiaowen liked this thing very much, and immediately filled the small teapot with water from the bathtub: "This small teapot has a big mouth!"

Everyone laughed even harder. Xiangzi straightened his body - because he doesn't know how to get better when he is proud - and walked out. He was very happy. This was something he had never experienced before. Everyone's smiling faces were all facing him, as if he was a very important person. Smiling, he took out the pieces of cash and gently put them one by one into the gourd jar, thinking in his heart: This is more reliable than anything else! When we have enough, we touch the wall and we find that there are more tiles than tiles!

He decided not to ask anyone for help. Even though Mr. Liu is so reliable, he is obviously awkward at times. The money cannot be lost because it is in Mr. Liu's hands, but he still feels a little uneasy. Money is like a ring, it is always better to have it in your own hands. This decision made him happy, and he felt as if his belt had tightened another buckle, making his chest straighter and harder.

It was getting colder and colder, but Xiangzi didn't seem to notice. With a certain idea in mind, there will be more light in front of your eyes; you will not feel cold in the light. Ice appeared on the ground for the first time, and even the soil on the sidewalk solidified, looking dry and solid everywhere. The color of the black soil had turned slightly yellow, as if the moisture had been dispersed. Especially early in the morning, there were several frost edges on the edges of the soil rolled up by the carts, and the small wind blew away the morning glow, revealing a very high, blue, and refreshing sky. Xiangzi was willing to pull the cart for an early trip, and the cool wind blew into his sleeves, making his whole body shiver as if he were taking a cold shower. Sometimes a strong wind blew up and knocked the wind out of him, but he lowered his head, gritted his teeth, and burrowed forward, like a big fish floating against the current; the stronger the wind, the greater his resistance, as if he was fighting to the death with the strong wind. The strong gust of wind made him unable to breathe. He closed his mouth, and for a long time, he let out a burp, as if he had been stabbed in the water. After hiccupping, he continued to run forward, rushing forward. Nothing could stop this giant; no muscle in his body was slack, like a green worm being besieged by ants, his whole body was shaking to resist. This sweat! When he put down the car, straightened his back, exhaled a long breath, and wiped the yellow sand from the corner of his mouth, he felt that he was invincible; watching the wind covered with gray sand sweep past him, he nodded. The wind bent the trees on the roadside, tore the shop curtains to pieces, removed the bills on the wall, and dimmed the sun. It was singing, shouting, roaring, and echoing! Suddenly he galloped straight, like a frightened elf, running wildly; suddenly panicked, rolling around in all directions, like a demon who decided to collide for some reason; suddenly swept across, taking advantage of it to attack everything on the ground, twisting branches, blowing off roof tiles, and breaking wires; However, Xiangzi was there watching; he had just come out of the wind, and the wind could not do anything to him! Victory belongs to Xiangzi! When there was a tailwind, he only had to hold the handlebars firmly and he didn't have to run. The wind would turn the wheel for him, like a good friend.

Naturally, since he was not blind, he must have also seen those old and weak coachmen. They were wearing rags that were worn through by a small breeze and torn apart by a strong wind. They had something tied to their feet. At the entrance of the car, they were shivering, their eyes glaring like thieves. No matter where someone came out from, they would always ask: "Car?!" After making a deal, they warmed up, and their thin and torn clothes were soaked with sweat. When they stopped, their sweat turned to ice on their backs. When the wind hits them, they can't lift a step and have to drag the car away; the wind hits them from above, and they have to lower their heads to their chests; the wind comes from below, and their feet can't find the ground; the wind comes from the front, and they fly kites when they raise their hands; the wind comes from behind, and they can't control the car and themselves. However, they tried every means and exerted all their strength to drag the car to a dead end, saving a life for a few copper coins. After being pulled away from the car, the dirt and dust were turned into mud by sweat and stuck to his face, leaving only three frozen red circles around his eyes and mouth. The days are so short and cold, and there are not many people on the street. Working hard all day like this may not necessarily earn a full meal; but the old ones still have wives and children at home; the young ones have parents and younger siblings! In winter, they are completely in hell. They have more vitality than ghosts, but they are not as free and at ease as ghosts. Ghosts are not as tired as they are! Dying like a dog on the street is their greatest peace and freedom; ghosts who freeze to death, it is said, have some smiles on their faces!

How could Xiangzi not see this? But he had no time to worry about them. Their sins were also his, but he was young and strong, could bear the hard work, was not afraid of cold or wind, had a clean place to live in at night, and had neat clothes during the day, so he felt that he was not comparable to them. Although he was suffering with them now, the degree of suffering was not exactly the same; now that he suffered less, he could escape from here in the future; he thought that if he reached old age, he would not still be pulling a broken cart to starve and freeze. He believed that superiority now would guarantee victory in the future. Just like when you meet a driver in a restaurant or outside a house, they are unwilling to chat together; the driver feels that it is beneath your status to have any contact with the foreign rickshaw drivers. The rickshaw driver's attitude towards the foreign rickshaw driver is a bit like Xiangzi's towards the old, weak and disabled soldiers; they are both in hell, but at a different level. They didn't expect that everyone had to stand together, but each went his own way. Individual hopes and efforts blinded everyone's eyes. Everyone felt that they could start a family and start a business with their bare hands, and each of them groped for his or her own path in the darkness. Xiangzi doesn't care about others. He only thinks about his own money and future success.

The streets are gradually starting to look like they are in the middle of the year. On a clear and windless day, although the weather is dry and cold, there are more colors on the roadside: New Year pictures, gauze lanterns, red candles, silk head flowers, and large and small honey offerings are all displayed, making people feel happy but also a little uneasy; because everyone wants to be happy for a few days during the New Year, but there are also some difficulties. Xiangzi's eyes became brighter, and when he saw the New Year's goods on the roadside, he thought that it was time for the Cao family to give gifts; a gift would always cost him a few cents of wine money. The fixed reward for the festival was two yuan, which was not much. But when people came to celebrate the New Year, he would give them away, and he would have to get two cents or thirty cents for each trip. If you put it all together, it will make a difference; don’t worry about the shortage, just get it in bits and pieces; his boring gourd jar will not hurt anyone! When he had nothing to do in the evening, he stared at the friend Wa who could only eat money but refused to spit it out, and whispered advice: "Eat more, eat more, man! If you eat more, I will be fine!"

The New Year is getting closer and closer, and it’s already Laba in a blink of an eye. Joy or fear forces people to plan and make arrangements; it is still a 24-hour day, but these days are different from usual. They do not allow anyone to spend them casually. They must do something, and they must be done towards the New Year. It is as if time suddenly has consciousness and emotions, allowing people to think and be busy with it. Xiangzi is on the happy side. The excitement on the street, the sound of hawkers, the hope of rewards and change, the New Year's rest, the imagination of good meals... all make him happy and hopeful like a child. He had thought about it, and if he broke out a piece of eighty cents, he would buy some gifts for Mr. Liu. He had to take something with him, firstly to apologize for not being able to see the old man these days because the house was very busy; secondly, he could just hand over the thirty yuan. Spending a dollar and getting that amount back is a good deal. With this in mind, he gently shook the piggy bank, imagining how heavy and sweet the sound would be if he added more than thirty yuan. Yes, as long as he gets the money back, he will have nothing to worry about!

One night, when he was about to shake the cornucopia again, Gao Ma called him: "Xiangzi! There is a lady at the door looking for you; I was coming back from the street, and she asked me about you." When Xiangzi came out, she added in a low voice: "She is like a big black tower! She is very scared of people!"

Xiangzi's face suddenly turned red like a ball of fire. He knew that something was going to be bad!
