Nine

Xiangzi had almost no strength to step out of the gate. I was dazed, my feet were still within the threshold, and I saw Miss Liu through the lights on the street. Her face was probably powdered again, and it looked a bit gray-green when illuminated by the light, like a layer of frost hanging on the black withered leaves. Xiangzi didn't dare to look at her.

The expression on Huniu's face was very complicated: there was a glimmer of desire to see him in her eyes; her mouth was open a little, showing a bit of a sneer; her nose had some wrinkles, folded with some disdain and eagerness; her eyebrows were ridged, showing a seductive and domineering look on her strange pink face. When she saw Xiangzi coming out, she curled her lips a few times and stared blankly at the ground with all the expressions on her face unable to find a suitable home. She swallowed, seemed to suppress her complex expressions and emotions, and used some of the outfield energy gained from Fourth Master Liu. She half-annoyed and half-smiled, pretending not to care and said, "You're good! A meat bun beats a dog and never looks back!" Her voice was high, just like when she usually quarreled with the drivers in the depot. After saying these two sentences, the smile on her face disappeared at all. Suddenly she seemed to feel ashamed and despicable, and she bit her lip.

"Stop yelling!" Xiangzi seemed to put all his strength on his lips and burst out these two words. The sound was very small, but extremely powerful.

"Huh! I'm not afraid!" She smiled maliciously, but she couldn't help but lower her voice slightly. "I can't blame you for hiding from me. I wish there was a little goblin-like old woman here; I've known for a long time that you are not a toy. Don't look at the stupid, big, black and thick one. A Tatar pulls out his pipe and pretends to be stupid when he is not stupid!" Her voice became louder again. "Don't yell!" Xiangzi was afraid that Gao Ma would be secretly obedient behind the door. "Stop yelling! Come this way!" he said as he walked towards the road.

"I'm not afraid of anything, I just have such a loud voice!" He protested, but she followed him.

After crossing the road and coming to Dongbian Road, Xiangzi squatted down against the red wall of the park - he had not forgotten his habit in the countryside. "Why are you here?" "Me? Humph, there are so many things to do!" She put her left hand on her waist and stretched her belly. She looked down at him, thought for a while, and seemed to feel some kindness and pity for him: "Xiangzi! I have something to do with you, something important!"

This soft "xiangzi" dispersed his anger a lot. He raised his head and looked at her. There was still nothing cute about her, but the "xiangzi" still rang slightly in his heart, with a gentle and kind tone, as if he had heard it somewhere before, arousing some undeniable and hard-to-break feelings. He still whispered, but more gently: "What's the matter?"

"Xiangzi!" She leaned closer and said, "I have it!"

"What's there?" He was confused for a moment.

"This!" She pointed to her belly. "You make up your mind!"

Stunned, he said "ah" and suddenly understood everything. Ten thousand things that he had never thought of rushed into his mind. They came so much, so urgently, and so chaoticly that his mind suddenly became blank, like a movie suddenly cut off. The street was very quiet. There were some gray clouds in the sky covering the moon. There was a small breeze from time to time on the ground, blowing the dead branches and leaves. There were a few sharp cats meowing in the distance. Xiangzi's heart was confused and blank, and he didn't even hear these sounds. He cupped his chin with his hands and stared at the ground blankly, as if the ground was about to move. He couldn't think of anything, and he didn't want to think about anything. He only felt that he was getting smaller and smaller, but he couldn't completely shrink into the ground. His whole life seemed to be based on this discomfort; nothing else! Only then did he feel cold, and even his lips trembled slightly.

"Stop squatting and talk! Get up!" She seemed to feel cold and was willing to take a few steps.

He stood up stiffly and followed her north, still unable to find anything to say and feeling numb all over, as if he had just woken up from the cold.

"You have no idea?" She glanced at Xiangzi, with a look of love for him in her eyes.

He had nothing to say.

"It's the twenty-seventh. It's the old man's birthday. You have to come." "Busy, I'll leave at the end of the year!" Xiangzi had not forgotten his own affairs in his extremely confused heart. "I know you're a tough kid, so I won't give you the promise!" Her voice rose again, and the calmness of the street made her voice particularly clear, which made Xiangzi particularly embarrassed. "Who do you think I'm afraid of? What are your plans? If you don't want to listen to me, I don't have time to waste time with you! If you break the rules, I will block your door and scold you for three days and three nights! I can find you wherever you go! I still don't care about you!"

"Can you please stop yelling?" Xiangzi took a step away from her.

"Afraid of shouting, don't be greedy for cheap! You've got a taste for it. You asked me to take the blame alone, and you didn't open your eyes to see who I am!"

"Speak slowly, and I'll listen!" Xiangzi originally felt very cold, but he suddenly felt hot after being scolded. The heat tried to push through his frozen skin. His whole body felt itchy, and there was a special tingling on his scalp that made him panic.

"It's over now! Don't make yourself uncomfortable!" She opened her mouth, revealing her two fangs. "Unyielding Heart, I really love you, don't be ungrateful! I'm not as good as you if you mess with me, let me tell you!"

"No..." Xiangzi wanted to say, "No need to slap him or rub him three times," but he didn't think it through. He knew a lot about Peiping's witticisms, but he just said them poorly. Others said he understood, but he couldn't say it himself.

"Nothing?"

"Tell me about you!"

"I have a good idea for you," the tiger girl stopped and said to him face to face: "Look, if you ask a matchmaker to talk to you, the old man will definitely not agree. He is the one who pulls the car, and you are the one who pulls it. He refuses to go down to visit relatives. I don't care, I like you, I like you. It doesn’t matter if you want me to do anything else! No one can help me with the matchmaking. Once I go to propose marriage, the old man will just think about his dozens of cars. No one who is higher than you can do this. I have to do this myself, so I chose you. We are going to kill him first and tell him later; since I already have it, neither of us can run away! However, it still won’t work if we just go to court and tell him. The boss is very good. Even though he is almost seventy years old, he really wants to marry a little wife. I dare not say that I can still get two or three children. Believe it or not!

"Just say it here, who cares!" She followed Xiangzi's eyes and saw the patrolman: "You didn't pull the car, why are you afraid of him? Who else can he bite off for no reason? That is evil! Let's talk about it! You see, I think this: catch up with the twenty-seven old man On the boss's birthday, you go and kowtow to him. When the new year comes, you go and say New Year's greetings to please him. If I think he likes it, I'll get him some wine or something and let him drink to his heart's content. When he's drunk, you can just accept him as your godfather. He knew that I was in trouble. He would interrogate me and I would tell him "Xu Shu enters Cao Ying without saying a word." When he got really anxious, I told him that he was the recently deceased Qiao Er, the second shopkeeper of our east side room. He had no relatives. Buried in the cemetery outside Dongzhimen, where can the old man go to find out? The old man has no idea. Let's take it easy. It's better to give me to you. I'm a godson and a son-in-law. Anyway, it's not that bad. I just go with the flow and save everyone from making a fool of myself. "

Xiangzi was speechless.

Feeling that she had reached a certain point in her words, Huniu began to walk north, nodding her head low, as if she was appreciating what she had said, and as if giving Xiangzi a chance to think about it. At this time, the wind blew apart a piece of gray cloud, revealing the moonlight, and the two of them had arrived at the north end of the street. The water in Yuhe River has been frozen for a long time. It is quiet, gray, flat and solid, supporting the walls of the forbidden city. There was no sound at all in the Forbidden City. The exquisite turrets, the golden archways, the Danzhu gates, and the pavilions on Jingshan Mountain were all silent, as if they were listening to some sounds that were difficult to hear again. A small breeze blew by, like a sigh, gently passing between the towers and pavilions, as if to tell some historical news. Huniu walked west, and Xiangzi followed Jin Aoyu. There were almost no pedestrians on the bridge, and the dim moonlight shone coldly on the two large ice rinks on the left and right of the bridge. The pavilions in the distance were dim with black shadows, as quiet as if they were frozen on the lake, with only the yellow tiles on the top shining a little. The trees are moving slightly, and the moonlight is even more dim; but the white tower towers high into the clouds, making everything feel cold and desolate, and the entire Three Seas shows the desolate coldness of the north in the artificial carvings. When we reached the bridge, the air-conditioning on both sides of the ice made Xiangzi shiver, and he didn't want to go any further. On weekdays, he pulls the car across the bridge, focusing entirely on his feet, fearing that something will go wrong, without even looking to the left or right. Now, he could take a free look, but in his heart he felt that this scene was a bit scary: the gray cold ice, the slightly moving shadows of the trees, and the pale towers were all so lonely that they seemed to suddenly scream wildly or start walking wildly! Even the large white stone bridge at our feet was remarkably empty and white, and even the lights were a bit desolate. He didn't want to leave, look, or stay with her anymore; he really wanted to jump down, headfirst, break the ice, sink, and freeze in the ice like a dead fish.

"See you tomorrow!" He suddenly turned around and walked back.

"Xiangzi! That's it. See you on the twenty-seventh!" She said towards Xiangzi's broad and straight back. After saying that, she glanced at the White Tower, sighed, and walked west. Xiangzi didn't even look back, as if he was followed by a ghost, and arrived at Tuancheng in a few steps. He walked so hastily that he almost bumped into the city wall. Holding the wall with one hand, he couldn't help but cry. After being stunned for a while, the bridge shouted: "Xiangzi! Xiangzi! Come here! Xiangzi!" Huniu's voice!

He moved two steps toward the bridge very slowly. Huniu leaned up and was walking down, her mouth opened a little: "I said Xiangzi, come here; here you go!" Before he could move a few steps, she was already in front of him: "Here you are, the more than thirty yuan you saved; how many dollars are there? I’ll give you a small change! It’s just to show my heart that I care about you and protect you! Don’t be ungrateful for anything else! Just take it and don’t blame me for losing it!”

Xiangzi took the money - a dozen bills - and was stunned for a while, unable to find anything to say.

"Okay, let's see you on the twenty-seventh! See you there or not!" She smiled. "The advantage is yours, you can calculate it carefully yourself!" She turned and walked back.

He clutched the dozen bills and stared at her blankly until he reached the back of the bridge and covered her head. Gray clouds covered the moonlight again; the lights became brighter, and the bridge was extremely white, empty, and cold. He turned around, let go, and walked back like crazy; when he reached the street gate, the pale and desolate shadow of the bridge was still in his heart, as if it had only been a blink of an eye.

When he got to the house, he first counted the bills; after counting them two or three times, the sweat in his palms made the bills sticky, and the total was not accurate. After counting, put them in a stuffy gourd jar. Sitting on the edge of the bed, staring blankly at the pottery, he planned not to think about anything; if he had money, he would have a solution, and he believed that this piggy bank would solve everything for him, so he didn't have to think about anything. Yuhe, Jingshan, White Pagoda, Bridge, Huniu, Belly... were all dreams; when I woke up from the dream, I had more than 30 yuan more in my bank account, really!

Having seen enough, he hid the money and planned to take a good nap. He would be able to sleep through the huge difficulties. Let’s talk about it tomorrow!

Lying down, he couldn't close his eyes! Those things are like a swarm of bees, you come out, I go in, there is a thorn on the tip of each belly!

He didn't want to think about it, but it was also because he couldn't think about it. Huniu had blocked the road and he couldn't escape.

Better to stomp away. Xiangzi can't leave. Even if he was asked to guard the White Tower in Beihai, he would be happy; he just couldn't go to the countryside! Going to another city? He couldn't think of a better place than Peiping. He couldn't leave, he wanted to die here.

Since you don't want to leave, you don't need to waste your energy thinking about anything else. If Huniu can say it, he can do it; if he doesn't follow her way, she will always follow him to make trouble; as long as he is in Peiping, she will find him! With her, you have to tell the truth and don't try to play tricks. If she gets anxious, she will carry out Mr. Liu. If Mr. Liu betrays one or two people - needless to say - he can kill Xiangzi in any secluded place!

After thinking about Huniu's words from beginning to end, he felt like he had fallen into a trap, and his hands and feet were all caught by the clamps, making it impossible for him to escape. He couldn't criticize her ideas one by one, so he couldn't find any loopholes in her. He just felt that she was casting a desperate net, and not even a small fish could escape! Since he couldn't think about it one by one, he made it all come together, like a thousand-jin gate, and pressed it all on his head. Under this irresistible oppression, he felt that a coachman's lifelong luck was contained in two words - bad luck! A rickshaw driver, since he is a rickshaw driver, should not do anything, not even the ladies should stick to it; if they stick to each other, they will make big mistakes. Mr. Liu has dozens of cars, but Huniu will bully him with a stinky guy! He didn't have to think too hard; if he was going to accept his fate, well, he could kowtow to his godfather and then wait to marry that stinking monster. If you don't accept your fate, you will have to break it!

Thinking of this, he put aside Huniu and Huniu's words; no, this is not her power, but the fate of the rickshaw driver, just like a dog must be beaten and beaten, even children will hit it with two sticks for no reason. What is the use of such a life? Just be willing to sacrifice everything!

He stopped sleeping, kicked off the quilt, and sat up. He decided to get some wine and get drunk; what are things and what are rules? ×Your grandma! Get drunk and sleep! Twenty-seven? Even if you are twenty-eight, you will not kowtow to see who wins Xiangzi!

Putting on his big cotton-padded jacket, he picked up the small rice bowl used as a tea bowl and ran out. The wind is getting stronger, the gray clouds in the sky have dispersed, and the moon is very small, giving off a cold light. Xiangzi just came out of the hot quilt and kept breathing. There were almost no pedestrians on the street, and there were only one or two foreign cars on the roadside. The driver covered his ears with his hands and stamped his feet beside the car to keep warm. Xiangzi ran to the small shop in the south. In order to save the heat, the door had been closed, and payment and delivery were collected through a small window. Xiangzi asked for four taels of white stems and three big ounces of groundnuts. Holding the wine bowl, Ping didn't dare to run, but walked as fast as a sedan bearer back to the house. He hurriedly got into bed, chatting his upper and lower teeth for a while, unwilling to sit up again. The wine had a spicy smell on the table. He didn't like smelling it, and he didn't even seem to be interested in the peanuts. This burst of cold air was like a basin of cold water waking him up. His hands were too lazy to stretch out, and his heart was no longer so warm. After lying there for a long time, his eyes looked at the wine bowl on the table beside the quilt. No, he couldn't destroy himself because of that entanglement, and he couldn't break his drinking habit. Things were indeed difficult to handle, but there was always a crack for him to slip through. Even if there is absolutely no way to escape, he should not roll into the mud first; he should open his eyes and see clearly how others push him down.

He turned off the light, covered his head completely with the quilt, and wanted to just sleep. Still unable to sleep, I opened the window and took a look. The window paper was reflected blue by the moonlight in the courtyard, as if it was about to dawn. The tip of my nose felt the coldness in the room, and the cold air contained the smell of alcohol. He sat up suddenly, touched the wine bowl, and took a big gulp!

ten

When it comes to individual solutions, Xiangzi is not that smart. He doesn't have the courage to do a complete liquidation. So, there was nothing I could do, and I was filled with grievances all day long. Just like all life, after being damaged, you have no choice but to clean up the mess yourself. The cricket that had shaken off its thighs still wanted to crawl on those little legs. Xiangzi didn't have a definite idea. He just wanted to pass by slowly day by day, one by one, and he didn't want to jump at all.

There were still more than ten days before twenty-seven, and he completely noticed that on this day, everything he thought about, chanted in his mouth, and dreamed about was twenty-seven. It seemed that as soon as he passed twenty-seven, he had a solution to everything, even though he knew he was deceiving himself. Sometimes he also thinks about things far away, such as going to Tianjin with dozens of dollars in his hand; when he gets there, he happens to change his career and stop pulling carts. Can Huniu still chase him to Tianjin? In his mind, any place he went to by train must be far away, and she couldn't catch him no matter what. It was a good idea, but he knew in his conscience that this was only a last resort, and it would be better to decide whether he would be in Peking or not! In this way, he thought about the day on the twenty-seventh. He still thought that it would save trouble. As long as he got through this level, he could get through the matter without changing the situation; even if he couldn't get rid of it completely, after passing each level, it was a level.

How to survive this level? He had two ideas: one was to ignore her and simply not go to celebrate her birthday. The other was to do as she told him. Although these two ideas are different, the result is the same: if he doesn't go, she won't give up; if he goes, she won't let him go. He still remembered that when he first pulled a cart, he imitated others and ducked into alleys when he saw them. In order to get closer, he mistakenly entered Luoquan Hutong; he made a circle and went back to the original street. Now he entered such a small alley again, as if no matter which end he went to, the result would be the same.

In the midst of having no other choice, he tried to think better of it, and just wanted her. So what couldn't he do? However, no matter where he thought about it, he felt suffocated. Thinking about her appearance, he could only shake his head. Regardless of appearance, think about her behavior; hum! Just because he is so strong and well-behaved, and yet he marries such a loser, he can't see anyone else, and he won't even have the honor to see his parents after he dies! Who knows for certain whether the child in her belly is his? Yes, she will bring several cars; can you be sure? Fourth Master Liu is not someone to be trifled with! Even if everything goes well, he can't stand it. Can he do it better than Huniu? All she had to do was stretch out her little finger, and she could make him dizzy, and he couldn't recognize the north, south, east, west. He knows how powerful she is! If you want to start a family, you can't have her at all, there's nothing else to say! If you want her, you will lose him, and he is not someone who looks down on himself! no way!

Having no way to deal with her, he turned to hate himself and wanted to slap himself in the mouth. But, to be honest, I did nothing wrong. She had arranged everything, just waiting for him to take advantage of her. The problem seems to be that he is too honest. If you are honest, you will definitely suffer. It is unreasonable!

What makes him even more sad is that he has no place to complain about his grievances. He has no parents, brothers, or friends. On weekdays, he felt that he was a hero with his head in the sky and his feet on the ground, free from worries. Only now did he understand and repent, one cannot live alone. Especially for those who are in the same industry, they all seem a little cute now. If he had a few friends on weekdays, he thought, he would not be afraid of a big man like himself, no matter how many Huniu there were; they would give him advice and work hard for him. However, he is always alone; it is not easy to catch friends at the last moment! He felt a little bit more afraid than he had ever felt before. If this continues, no one will bully him; he alone cannot withstand the sky!

This fear made him doubt himself. In winter, when the host was having dinner or listening to a play, he would always carry the water bottle of the carbide lamp in his arms because it would freeze if left in the car. He had just run away, and he was covered in hot sweat. He pressed the cold water bottle to his chest, which made him shiver immediately. It may take a while before the water bottle becomes a little warm and energetic. But on weekdays, he doesn't think there's anything unreasonable about it; sometimes when he wears it, he thinks it's a kind of advantage. Those who pull broken cars don't need carbide lamps at all. Now, he seemed to see that he could only earn so much money in a month, but he had to endure all the hardships. Even a small water bottle was not allowed to be frozen, but had to be held in front of his chest. His chest was so wide, it seemed that a small water bottle was not worth anything. Originally, he thought that pulling a cart was his ideal job, as he could start a family and start a business by pulling a cart. Now he shook his head secretly. Don't blame Hu Niu for bullying him, he turned out to be just a person who is worse than a small water bottle!

On the third night when Huniu was looking for him, Mr. Cao went to watch a night movie with his friends. Xiangzi was waiting in a small teahouse with the small tube like a piece of ice on his chest. It was extremely cold, and the doors and windows in the small teahouse were tightly closed. It was filled with gas, the smell of sweat, and the smell of dry cigarette smoke. Despite this, there is still a layer of ice on the window. Most of the people drinking tea were people pulling monthly rickshaws, and some leaned their heads against the wall, taking advantage of the warmth in the room and closing their eyes to take a nap. Some of them held bowls of white wine and let everyone drink it, and then slowly talked about it in an orderly manner. After taking a sip, they smacked their lips and let out a loud sigh. Some of them were holding big rolls and biting off half of them, making their necks thick and red. Some of them had a sullen face and complained to everyone, how come from early in the morning to now, he has not stopped yet, and his body has been wet and dry, dry and wet, countless times! Most of the rest of the people were chatting among themselves. After hearing these two sentences, they all fell silent for a while. Then, like birds exploding their nests, they all thought of the grievances of the day and wanted to tell everyone about them. Even the man eating the big cake made enough space in his mouth to move his tongue. He swallowed the cake and spoke at the same time. Even the tendons in his head jumped: "You think you're a fucking postpartum woman? I'm going to burp!" at two o'clock. I still haven’t had my teeth cracked yet! I’ve been to the front door three times! This day, my butthole is so cold that I’m so deflated!” He glanced at everyone, nodded, and took another bite of the cake.

This turned everyone's words to the weather, and everyone talked about their hardships with the weather as the center. Xiangzi never said a word, but he paid attention to what they said. Although everyone's words are different in tone, tone, and facts, they are all curses and injustices. These words, touching the grievances in his heart, were like raindrops falling on the dry soil, and he absorbed them all. He could not, and would not, tell everyone what he said from beginning to end; he could only absorb some of the bitter taste of life from other people's words. Everyone was suffering, and he was no exception; after getting to know himself, he also wanted to sympathize with everyone. When everyone talked about the sad parts, he frowned; when everyone talked about the funny parts, he curled his lips. In this way, he felt that he was on the same page with them, and that everyone was a bitter friend. Even though he said nothing, it didn't matter. In the past, he thought that everyone was short-tongued and could not make a fortune just by talking all the time. Today seemed to be the first time that I felt that they were not just talking, but talking for him, telling him and all the coachmen's difficulties.

While everyone was chatting in the middle of the excitement, the door suddenly opened and a burst of air-conditioning came in. Almost everyone looked out angrily to see who was so unpopular and pushed the door open. The more anxious everyone is, the slower the people outside the door are, as if they are deliberately annoying[1]

. The waiter in the teahouse yelled half-hurriedly and half-laughing: "Hurry up, I'm a lonely uncle! Don't let out all the heat!"

Before he finished speaking, the person outside the door came in, also a cart puller. He looked to be in his fifties, and he was wearing a cotton-padded jacket that was neither short enough nor long enough, like a lotus basket, and the cotton on his lapels and elbows was exposed. The face seemed to have not been washed for many days, and the flesh color could not be seen. Only the two ears were red from the cold, as red as fruit about to fall. His pale hair was tied to a messy moustache under a broken hat; there were some ice beads hanging on his eyebrows and short beard. As soon as he came in, he grabbed the bench and sat down, struggling to say: "Make a pot."

This teahouse has always been a gathering place for monthly rickshaw pullers. Old rickshaw pullers like this would never come in on ordinary days.

Everyone looked at him, as if they felt something deeper than what he just said, and no one wanted to speak again. On weekdays, there would always be one or two young men who were not very sensible and would come up with a few wisecracks to make fun of such tea guests, but today no one said anything.

Before the tea was brewed, the old coachman's head slowly lowered, lowering and lowering, and his whole body slipped away.

Everyone immediately stood up: "What's the matter? What's the matter?" As they said this, they all wanted to run forward.

"Don't move!" The experienced teahouse owner stopped everyone. He went over alone, untied the old coachman's neck, helped him up on the spot, put a chair behind his back, and held his shoulders with his hands: "Sugar water, hurry up!" After saying that, he listened to the old coachman's neck and said to himself: "It's not phlegm!"

No one moved, but no one sat down again. They all blinked and looked towards the door in the smoke that filled the room. Everyone seemed to be thinking in unison: "This is our example! By the time your hair turns gray, no one will fall to their death!"

As soon as the sugar water was placed on the old coachman's lips, he hummed twice. Still closing his eyes, he raised his right hand - it was so black and shiny, as if it had been painted - and wiped his mouth with the back of his hand.

"Drink some water!" the shopkeeper said into his ear.

"Ah?" The old coachman opened his eyes. Seeing that he was sitting on the ground, he curled up his legs and tried to stand up.

"Drink some water first, don't be busy." The shopkeeper said, letting go of his hand. Almost everyone ran over.

"Hey! Hey!" The old coachman glanced around, held the tea bowl in both hands, and drank the sugar water in mouthfuls.

After slowly drinking the sugar water, he glanced at everyone again: "Hey, thank you for your help!" He said it very gently and kindly, and it definitely didn't sound like it came from the mouth of that unshaven man. After saying that, he wanted to stand up again, but three or four people went over to help him up. There was a smile on his face, and he said so gently: "Okay, okay, it doesn't matter! I'm cold and hungry, and I feel dizzy for a while! It doesn't matter!" Although his face was covered with thick mud, the smile made everyone seem to see a gentle and fair face.

Everyone seemed moved. The middle-aged man holding the bowl of wine had already drank all the wine, his eyes were red, and he was crying now: "Come on, two taels!" When the wine arrived, the old coachman was already sitting on a chair against the wall. He was a little drunk, but he put the wine in front of the old coachman politely: "My invitation, you can drink it! I am also forty years old. To be honest, it is just a matter of making do with monthly payments. One year is a matter of one year, and the legs know it! In two or three years, I will be like you! You are [2]

Are you almost sixty? "

"You're still young, fifty-five!" The old coachman took a sip of wine. "It's cold and I can't sit in the seat. My stomach is empty. I drank wine for a few days, so I feel warmer! Walking here, I really can't hold on anymore and want to come in to get warm. It's too hot in the room and I haven't eaten, so I fainted. It doesn't matter, it doesn't matter! Thank you guys!"

At this time, the old man's gray hair like hay, mud on his face, hands like charcoal sticks, and the shabby hat and cotton-padded jacket all seemed to be shining with pure light, like the statues in the ruined temple. Although broken, they were still dignified. Everyone looked at him as if they were afraid he would leave. Xiangzi remained speechless and stood there blankly. When the old coachman said that his stomach was empty, he ran out quickly, and then ran back again, holding ten steamed buns stuffed with mutton on a cabbage leaf in his hand. He brought it to the old man's eyes and said: Eat it! Then, he sat down and lowered his head, as if he was very tired. "Hey!" The old man seemed to be happy or crying, nodding to everyone. "It's my brother after all! Hold the seat, no matter how hard you work for him, it's hard to ask for an extra penny before the end!" As he said this, he stood up and started to walk out.

"Eat!" Everyone shouted almost in unison.

"I asked Xiao Ma to go, my little grandson is watching the car outside!" "I'll go, you sit down!" said the middle-aged coachman, "You can't lose the car here, don't worry, the patrol cabin is just across the way." He opened the door a little and said: "Little Ma! Little Ma! Your grandfather told you to put the car here!"

The old man touched the bun several times with his hand, but never picked it up. As soon as Xiao Ma entered the door, he picked one up: "Little Ma, be good, here it is for you!" Xiao Ma was about twelve or thirteen years old, with a thin face and round clothes. His nose was red from the cold, with two strips of white snot hanging from his nose, and he wore a pair of broken ear hats on his ears. Standing next to the old man, he took the buns with his right hand, then automatically picked up another with his left hand, and took a bite on each one.

"Hey! Slowly!" The old man put one hand on his grandson's head, picked up a bun with the other, and slowly put it into his mouth. "Grandpa, just eat two, and it's all yours! After we finish eating, let's pack up the car and go home. If it's not so cold tomorrow, let's get out of the car earlier. Right, little horse?"

Xiao Maer nodded to the buns and sniffed: "Grandpa, please eat three, and the rest is mine. I'll take grandpa home later!" "No!" The old man smiled proudly at everyone: "Let's walk later, it's cold sitting in the car."

The old man finished his portion, drank the wine in his glass, and waited for the pony to finish the buns. He took out a rag, wiped his mouth, and nodded to everyone: "My son went to the army and never looked back; my wife——"

"Don't say that!" Xiao Ma'er's cheeks were as big as two peaches, and he kept eating and talking to stop Grandpa.

"It doesn't matter what you say! We are not outsiders!" Then he whispered to everyone: "The child is serious, not to mention how strong he is! My wife is also gone. We, father and son, live in this car. The car is broken, but it is our own, so we don't have to worry about the car every day. We earn more and less, and we, father and son, are struggling, helpless! Helpless!"

"Grandpa," Xiao Ma'er finished eating the buns and pulled on the old man's sleeve, "we still have to make a trip. We still don't have money to buy coal tomorrow morning! It's all you. Just now, the twenty-year-old pulled the back door, just follow me, and you won't go! There will be no coal tomorrow morning, let's see what you do!"

"If you have a way, grandpa will lend you five kilograms of briquettes on credit."

"Want some more firewood?"

"That's right! Good boy, let's eat; after eating, we should take a walk!" After saying that, the old man stood up, walked around in a circle and said to everyone: "Thank you for your help, brothers!" He reached out to pull the little horse, and the little horse stuffed the uneaten bun into his mouth. Some of them sat still, while others followed them out. Xiangzi was the first to follow. He wanted to see the car.

A very dilapidated car, the paint on the car panel has been cracked, the handlebars have been worn to reveal the wood grain, a broken lamp that makes a slight noise, and the poles of the carport are tied with hemp ropes. The pony found a match in his ear cap, struck it on the sole of his shoe, held it in his two little black hands, and lit the lamp. The old man spat into the palm of his hand, groaned, and picked up the handlebar, "See you tomorrow, buddies!"

Xiangzi stood blankly outside the door, looking at the old man, the young man and the broken car. The old man was talking as he walked, his voice rising and falling; the lights and shadows on the road were sometimes bright and sometimes dark. Xiangzi listened and watched, and felt a kind of discomfort in his heart that he had never felt before. In the little horse, he seemed to see his past; in the old man, he seemed to see his future! He had never let go of a piece of money easily, but now he felt very happy and bought ten buns for the old and the young. He didn't go back into the house until they were out of sight. Everyone started talking and laughing again, and he felt confused. After paying for the tea, he walked out again, pulled the car outside the cinema and waited for Mr. Cao.

It's so cold. There was some gray sand floating in the sky, and the wind seemed to be rushing on it. The stars were not very visible, only a few big ones were trembling slightly in the sky. There was no wind on the ground, but there was a cold air everywhere. There were several long cracks cracked by frost on the ruts. The soil was gray-white, as cold and as hard as ice. Xiangzi stood outside the cinema for a while, already feeling cold, but unwilling to go back to the teahouse. He wanted to think quietly by himself. The old man and the young man seemed to have shattered his best hope - the old man's car was his own! Since the first day he pulled a car, he decided to buy his own car, and he still works hard all day long for this goal; with his own car, he thought that he would have everything. Humph, look at that old man!

Isn't it because he wants to buy a car that he doesn't want Huniu? Buy a car, save money, and then marry a wife with nothing; hey, look at the pony! Having a son of his own may not be like that.

Thinking about it this way, it seems that there is no need to resist Huniu's threat; anyway, I can't jump out of the circle, what kind of girl can't I want? Besides, she might have brought a few cars with her, so why not enjoy the ready-made blessing for a few days! Once you have seen through yourself, there is no need to look down on others. Huniu is Huniu, so don’t say anything!

After the movie ended, he hurriedly put the water bottle in place and lit the lamp. He even took off his cotton-padded jacket, leaving only a small coat. He wanted to run away and forget everything. It wouldn't matter if he fell to his death!

When Xiangzi thought of the old man and the pony, he gave up all hope and thought it would be fun one day at a time. Why would he grit his teeth and be hard on himself all the time? ! He seemed to understand that the fate of the poor was that the two ends of the date pit were pointed: it was lucky to avoid starving to death when he was young; it was difficult to avoid starving to death when he was old. Only the middle section is young and strong, not afraid of hunger, fatigue and hard work, and can still look like a human being. In this period, if you don't dare to do it when you should be happy, you are a complete fool; after passing this village, there will be no such shop! Thinking about it this way, he didn't even want to worry about Huniu.

When he saw the stuffy gourd jar, his mind changed again. No, you can’t just do it casually; you only have a few dozen yuan left to buy a car, so you can’t waste all your hard work; at least you can’t just throw away the little savings in the pot, it’s not easy to save it! We still have to take the right path, for sure! But, what about Huniu? There is still no way, I still have to worry about that hateful Twenty-Seven.

Worried to the point of being helpless, he hugged the earthen jar and muttered to himself: Do whatever you want, this money is mine anyway! No one can take it away! With this little money, Xiangzi is not afraid of anything! If I am anxious, I will stomp my feet and run. If I have money, my legs will move!

The street is getting more and more lively. Sugar melons for sacrifice to the kitchen stove are placed all over the street. Wherever you go, you can hear the sound of "come and chop the sugar". Xiangzi was originally looking forward to celebrating the New Year, but now he is not excited at all. The more chaotic the streets are, the tighter his heart is. The terrible twenty-seven is right in front of him! His eyes were sunken, and even the scar on his face was a little dark. While pulling the car, the street was so messy and the ground was so slippery, he had to be extra careful. Worrying and paying attention were mixed, and he felt that his energy was not enough. Thinking about this, he forgot about that. He was often startled, and his body was itchy like a child having prickly heat in the summer.

On the afternoon of the day of the memorial ceremony, the soft east wind brought dark clouds. The weather suddenly became warmer. When it was almost time to turn on the lanterns, the wind became lighter and sparse snowflakes fell from the sky. The sugar melon sellers were all in a hurry. The weather was warm and there were snowflakes. Everyone sprinkled white soil on the sugar, fearing that it would all stick to one place. The snowflakes fell a little and turned into small snow particles, which fell white to the ground with a soft sound. After seven o'clock, shops and people began to offer sacrifices to the stove. Dense light snow was caught in the shadows of the incense and cannons, adding a bit of a gloomy atmosphere to the bustle. People on the street looked a little anxious. Those on foot and in cars were all eager to go home to worship the gods, but the ground was slippery and they did not dare to walk. The candy vendors were eager to sell the goods for the festival, and they were shouting out of breath, which was shocking to hear.

It was about nine o'clock, and Xiangzi took Mr. Cao home from Xicheng. After passing the lively market section of Xidan Archway and heading east into Chang'an Street, the crowds gradually became sparse. There was a thin layer of snow on the flat asphalt road, which was a little dazzling by the street lights. Occasionally a car would pass by, its lights shining from a distance, and the small snow particles would shine a little yellow in the light, like thousands of gold sands sprinkled on it. Approaching the Xinhuamen area, the road was originally extremely wide, and the light snow made it even more eye-opening, and everything seemed more serious. "Chang'an Archway", the gate tower of Xinhua Gate, and the red wall of Nanhai are all wearing plain crowns, with red walls and red pillars, quietly showing the dignity of the old capital under the light. At this time, it seems that there are no residents in Peiping. It is just a jade palace with only some old pines silently catching the snowflakes. Xiangzi didn't have time to look at these beautiful scenery. When he saw the "Jade Road" in front of him, he just wanted to run home in one step. The straight, white, calm road seemed to keep his home in his mind. But he couldn't run fast. Although the snow on the ground was not thick, when he picked up his feet, a thick layer would stick to the soles of his shoes. When he stamped on it, it would stick to the soles again. Graupel grains are very small, but they are heavy and heavy. They are both heavy to the feet and blind to, so he cannot run very fast. It was not easy for snow particles to melt when they hit him. A thin layer had accumulated on his shoulders. Although it was nothing, it made him feel uncomfortable being wet. There are not many shops in this area, but the sound of cannons in the distance continues, and from time to time a double shot or five ghosts are fired in the black sky. Sparks scattered, and the sky became increasingly black, almost terrifying. He listened to the cannons and saw the sparks and darkness in the air, and he wanted to get home immediately. But he didn't dare to let go of his legs, it was awkward!

What made him even more unhappy was that starting from Xicheng, he felt like there was a bicycle following him. When we arrived at West Chang'an Street, the street became a little quieter, and we could feel the following behind us - the vehicles were rolling through the thin snow. Although the sound was not loud, it was noticeable. Xiangzi, like other rickshaw drivers, hates bicycles. Cars are nasty, but they make a lot of noise and can be avoided from a distance. The bicycle would get into any cracks it saw, and it would swing this way and that, making me dizzy just looking at it. It’s better to make sure you don’t make any mistakes when bringing something with you. If something goes wrong, it’s always the rickshaw driver’s fault. The patrolmen’s calculation is that the rickshaw driver is easier to deal with than the cyclist anyway, so it’s not the right one to send the rickshaw driver first. Several times, Xiangzi wanted to use the brakes to stop the car, causing him to fall behind him. But he didn't dare. The man pulling the cart had to endure his anger. Whenever he wanted to stamp his soles, he had to shout: "Stop!" When he arrived at Nanhai Qianmen, the street was so wide, and the bicycle was still following closely behind. Xiangzi got even more angry. He deliberately stopped the car and dusted off the snow on his body. He stood still, and the bicycle passed by the car. The people in the car also looked back. Xiangzi deliberately bothered him and waited until the bicycle was far away before he picked up the handlebar and cursed: "I hate it!" Mr. Cao's "humanism" made him unwilling to put up the cotton shed to protect the wind. Even the canvas shed was not allowed to be put up until heavy rain, in order to teach the driver to save some energy. With this light snowfall, he thought there was no need to set up a carport, and besides, he wanted to see the snow scene at night. He also noticed the bicycle. After Xiangzi finished cursing, he whispered, "If he keeps following you, don't stop at the door of your house. Go to Mr. Zuo's place at Huanghuamen; don't panic!"

Xiangzi was a little panicked. He only knew that riding a bicycle was annoying, but he didn't know that there was something scary about it - since Mr. Cao didn't dare to go home, this guy must have a great background! After running for dozens of steps, he caught up with the man; he was deliberately waiting for him and Mr. Cao. The bicycle passed to Xiangzi, and Xiangzi glanced at the people on the bicycle. It was clear at a glance that he was on the detective team. He often met people from the team in teahouses. Although he never spoke to them, he knew their looks and dress. This person's outfit looked familiar to him: big green coat, woolen hat, worn very low.

When he arrived at the intersection of Nanchang Street, Xiangzi took advantage of the opportunity to turn a corner and glanced back. The man was still following him. He almost forgot about the snow on the ground and stepped harder on his feet. The long, straight and white road has only some cold lights, and a detective is chasing behind it! Xiangzi had never had this kind of experience, and he was sweating. When he arrived at the back gate of the park, he turned around and still followed! When he arrived at the door of his house, he didn't dare to stop, and he was a little reluctant to leave. Mr. Cao didn't say a word, so he had to continue running north. I ran to the north exit in a hurry, and the bicycle was still following me! He entered the alley and followed him! Out of the alley, still following! When he went to Huanghuamen, he shouldn't have entered the alley. It wasn't until he reached the north entrance of the alley that he realized it. He admitted that he was a little lost and became even more angry. After running behind Jingshan, the bicycle headed north to the back door. Xiangzi wiped his sweat. The snow is lighter, but there are a few more snowflakes among the snow particles. Xiangzi seemed to like snowflakes, flying freely in the air, not as annoying as snow particles. He turned around and asked: "Where are you going, sir?"

"Go back to Zuozhai. Someone asked you about me, but you said you don't know me!"

"Yes!" Xiangzi was excited in his heart, but he couldn't ask in detail.

When they arrived at Zuo's house, Mr. Cao asked Xiangzi to pull the car in and quickly close the door. Mr. Cao was still calm, but he didn't look very good. After giving instructions to Xiangzi, he walked in. As soon as Xiangzi pulled the car into the doorway and put it away, Mr. Cao came out again, accompanied by Mr. Zuo. Xiangzi knew Mr. Zuo and knew that Mr. Zuo was a good friend of the house. "Xiangzi," Mr. Cao's mouth moved quickly, "You go back by car. Tell your wife I'm here. Teach them to come too, come by car, and call another one. You don't have to teach the car you took to wait. Understand? Okay! Tell your wife to bring the application things and the paintings in the study. Do you understand? I'm calling my wife right now to tell you again, in case she gets anxious and forgets what I said, so you can remind her."

"Can I go?" Mr. Zuo asked.

"No! That person just now may not be a detective, but I have something going on in my mind, so I have to be on guard. Can you call a car first?" Mr. Zuo went to call for a car. Mr. Cao told Xiangzi again: "When the car comes, I gave you the money. Teach the wife to pack her things quickly; it doesn't matter anything else, just be sure to take the children's things, and the few paintings in the study, those paintings! When the wife has finished packing, teach Gao Ma to call for a car and come here. Do you understand this? After they leave, you lock the door and move to the study to sleep. There is a phone there. Can you make phone calls?"

"I won't make outgoing calls, but I will answer them." In fact, Xiangzi didn't like connecting on the phone, but he didn't want to make Mr. Cao anxious, so he had to agree.

"That's okay!" Mr. Cao continued, speaking very quickly: "If there is any movement, don't open the door! We have all left, and you are the only one left. They will never let you go! If things go wrong, you turn off the light, jump to the backyard and jump to the Wang family. Do you know the Wang family? Yes! Hide in the Wang family for a while before leaving. My things , don’t worry about your own things, just jump over the wall, so I don’t have to take it away from you! If you lose something, I’ll pay for it in the future. Okay, I’ll call my wife, and you’ll tell her again later. Don’t worry, the guy on the bike just now may be a detective, or he may not be.”

Xiangzi was very confused and seemed to have many questions to ask, but because he was eager to remember what Mr. Cao had told him, he did not dare to ask any more questions.

The car came, and Xiangzi got in with a dazed look on his face. The snow was falling moderately, and the things outside the car could not be seen clearly. He sat with his back straight, his head almost touching the carport. He wanted to think about it, but his eyes were only focused on the red arrow in front of the car, which was so bright and cute. The small brush in front of the driver automatically moves left and right to brush away the breath on the glass, which is also quite interesting. Just now he seemed tired of seeing this, the car had arrived at the door of his house, and he got out of the car feeling strange.

Just as he was about to ring the electric bell at the street door, someone seemed to come out of the wall and grabbed his wrist. Xiangzi instinctively wanted to grab his hand, but he had already seen the person clearly and stopped moving. It was the detective who was riding the bicycle just now.

"Xiangzi, don't you recognize me?" The detective smiled and let go.

Xiangzi took a breath and didn't know what to say.

"Don't you remember when you taught us to pull us to Xishan? I am that Platoon Commander Sun. Do you remember?"

"Ah, Platoon Leader Sun!" Xiangzi couldn't remember. When he was dragged up the mountain by the soldiers, he didn't care who was the platoon commander or the company commander.

"You don't remember me, but I remember you; the scar on your face is a good mark. I just followed you for a long time, and I was a little afraid to recognize you at first. I looked left and right, and there can't be anything wrong with this scar!"

"Is something wrong?" Xiangzi went to ring the bell again.

"Of course there's something going on, and it's important! Let's go in and talk about it, okay?" Platoon Leader Sun - now a detective - reached out and rang the bell.

"I have something to do!" Xiangzi's head suddenly broke out in sweat, and he said fiercely in his heart: "It's not enough to hide from him, how can I invite him in?"

"Don't worry, I'm here for your own good!" the detective showed a sly smile. When Gao Ma opened the door, he stepped in and said, "Excuse me!" Before Xiangzi could say a word to Gao Ma, he pulled him inside and pointed at the concierge: "Do you live here?" After entering the house, he looked around and said, "The house is quite clean! You're not bad at all!"

"Is something wrong? I'm busy!" Xiangzi couldn't listen to these idle questions anymore. "Didn't I tell you? It's important!" Detective Sun was still smiling, but his tone was very stern. "Let me tell you, the guy named Cao is a traitor. If you catch him, he will be shot. He still can't run away! We finally have acquaintance, and you served me in the barracks. Besides, we are both people on the street, so I have to bear a big punishment to send you a message! If you run away a step late, you will be stuck in the nest when you come back, and no one can run away. We work hard to eat, how can we file a wrongful lawsuit with them? Is this right?"

"I'm sorry!" Xiangzi was still thinking about Mr. Cao's instructions. "Who are you sorry for?" Detective Sun had a smile on his lips and angular eyes. "They caused the trouble themselves, who are you sorry for? They dared to take responsibility, and we have to suffer, so we can't get along! Needless to say, I will lock you up for three months. You are used to it like a wild bird, and I will make you sit in a dark room. Can you bear it? Besides, they are in jail and have money to support them, so they can't bear the pain; you, my good brother, if you don't have a hard hand, you must be tied to a urine bucket! This It’s a small matter, but if they spend money to carry out a campaign, they will be sentenced to several years in prison. If they can’t explain it to the officials, it will be strange if we don’t offend anyone. Is it unfair to go to the bridge to eat black dates? You are a sensible person, and you can’t bear the consequences. Let me tell you, good brother, there is no such thing as our miserable brothers!”

Xiangzi was frightened. Thinking of the pain of being dragged away by the soldiers, he would imagine what it would be like to be imprisoned. "Then I have to go and leave them alone?"

"You care about them, who cares about you?!"

Xiangzi had no answer. After being stunned for a while, even his conscience nodded: "Okay, I'm leaving!"

"Are you leaving just like that?" Detective Sun sneered.

Xiangzi lost his head again.

"Xiangzi, my good friend! You are so stupid! As a detective, am I willing to let you go?"

"Then -" Xiangzi was so anxious that he didn't know what to say.

"Don't pretend to be stupid!" Detective Sun stared at Xiangzi's eyes: "Maybe you also have some savings, use it to buy a life! I don't earn as much as you do in a month, so I have to eat, clothe and support the family, so I just rely on outsiders to talk to you! Think about it, I can I can’t let you go with a slap in the face. Friendship is friendship. Can I come to persuade you if there is no friendship? But things are things. I don’t want to do anything. I don’t need to talk to people outside. "

"How much?" Xiangzi sat on the bed.

"You get as much as you want, the price is determined!"

"I'm waiting to go to jail!"

"Is this what you said? Don't regret it?" Detective Sun put his hand into his cotton robe, "Look at this, Xiangzi! I can take you right away. If you resist arrest, I will shoot! I will take you away right away, don't say anything Money, even your clothes have to be peeled off as soon as you enter the prison gate. You are a sensible person, you can do it yourself! "If you have time to squeeze me, why not squeeze Mr. Cao?" Xiangzi said after a long time.

"That's the real criminal. If you catch it, there will be a reward. If you can't hold it, it's not. You, you, my stupid brother, letting you go is like a fart; killing you is like wiping a bug! Take the money, you go your own way; if you don't take it, okay, see you on the overpass! Don't bother. Come on, you’re such a big man! Besides, I can’t afford this money all by myself. The guys have to give me some help. How much money do you have to pay for it? How much money do you have?”

Xiangzi stood up, his mind jumped so high, and he clenched his fists.

"It's none of your business. Let me tell you first. There are a lot of people outside! Hurry up and get the money! I'm looking for a face, so don't be ungrateful!" Detective Sun's eyes were very ugly.

"Who did I offend?" Xiangzi said with a crying voice and sat on the edge of the bed again. "You didn't trick anyone; you just hit the spot! People are born rich, and we are all born from the bottom. Don't say anything anymore!" Detective Sun shook his head, as if with infinite emotion. "Come on, consider it my fault that I have wronged you, don't bother me anymore!"

Xiangzi thought for a while, but there was nothing he could do. His hands were trembling as he took the stuffed gourd jar out of the quilt.

"Let me take a look!" Detective Sun smiled, took the jar and touched it against the wall.

Xiangzi's heart burst when he saw the money scattered on the ground.

"That's it?"

Xiangzi didn't make a sound, only trembling.

"Forget it! I won't kill them all, friends are friends. You must also know that it is cheap to buy a life with this money!"

Xiangzi didn't say anything yet, he was shivering and trying to wrap himself up in blankets.

"Then don't move!"

"It's so cold..." Xiangzi's eyes became angry.

"I told you not to move, just don't move! Get out!"

Xiangzi swallowed, bit his lip, opened the door and walked out.

The snow had fallen more than an inch, and Xiangzi walked with his head down. Everything is white except for some big black footprints behind him.

twelve

Xiangzi wanted to find a place to sit down and think over everything, even if he could only cry once after thinking about it, he would know what he was crying about; things were changing so fast that his brain could no longer catch up. There was no place for him to sit, and there was snow everywhere. The doors of the small teahouses were all closed, and it was past ten o'clock; even if they were open, he would not go in. He would rather find a quiet place, knowing that the tears in his eyes could fall at any time. Since there is no place to sit, I have to walk slowly; but where to go? In this silvery world, there is no place for him to sit down, and no place for him to go; in this vast white world, only hungry birds and desperate people know what it means to lament.

Where to go? This has become a problem, so don’t think of anything else! Going to a small shop? no! With his clothes, he could lose something in the middle of the night, not to mention how terrible the lice in the store were. Go to a bigger store? He couldn't afford to go, he only had five dollars in hand, and it was his entire property. Going to the bathhouse? Come to the door at twelve o'clock, you can't stay overnight. Nowhere to go.

Because I had nowhere to go, I felt more and more embarrassed. After living in the city for several years, all I have left is a pair of clothes and five yuan; even the bedding is gone! From this, he thought about tomorrow, what should he do tomorrow? Pulling a car, and then pulling a car, huh, the result of pulling a car is that I can't find a place to live, and I just have some money left and it is robbed by others! For a small business, the capital is only five yuan, and even the carrying poles have to be bought immediately. Besides, which business can definitely earn a profit? Pulling a cart can be done on flat ground for only thirty to forty cents, but small business requires capital and there is no hope of earning three meals a day. After all the capital is invested, and then you go to pull the car, why don't you take off your pants and fart, and lose five yuan in vain? You can't let go of these five dollars easily. This is your last hope! When you are a servant, you are not good at it: you are not good at serving people; you are not good at washing clothes and cooking! I can't do anything, I can't do anything, I'm just a stupid, black and thick piece of trash!

Unknowingly, he came to Zhonghai. When we got to the bridge, the left and right sides were empty, and everything we could see was covered with snowflakes. Only then did he seem to realize that the snow had not settled yet. He touched his head and found that the woolen hat was already very wet. There was no one on the bridge, not even the policeman was hiding. A few electric lights were covered in snowflakes and seemed to blink uncontrollably. Xiangzi looked at the snow outside and felt confused.

He stood on the bridge for a long time, and the world seemed to have died, without any sound or movement. The gray snowflakes seemed to have had a chance, falling in a panic and briskly, trying to bury the world without anyone noticing. In this silence, Xiangzi heard the whispers of his conscience. Don't worry about yourself first, you still have to go back and see the Cao family first. Only Mrs. Cao and Gao Ma were left, not a single man! Wasn't the last five yuan given by Mr. Cao? Not daring to think anymore, he picked up his legs and walked back very quickly.

There were some footprints outside the door, and there were two newly marked car lanes on the road. Could it be that Mrs. Cao has left? Why didn't the man named Sun take them?

I didn't dare to go over and push the door open, for fear of being caught again. Looking around, there was no one. His heart beat fast. Let's give it a try. He was homeless anyway. If he was caught, he would be caught. I pushed the door gently, but it was open. After walking two steps along the wall, I saw the light in my own house, my own house! He wants to cry. I bent down and walked over, went to the window and listened. There was a cough in the room, Gao Ma's voice! He opened the door. "Who? Oh, you! You scared me to death!" Gao Ma covered her heart, composed herself, and sat on the bed. "Xiangzi, what's going on?"

Xiangzi couldn't answer, he just felt like he hadn't seen her for many years, and there was a ball of heat in his heart.

"What's going on?" Gao Ma asked as if she was about to cry: "You haven't come back yet. My husband called us and asked us to go to the left house. He also said that you would be here right away. When you came, didn't I open the door for you? When I saw that you were with a stranger, I said nothing and hurried in to help my wife pack things. You never went in. In the dark, I taught my wife and I to scratch around. The young master was already sleeping soundly. I wrapped my bag and went to the study to pick up the paintings. What's wrong with you? I asked you! I came out to see you, okay, you're gone! My wife was so angry that I had to call for a taxi. Okay, my wife and I were trembling.

, I said, madam, let’s go, I watched. When Xiangzi comes back, I will rush to Zuozhai immediately; if he doesn’t come back, I will accept my fate! How could you say this! What's the matter with you? Tell me! "

Xiangzi said nothing.

"Speak! Is it alright to just stand there in a daze? What's going on?"

"Let's go!" Xiangzi finally found a sentence: "Let's go!" "Are you looking after the house?" Gao Ma's anger subsided a little.

"When you meet sir, you say, the detective caught me, but, but, he didn't catch me!"

"What kind of words does this sound like?" Gao Ma was so angry that she almost laughed.

"Listen!" Xiangzi gasped: "Tell sir, run away. The detective said, we can definitely catch him. The left house is not a safe place either. Run! Now that you're gone, I'll jump to Wang's house and sleep overnight. I'll lock the door here. Tomorrow, I'll go find my business. I'm sorry, Mr. Cao!"

"The more you talk, the more confused I become!" Gao Ma sighed. "Come on, let me go. The young master may still be cold, so hurry up and take a look! When I see the husband, I will tell him what Xiangzi said and teach him to run quickly. Tonight Xiangzi locks the door and jumps to Wang's house to sleep; tomorrow he will go find trouble. Isn't that what it is?"

Xiangzi nodded with great shame.

After Gao Ma left, Xiangzi locked the door and returned to the house. The broken gourd jar was still thrown on the ground. He picked up a tile, looked at it, and threw it on the ground as before. The bedding on the bed was not moved. Strange, what is going on? Is Detective Sun not a real detective? cannot! If Mr. Cao didn't see any danger, why would he abandon his home and run away? not understand! not understand! He sat on the edge of the bed unconsciously. As soon as he sat down, he stood up again as if he was shocked. Can't stop here for long! What if the man named Sun comes back again? ! My mind was spinning very fast: I'm sorry for Mr. Cao, but Gao Ma brought back a letter to teach him to run quickly, so it was okay. In terms of conscience, Xiangzi did not intend to deceive others, and he was wronged himself. My own money is lost first, so I can no longer care about Mr. Cao's. Talking to himself, he picked up and packed his clothes while chatting like this.

He picked up his bedding, turned off the lights, and ran to the backyard. He put down his bedding, grabbed the wall with his hands and shouted in a low voice: "Old Cheng! Old Cheng!" Old Cheng is the Wang family's coachman. No one agreed, so Xiangzi made up his mind to jump over first. Throwing the bedding over, it fell on the snow without making any sound. His heart skipped a beat. Then he climbed up the wall and jumped over. Picking up the bedding on the snow, he quietly went to find Lao Cheng. He knows where Lao Cheng is. Everyone seemed to have gone to sleep, and there was no sound in the whole hospital. Xiangzi suddenly felt that it was not difficult to be a thief. He gained some courage and walked down-to-earth. The snow was very solid and made a little noise. After finding Lao Cheng's house, he coughed. Lao Cheng seemed to have just laid down: "Who?"

"I, Xiangzi! Open the door!" Xiangzi said very naturally and softly, as if he heard Lao Cheng's voice, like hearing the comfort of a relative. Lao Cheng turned on the light, put on a ragged fur coat, and opened the door: "What's the matter? Xiangzi! It's midnight!"

Xiangzi went in, put the bedding on the ground, sat down on it, and said nothing.

Lao Cheng was in his thirties, and the flesh on his face and body was all lumpy and hard. On weekdays, Xiangzi didn't have much friendship with him. They just nodded and talked when they met. Sometimes, Mrs. Wang and Mrs. Cao went out to the streets together, and they had the opportunity to drink tea and rest together. Xiangzi admired Lao Cheng very much. Lao Cheng ran very fast, but he was in a panic and couldn't hold the handlebars firmly. Although Lao Cheng was quite good as a person, Xiangzi could not fully admire him due to this shortcoming.

Today, Xiangzi thinks Lao Cheng is completely cute. Sitting there, I couldn't say anything, but I felt grateful and affectionate. Just now, he was standing on the bridge in Zhonghai; now, he was sitting in the house with an acquaintance; the sudden change made his heart feel empty and at the same time, he felt a little warm.

Lao Cheng got under the quilt again, pointed to the ragged leather jacket and said, "Xiangzi, you can smoke, there is one in your pocket, Bieye." The Villa brand cigarettes have been changed to "Bieye" by the coachmen since they were born.

Xiangzi didn't smoke, but it seemed he couldn't refuse this time. He took a cigarette and put it between his lips and smoked it.

"What happened?" Lao Cheng asked, "Quited your job?"

"No," Xiangzi was still sitting on the bed, "there was trouble! Mr. Cao's family has all run away, and I don't dare to look after the house alone!"

"What's the trouble?" Lao Cheng sat up again.

"I can't tell. Anyway, there was quite a mess. Even Gao Ma left!"

"The four doors are wide open and no one cares?"

"I locked the door!"

"Hmph!" Lao Cheng pondered for a long time, "Can I tell Mr. Wang to go ahead?" He said and started to put on his clothes.

"Let's talk about it tomorrow, it's hard to explain the matter!" Xiangzi was afraid that Mr. Wang would question him.

The thing that Xiangzi couldn't explain was this: what hours does Mr. Cao teach at a university? There is a student named Ruan Ming in the school who has always been good to Mr. Cao and often comes to talk to him. Mr. Cao is a socialist, and Ruan Ming’s thoughts are more intense, so the two get along very well. However, their age and status caused a slight conflict between them: Mr. Cao, from the perspective of a teacher, should teach with all his heart, while students should hand over their homework well and not be careless about their grades because of personal feelings. In Ruan Ming's view, in this chaotic world, an ambitious young man should engage in some revolutionary undertakings, regardless of whether his homework is good or bad. He interacts with Mr. Cao, firstly because he can still get along with each other, and secondly because he hopes that because of his relationship, he can get enough grades to upgrade, no matter how bad his test scores are. People with ideals in troubled times are often scoundrels, and there are many forgivable examples in history.

When it came time to take the exam, Mr. Cao did not give Ruan Ming a passing grade. Ruan Ming's grades, even if Mr. Cao gave him a passing grade, were enough to get him suspended from school. But he particularly hates Mr. Cao. He thought Mr. Cao didn't understand face too much; face has the same value as revolution in China. Because he was eager to do something, Ruan Ming despised learning. Because I despise learning, I slowly get used to laziness, and want to gain everyone's admiration and love without any effort; no matter what, my thoughts are progressing! Mr. Cao didn't give him a passing grade. He clearly didn't understand an aspiring young man. So, don't get close to each other on weekdays! Since he had a good friendship with him on weekdays, but was embarrassed during the exam, he thought Mr. Cao was insidious. His grades were irreparable, and he couldn't resist even if he was suspended from school. He wanted to vent his anger on Mr. Cao. Since I have lost my studies, I will ask a teacher to accompany me. In this way, it can be a bit artificial and show off one's own power. Ruan Ming is not someone to be trifled with! Besides, if you can break into a new group through this incident, it's better than having nothing to do.

He edited what Mr. Cao said in the lecture hall and what he chatted with him about political and social issues on weekdays, and went to the party headquarters to report that Mr. Cao was promoting radical ideas among young people.

Mr. Cao also heard about it, but he thought it was funny. He knew how incomplete his socialism was, and how his traditional art interests hindered radical action. Ridiculous, he actually got the title of revolutionary mentor! It was ridiculous, so he didn't pay much attention to it, even though his students and colleagues told him to be careful. Calmness does not - in troubled times - guarantee safety. Winter break is a good opportunity to clear out the school, and detectives get busy investigating and making arrests. Mr. Cao has felt someone following him several times. The figure behind him changed his expression from playful to serious. He needs to think about it: this is a good opportunity to build a reputation; spending a few days in jail is less troublesome and safer than setting off a bomb, and has the same value. Going to jail is a qualification for being an important person. However, he refused. He refused to use any means to create a false reputation for himself. Based on his conscience, he hated that he could not become a warrior; based on his conscience, he also refused to be a fake warrior. He went to Mr. Zuo. Mr. Zuo had an idea: "When necessary, move to my place and they won't search me!" Mr. Zuo knows people; people are more powerful than the law. "You come here to stay for a few days and hide away. Finally, we are afraid of them. Then we can clear it out, which may cost a couple of dollars. If you have enough face and money, you will be fine if you go home."

Detective Sun knew that Mr. Cao often went to the left house, and he also knew that he would definitely go to the left house when he was caught up. They did not dare to offend Mr. Zuo, but had to scare Mr. Cao. Let's drive him to the left house, so that they can hope to get money, and it will be very respectable. Knocking on Xiangzi was not part of the detectives' plan, but since they saw Xiangzi and took charge of the job, why not pick up the pieces first?

By the way, Xiangzi met a "point" and deserved it. Everyone has a way, there are gaps everywhere, only Xiangzi can't run away, because he is a cart puller. A cart puller swallows coarse grains, and what comes out is blood; he has to exert his greatest strength and get the lowest reward; he has to stand at the lowest point in the world, waiting for the blows of all people, all laws, and all hardships.

After burning a cigarette, Xiangzi still couldn't figure out the reason. He was like a chicken being held in the hands of a cook. He just wanted to take a breath and had no other ideas. He was very willing to talk to Lao Cheng, but he had nothing to say. His words were not enough to express his thoughts. He had experienced all the hardships, and his mouth could not open, like a mute. If you buy a car, the car is lost; if you save money, the money is lost; all your efforts are only for others to bully you! No one dared to provoke him, even a wild dog had to hide away, and he was still bullied until he was angry! Don't think about the past now, what about tomorrow? I can’t go back to Cao’s house, so where can I go? "I'll sleep here for the night, okay?" he asked, as if a wild dog had found a sheltered corner and would tolerate it for a while; but even this matter had to be understood clearly to see if it would hinder others.

"You can stay right here. Where can you go in the snow and ice? Is the ground okay? You can come up and squeeze in!"

Xiangzi refused to go up and squeeze, so the ground was fine.

When Lao Cheng fell asleep, Xiangzi tossed back and forth and still couldn't fall asleep. The cold air on the ground soon turned the mattress as cold as iron. He curled up his legs, and his calves seemed to be cramped. The cool breeze that came in through the crack in the door pricked my head like a bunch of tiny needles. He closed his eyes tightly and covered his head, unable to sleep. Hearing Lao Cheng's cry, he felt impatient and wished he could stand up and give Lao Cheng a good beating. It was getting colder and colder, making my throat itchy from the cold, and I was afraid of waking up Lao Cheng with a cough.

Unable to sleep, he really wanted to get up secretly and visit Cao's house again. Anyway, the matter has been blown, and there is no one in the courtyard, why not go and get a few things? The few money that I had saved so hard was robbed by others because of Cao Zhai's affairs. Why not steal something. If you lose money because of Cao Zhai's affairs, wouldn't it be appropriate for Cao Zhai to pay for it? Thinking of this, his eyes lit up and he immediately forgot about the cold; let's go! It’s so hard to get money, lost, and it’s so easy to get it back, let’s go!

He had already sat up and hurriedly lay down again, as if Lao Cheng was looking at him! My heart jumped. No, you can't be a thief, no! I just took off my clothes and failed to do what Mr. Cao asked me to do, which is a shame. How can I steal from him again? Can't go! If you die poor, don’t steal!

How do you know others won't steal it? Who knows that the man named Sun took something? He sat up again. A dog barked in the distance. He lay down again. Still can't go, others will steal, just steal, your own conscience is clear. I am so poor that I can’t let myself have so many dark spots in my heart!

Besides, Gao Ma knows that when he comes to Wang's house, if something is lost at night, it must be him, or it must be him if it's not him! Not only did he refuse to steal it, he was also afraid that others would get in. If you really lose something this night, you won’t be able to wash it off even if you jump into the Yellow River! He wasn't cold anymore, but his palms were a little sweaty. What to do? Jump back to the house and watch? Don't dare. Your life was bought with money, you can no longer throw yourself into a trap. If you don’t go, what if you lose something?

Can't think of an idea. He sat up again, with his legs hunched over, his head almost touching his knees. My head feels heavy and I want to close my eyes, but I dare not sleep. The night was so long that there was no time for Xiangzi to close his eyes.

I sat there for an unknown amount of time and changed my mind countless times. Suddenly his heart lit up, and he stretched out his hand to push Lao Cheng: "Lao Cheng! Lao Cheng! Wake up!"

"What are you doing?" Lao Cheng was very reluctant to open his eyes: "Pee, there is a chamber pot under the bed."

"Wake up! Turn on the lights!"

"What's the matter with a thief?" Old Cheng sat up in a daze.

"Are you awake?"

"Um!"

"Old Cheng, look at it! This is my bedding, these are my clothes, and these are the five yuan given by Mr. Cao. Is there nothing else?"

"No, what are you doing?" Lao Cheng yawned.

"Are you aware? These are the things I have. I didn't take anything from the Cao family?"

"No! My brothers, we have been living in the house for a long time, and our hands are sticky, how about it? If you can do it, do it; if you can't do it, don't do it; you can't take other people's things! Is this the problem?"

"Do you understand?"

Lao Cheng smiled: "That's right! I said, aren't you cold?"

"OK!"
