A cross-stroke across the bridge of the nose is guaranteed.

Thirteen

Because of the snow light, the sky seemed to light up earlier. As the end of the year approaches, many people buy chickens and feed them, and the chickens crow several times more than before. Roosters crow everywhere, and there is a lot of auspicious snow in a good year. Xiangzi didn't sleep well all night. In the second half of the night, he endured several naps, feeling dazed and sleepless, rising and falling like floating on water, feeling uneasy. The more he slept, the colder he got. He heard the crow of chickens everywhere, and he couldn't hold on any longer. Not wanting to disturb Lao Cheng, he curled up his legs, covered his mouth with the quilt and coughed, not daring to get up. I endured and waited, feeling very anxious. After finally waiting until daybreak, the sound of cart wheels and the shouts of the driver could be heard on the street, and he sat up. It was cold even sitting down, so he stood up, fastened the buttons, opened the door a little and looked outside. The snow was not that thick, and it probably stopped falling in the middle of the night. The sky seemed to be clear, but it was so gray that it was hard to see clearly, and even there was a very light gray shadow on the snow. At a glance, he saw the big footprints he left last night. Although they were buried in snow again, the pits were still very real.

Firstly because he had something to do, and secondly because he wanted to eliminate traces of the snow. Without saying a word, he fumbled for a broom in the corner of the room and went to sweep away the snow. The snow was heavy and difficult to sweep, and he couldn't find a big bamboo broom for a while. He bent down very low and scraped hard. When the upper layer was swept away, some snow particles remained close to the ground, as if they had grasped the ground. After straightening his back twice, he swept the entire outer courtyard and piled all the snow under the two small willow trees. He was a little sweaty, warm, and relaxed. Stomping his feet, he took a long breath, which was very long and white.

Entering the house and putting the broom in its place, he wanted to pack up his bed. Lao Cheng woke up and yawned. Before he could finish speaking, he spoke with his hands, "Isn't it early?" His tone was very complicated. After saying that, he wiped his tears and took out a cigarette from his leather jacket pocket. After taking two puffs of cigarette, he finally woke up. "Xiangzi, don't leave yet! Wait for me to get some boiling water, and let's have a hot pot of tea. This night will be enough for you!"

"Shall I go?" Xiangzi also expressed his kindness. However, as soon as he said it, he remembered the horror of last night, and his heart suddenly became clogged.

"No; I'm going! I still have to treat you!" With that, Lao Cheng quickly put on his clothes, leaving all the buttons unbuttoned. He only put a sack on his ragged leather jacket and ran out with a cigarette in his mouth: "Hey! The yard has been swept? You really can do it! Please treat you!" Xiangzi felt a little happier.

After a while, Lao Cheng came back, carrying two large bowls of sweet porridge, as well as countless water chestnut pancakes and small fried fried ghosts. "I haven't made tea, let's have some porridge first, come on, eat it; if it's not enough, go buy it; if you don't have money, we can get it on credit; do the hard work, just don't pout, come on!"

The sky was completely bright, and the room was deserted and bright. The two of them held the bowls and drank, the sound was loud and sweet. No one said anything, and they ate all the sesame cakes and fried dough sticks in one go. "How?" Lao Cheng picked a sesame on his teeth.

"It's time to go!" Xiangzi looked at the bedroll on the ground.

"Tell me, I still don't understand what's going on!" Lao Cheng handed Xiangzi a cigarette, and Xiangzi shook his head.

After thinking about it, Xiangzi was embarrassed not to tell Lao Cheng everything. Stuttering, he told what happened last night. Although it was very laborious, it was not incomplete.

Lao Cheng curled his lips for a long time, seeming to think of something interesting. "In my opinion, you should go find Mr. Cao. The matter can't be left alone, and the money can't be lost like this! Didn't you just say that Mr. Cao told you to run away if something goes wrong? Then, who is to blame when you teach the detective to stop you as soon as you get out of the car? It's not that you are unfaithful, but that the matter is too evil, and you can't help but look after yourself first. Let me see, there is nothing wrong with this. Go and tell Mr. Cao everything. I think he will not blame you, and he will pay for it by chance! Go ahead, put your bed here, and go to him as soon as the sun comes out."

Xiangzi felt relieved and felt a little sorry for Mr. Cao, but what Lao Cheng said was very reasonable - how could the detective block himself with a gun and still care about the Cao family's affairs? "Let's go!" Lao Cheng urged again. "I think you were a little confused last night; no one can keep their head off when something urgent happens. The advice I'm giving you now is definitely good. I'm older than you, so I've always been through more things. Let's go, isn't the sun shining?"

A little light from the morning sun, through the snow, has illuminated the whole city. The sky is blue, the snow is white, there is light in the sky, there is light on the snow, and a golden flower flashes between the blue and white, which makes people so happy that they can't open their eyes! Just as Xiangzi was about to leave, someone knocked on the door. Lao Cheng went out to take a look and called out from the doorway: "Xiangzi! I'm looking for you!"

Wang Er from the left house, his nose dripping with cold water, stomped the snow off his feet in the doorway. When Lao Cheng saw Xiangzi coming out, he said, "Sit inside!" The three of them came to the house together.

"What's that?" Wang Er rubbed his hands and said, "I'm here to see the house. How do I get in? The door is locked. What's that? It's cold after the snow. It's so cold! What's that? Mr. Cao and Mrs. Cao left early in the morning to go to Tianjin, maybe Shanghai, I can't tell. Mr. Zuo asked me to come and see the house. What's that? It's so cold!"

Xiangzi suddenly wanted to cry! Just when I was about to follow Lao Cheng's advice and go find Mr. Cao, Mr. Cao would leave. After being stunned for a long time, he asked: "Mr. Cao didn't say anything about me?"

"What the hell, no. We all got up before dawn, and couldn't even think about talking. The train is, what, it leaves at 7:40! So what, how do I get across that courtyard?" Wang Er was eager to get there.

"Jump over!" Xiangzi glanced at Lao Cheng, as if handing Wang Er to Lao Cheng, and he picked up his bedroll.

"Where are you going?" Lao Cheng asked.

"There is no other place for people and factories to go!" This sentence expressed the grievance, shame and helplessness in Xiangzi's heart. He had no choice but to surrender! All roads were sealed, and he could only look for the black tower-like tiger girl on the snow-white ground. He cares about dignity, strength, loyalty, and loyalty; all of them are of no use because he has a "dog" life!

Lao Cheng took it and said, "You can go your own way. This is not in front of Wang Er. You haven't touched a single plant or tree in Cao's house! Let's go. When you come to this street, come in and have a chat. Maybe I can find out something good and recommend it to you. After you leave, I will send Wang Er over there. Is there any coal?"

"Coal and firewood are all in the hut in the backyard." Xiangzi carried the bedding.

The snow on the street is no longer so white, and the snow on the road has been rolled down by the wheels, revealing a bit of ice color. On the dirt road, the ground was blackened and whitened by horses, which was a pity. Xiangzi didn't think anything, just carried the bedding and walked forward. Went to Renhe Depot in one breath. He didn't dare to stop. Once he stopped, he knew he wouldn't have the courage to go in. He kept walking in, his face burning with heat. He made up a sentence to say to Huniu: "I'm here, let's see what happens! It doesn't matter what you do, I can't do anything!" When he saw her, he repeated this sentence in his heart several times, but he still couldn't say it out, because his mouth was not so convenient.

Huniu had just gotten up, her hair was in a moustache, her eyelids were swollen, and her dark face was covered with a layer of white goosebumps, like a frozen chicken with its hair plucked off.

"Yo! You're back!" It was very affectionate, and her eyes shone with laughter. "Lease me a car!" Xiangzi lowered his head and looked at the snow that had not melted away on the toes of his shoes.

"Talk to the old man." She whispered, and after speaking, she pursed her lips towards Dongjian.

Mr. Liu was drinking tea in the room. There was a large white stove in front of him, with a flame more than half a foot high. Seeing Xiangzi come in, he said with half annoyance and half smile: "How are you still alive?! You forgot about me! Calculate, how many days have you not been here? How are things going? Have you bought a car?"

Xiangzi shook his head, feeling a stabbing pain in his heart. "You still have to give me a car to pull you, Fourth Master!"

"Hmph, it's blown again! Okay, go pick one for yourself!" Fourth Master Liu poured a bowl of tea, "Here, have a bowl first."

Xiangzi picked up the bowl, stood in front of the stove, and drank heavily. The tea was very hot and the fire was very hot. He felt a little sleepy. He put down the bowl and was about to come out when Master Liu stopped him.

"Wait, let's go, what are you busy with? Let me tell you: you came just in time. The 27th is my birthday, and I have to build a tent. Please treat me. You can help me for a few days, so there is no need to pull the cart. They," Mr. Liu pointed to the courtyard, "are not reliable. I don't want to teach them to be careless. You're just kidding. Just do whatever you have to do, don't wait for me to tell you. I'll treat you to hot pot at noon." "Yes, Fourth Master!" Xiangzi thought. Now that he's back here, let's leave everything to him. They can do whatever they want. "Isn't that right?" Tiger Girl said while holding it[1]

Came in, "It's still Xiangzi, the others are not as good."

Fourth Master Liu smiled. Xiangzi lowered his head further.

Walking to her room, she counted the money for Xiangzi and whispered in a low voice: "Be energetic! Please please the old man! There is hope for our affairs!"

Xiangzi was speechless and not angry. He seems to have given up and doesn't want to think about anything. He just wants to mess around for a day. Eat when you have something to eat, drink when you have something to drink, and work when you have something to do, and keep your hands and feet busy, turning around every day. I am like a donkey who is trying to learn how to pull a millstone.

He also felt that he would not be happy no matter what. Although he refused to think, speak, or lose his temper, there was always something stuck in his heart. When he was working, he temporarily forgot about it. Whenever he had some free time, he would feel this thing - soft, but always so big; it didn't have any definite smell, but it was choking, like a sponge. With this thing stuck in his heart, he worked hard to do things in order to make himself too tired to move so that he could fall asleep. Leave the things at night to Meng and the things during the day to his hands and feet. He seems to be a dead man who can work. He sweeps the snow, he buys things, he orders gas lights, he washes the car, he moves tables and chairs, he eats the reward meal from Mr. Liu, he sleeps, he knows nothing, there is no words in his mouth, no thoughts in his heart, he can only vaguely feel that sponge-like thing!

After the snow on the ground was swept away and the snow on the house gradually melted, the shed builder "called Gao'er" went up to the house and set up the shed. What we talked about was a greenhouse that could look into the yard, with eaves on three sides, railings on three sides, and glass windows on three sides. There are glass partitions and hanging screens in the shed, and any wood is wrapped in red cloth. Fourth Master Liu, because Qingjiu has to do something lively, so the first thing to do is to build a decent shed. Because of this, he sent Xiangzi to activate the gas lamp. Cook, be sure not to miss the mark. In fact, these two items will never be mistaken, but the old man is worried. Xiangzi just came back because of this, and Fourth Master Liu asked him to borrow mahjong tiles, three or four pairs, so that he would have to gamble happily. I borrowed playing cards and was sent to borrow a gramophone. There must be some noise for birthday celebrations. Xiangzi's legs didn't stop for a while, and he ran until eleven o'clock at night. He was used to pulling a cart, so walking with empty hands was even more tiring than running; when he came back from the last trip, he, even he, couldn't lift his feet. "Good boy! You can do it! Huniu stood aside and winked at Xiangzi.

The color screen hangs above, depicting battle scenes from the "Three Kingdoms", including the Third Battle of Lu Bu, Chang Ban Slope, the Burning Battalion, etc. The big painted faces and the second painted faces are all riding horses and holding swords and guns. Fourth Master Liu did not answer Bie'er, but thought for a moment: "Where is the chatterbox? Sing, sing!"

I don’t know where I borrowed a broken gramophone. Every sound sounded like a cat stepping on its tail, making it sound so heart-wrenching! Fourth Master Liu didn't care, as long as there was some noise.

By the afternoon, everything was ready, just waiting for the cook to come and sit down the next day. Mr. Liu made a tour of the place and saw flowers and trees everywhere, and nodded to himself. That night, he went to ask a gentleman from Tianshun Coal Shop to take care of the accounts. His surname was Feng, a native of Shanxi, and he was the most careful in accounting. Mr. Feng immediately came over to take a look and asked Xiangzi to buy two red ledgers and a piece of red paper. He cut the red paper, wrote some words about "longevity" and pasted it everywhere. When the cards failed, Mr. Liu got a little angry and called some cart drivers, "Kaibao, do you have the guts?"

Everyone is willing to come, but they don't have the courage to come with Fourth Master Liu. Who doesn't know that he has opened a treasure game before!

"Coming for Tongzi'er?" a coachman tried to ask.

"Keep your copper, Liu Si won't let the children play!" The old man swallowed a cup of tea and touched his bald head. "Forget it, you won't come if you ask me to come! I said, you go tell everyone: sit down tomorrow, relatives and friends will come in the middle of the day, and the cars will be collected before four o'clock, and those who can't get in and out will pull the cars and squeeze in! I don't want the car tomorrow, and the cars will be collected at four o'clock. I will teach you to pull the car for a day, and I will give you a lot of thoughts in your heart. "You're going to be late tomorrow," a middle-aged driver asked. "Why do you want to collect the car at four o'clock?

"Come back after 11 o'clock if you are late! Anyway, don't crowd around when there are people in the shed! You are pulling the cart, Liu Si is not traveling with you, do you understand?"

Everyone had nothing to say, but they couldn't find a step to go out, and they stood there stiffly. Fourth Master Liu's words made everyone feel a little bit angry. Although it was a bargain to have a car for a day, whoever wanted to eat for free would not be able to pay at least forty coppers as a gift; besides, Liu Si's words were so unpleasant, as if he was celebrating his birthday and they all had to hide like mice. Liu Si could not afford to sacrifice a day's income, so everyone accompanied him to "bubble"[3]

Huniu pulled Xiangzi, and Xiangzi followed her out.

. Xiangzi didn't know this at all, so he helped the Liu family to take away the troubles in his heart. He had nothing to say to everyone at night because he had nothing to say. Everyone followed Xiangzi with their eyes and wanted to move their legs, so they all came out to chat with him. Miss Liu was talking to Xiangzi under the gas lamp, and everyone nodded to each other.

fourteen

The Liu family's affairs were very lively. Fourth Master Liu was very satisfied that so many people came to kowtow to him and celebrate his birthday. What makes me even more proud is that many old friends also rushed to congratulate me. From these old friends, he saw that his work was not only lively, but also "improved." The clothes of those old friends are out of date, but the fourth master's leather robe and mandarin jacket are all newly made. In terms of career, several of his friends were richer than him back then, but now - after the changes of the past 20 to 30 years - their status has become lower and lower, and it is difficult for some of them to have enough to eat. Looking at them, and then at his wedding tent, longevity hall, the hanging screen with the long slope, and the three sea bowl seats, he felt that he was indeed a head above them, and he had "reformed." Even when gambling, he was prepared to play mahjong tiles, which was much more elegant than betting. However, in this lively situation, he also felt a little sad and sad. Being used to living a single life, he originally expected that the people who came to celebrate his birthday would be just the shopkeepers and gentlemen in the shop, as well as the bachelors he had known from the past. Unexpectedly, some female guests also came. Although Huniu could entertain him, he suddenly felt lonely. He had no wife, only a daughter, and she looked like a man. If Huniu were a man, of course he would have already married and had children. Even if he was an old widower, he might not be so lonely. Yes, I lack nothing but a son. The longer one lives, the smaller the hope of having a son. A birthday celebration is a happy event, but it seems that one should shed tears. No matter how you reform yourself, if no one continues your career, is everything still in vain?

For the first half of the day, he liked it very much. Everyone wished him birthday, and he accepted it grandly, as if he felt that he was an old hero who had won honors in Aoli. In the second half of the day, his energy dropped a little. Looking at the children brought by the female guests, he was envious and jealous, but he did not dare to get close to the children, and felt awkward if he did not get close to them. He wanted to lose his temper, but he refused to get angry immediately. He knew that he was an outsider and could not make a fool of himself in front of his relatives and friends. He was willing to let this day pass quickly and not suffer this pain again.

There was also a slight fly in the ointment. When serving meals to the coachmen in the morning, Xiangzi almost got into a fight with someone.

Dinner started at around eight o'clock, and the coachmen were a little reluctant. Although there was a day's worth of car sharing yesterday, no one came to eat with nothing today. Whether it was a dime or forty cents, there were gifts of all sizes. On weekdays, everyone is a hard man and Liu Si is the factory owner; today, according to everyone, they are guests and should not be treated like this.

Besides, you have to leave after eating, and you’re not allowed to get out of the car. It’s the end of the year!

Xiangzi must have known that he was not among those who would leave after eating, but he was willing to eat with everyone. Firstly, he had eaten early so that he could go about his work, and secondly, he was obviously friendly. Sitting down with everyone, everyone shifted their dissatisfaction with Liu Si to him. As soon as he sat down, someone said, "Hey, you are a distinguished guest, why are you sitting with us?" Xiangzi smiled stupidly, but did not understand the meaning of the words. In the past few days, he himself had not spoken any gossip, so his mind seemed to be out of control. Everyone didn't dare to get angry with Liu Si, so we had to eat more of him; no more food could be added, but no restrictions on wine, wedding wine! They all wanted to drink wine to kill. Some were shouting, and some were guessing their punches; Old Man Liu couldn't stop them from guessing their punches. Xiangzi saw everyone drinking, but he was too unconventional, so he drank two cups. As we drank, everyone's eyes became red and their mouths were no longer under control. Some said: "Xiangzi, Camel, you are doing a great job! You can feed me for a whole day and serve the lady! You don't have to pull the cart tomorrow, so you'd better go with me!" Xiangzi heard something, but he didn't take it to heart yet; as soon as he entered Renhe Factory, he decided not to pretend to be a hero anymore, and left everything to fate. Whoever wants to say whatever he wants, say whatever he likes. He took his breath away. Some of them said, "Xiangzi is going a different way. We make money with our strength, but Xiangzi is an internal force!" Everyone laughed. Xiangzi felt that everyone was "biting" him, but he had suffered such a huge grievance, so why bother with these gossips? He hadn't said anything yet. The people at the next table saw the advantage, and some stretched their necks and shouted: "Xiangzi, you will become the owner of the factory tomorrow, don't forget your brothers!" Before Xiangzi could say anything, the people at the table said again: "Speak, Camel!"

Xiangzi's face turned red and he whispered: "How can I be a factory owner?!" "Humph, why can't you? Just look at it...[1]

La! Xiangzi didn't ask what "dong dong dong" meant, but he intuitively guessed that it was referring to his relationship with Huniu. His face slowly turned from red to white, and all the grievances he had suffered in the past suddenly came to mind, and they were all blocked in his heart. For several days, It seems that the silence can no longer be maintained, like a bottle of water that is about to rush out when it encounters an exit. At this moment, a driver pointed at his face and said: "Xiangzi, what about you, you are really a 'dumb man who eats flat food - you know what you want'." Isn’t it, say it yourself, Xiangzi? Xiangzi? "

Xiangzi stood up suddenly, his face turned pale, and asked the man: "Do you dare to go out and tell me?"

Everyone was stunned. They did intend to "bite" him and leave some idle dishes, but they were not prepared to fight.

There was a sudden silence, like a songbird in the forest suddenly seeing an eagle. Xiangzi stood there alone, much taller than the others, and he felt his isolation. But with anger in his heart, he seemed to be convinced that even if they all took action, they would not be his opponent. He added: "Does anyone dare to go out?"

Everyone suddenly thought about it and almost all said at the same time: "Come on, Xiangzi, I'm just teasing you!"

Mr. Liu saw it and said: "Sit down, Xiangzi!" Then he said to everyone, "Don't bully anyone who is honest. If you get anxious, I will kick you all out! Eat quickly!" Xiangzi left the table. Everyone teased Old Man Liu with their eyes and picked up their food. After a while, he started chirping again, like a forest bird that had passed the danger and chirped softly again.

Xiangzi squatted at the door for a long time, waiting for them. If any of them dare to gossip again, beat them up! If you have nothing, leave it alone[2]

Bar! But everyone came out in groups and didn't look for him again. Although he didn't succeed, he was somewhat angry. Then I thought about it, today's move offended many people. On weekdays, I have no close friends, so I have nowhere to complain; how can I offend others again? He regretted it a bit. The little thing I just ate was sitting sideways in my stomach, and it hurt a little. He stood up, no matter what, didn't those people who fight every day and cause famine have a very interesting life? Are honest rules necessarily beneficial? Thinking of this, he drew another path for himself. The Xiangzi on this path was completely different from what he had hoped for before. This is a person who makes friends with everyone he meets, takes advantage of others, drinks other people's tea, smokes other people's cigarettes, refuses to pay back money he borrows, refuses to hide from cars, urinates wherever he sees, plays tricks with the patrolmen all the time, and dragging him to the "area" for two or three days is nothing. Yes, such a coachman is alive and happy, at least happier than Xiangzi. Well, being honest, disciplined, and being strong are all useless, so it’s not bad to become such a scoundrel. Not only is it good, Xiangzi thought, but it also has the spirit of a heroic man. He is not afraid of the sky or the earth, and he will never lower his head and suffer the consequences of being dumb. That’s right! This is what should be done! Bad quacks are made by good people. On the contrary, I feel a little regretful that this fight could not be fought. Fortunately, I'm not busy. From now on, I won't bow to anyone anymore.

Mr. Liu did not rub sand in his eyes. Putting everything he had heard and seen before and after in one place, he already understood 80% to 90% of it. These days, the girl has been very obedient, hum, because Xiangzi is back! Looking at her eyes, she always follows him. The old man felt even more desolate and sad when he kept this in his heart. Think about it, if you don’t have a son, you can’t start a family in a hurry; if the girl leaves with someone else! My whole life has been wasted! Xiangzi is indeed good, but when it comes to being a son and a son-in-law, he is far worse than a stinky cart driver! After running around all his life, fighting in gangs, kneeling on iron ropes, and finally teaching a country boy, he moved away with his daughter and property? It's not that easy! Even if there is, don’t even think about getting it from Liu Si! Liu Si has been a farter since he was a child!

At three or four o'clock in the afternoon, some birthday greeters came, but the old man already found it boring. The more the guests praised him for his toughness and good character, the less interesting he felt.

After arriving to hold the lamp, the guests dispersed one after another, and only a dozen people who lived nearby and had close friendships were still here. Looking at the empty shed in the courtyard, which was illuminated blue by the water moon lamp, and the table with the apron removed, the old man felt empty and boring, as if it would be like this when he died, just changing the wedding shed into a white shed. There were no children or grandchildren in front of the coffin, only some unrelated people playing mahjong and keeping vigil! He really wanted to drive out the guests who had not left yet; while he was still alive, he should show off his power! However, I am too embarrassed to kill my friends. His anger turned around, and the more he looked at the girl, the more displeased he became. Xiangzi was sitting in the shed, looking like a human and a dog, and the scar on his face looked like a piece of jade when illuminated by the light. What does the old man think of this couple? How awkward they are!

Tiger girl has always been wild and dull. Today, she was dressed up head and feet, and had to pretend to entertain guests, not only to win praise from everyone, but also to show off her skills in front of Xiangzi. In the first half of the day, I thought this monster was interesting. When I arrived after noon, I was a little tired, so I felt disgusted and wanted to scold someone. By night, she didn't even have the slightest patience left, and her eyebrows were always standing straight up because of their own tension.

It's past seven o'clock, and Fourth Master Liu is a little sleepy, but he refuses to go to sleep. Everyone asked him to join in and play a few rounds of cards, but he refused to say that he was not energetic enough, but said that playing cards was not enjoyable, and that betting on money or Pai Gow suited his taste. No one wanted to change midway, so he had to sit aside. In order to cheer up, he would drink a few more cups, saying that he was not full, and complaining that the cook made too much money and the dishes were not full. From this point on, he overthrew all the satisfaction he had felt during the day: the shed, the guy's seat, the cook, and everything else were not worth the money, and they were all wronged!

Mr. Feng, who was in charge of the accounts, had already settled the accounts at this time: twenty-five birthday curtains, three bowls of birthday peach noodles, a jar of birthday wine, two pairs of birthday candles, and a gift of about twenty yuan. There are many numbers, but most of them are forty coppers or ten cents.

After hearing this report, Fourth Master Liu became even more angry. If I had known this, I should have prepared "stir-fried noodles"! A ten cent favor for eating three bowls of sea food? This is simply taking the old man for granted! I will never do anything again, and I can’t pay for this useless money! Needless to say, everyone, including relatives and friends, wanted to eat him for nothing; the sixty-nine-year-old man was actually smarter than ever, and in a moment of confusion, he taught a group of monkeys to eat the bastard! The old man became more and more angry as he thought about it, and even the satisfaction he felt during the day was regarded as his own confusion. Thinking this in his heart, he muttered many curses that are no longer popular on the street.

Before all the friends left, Huniu wanted to stop her father from acting wildly to save everyone's face. However, seeing that everyone was paying attention to the cards in their hands, they didn't seem to pay attention to what the old man was nagging. It was not convenient for her to speak, so as not to clarify the matter. Let him nag, let him get over it.

Who knows, the old man walked around her as he spoke. She decided not to do this! He was celebrating his birthday, and she was busy with it for several days, but it didn't turn out well. She couldn't let it go! Sixty-nine or seventy-nine is not enough, you still have to be reasonable! She immediately returned it:

"You have to spend your own money to do things, why are you bothering me?"

The old man encountered a counterattack and his spirits suddenly lifted. "What's bothering you? I'm just following you! Why do you think my eyes are doing nothing?"

"What did you see? I've been tired all day, and you're going to kill me at the end. Wait a minute! Tell me, what did you see?" The tiger girl's fatigue was relieved, and her mouth was very flexible.

"Don't watch me do things. Are you hot-eyed? Did you see it? I've seen it all, huh!"

"Why am I so hot-eyed?!" she said, shaking her head. "What exactly did you see?"

"Isn't that?!" Liu Si pointed towards the shed - Xiangzi was bending over to sweep the floor. "He?" Huniu trembled in her heart. She didn't expect the old man's eyes to be so sharp. "Hmph! How is he?"

"Don't assume that you understand, just say that you are confused!" The old man stood up. "If he wants to live without me, if he wants me to live without him, just tell you. I am your father! I should take care of it!"

Huniu didn't expect that things would break down so quickly. She had only accomplished less than half of her plan, but the old man had already figured it out! What to do? Her face turned red, dark red, half-remaining pink, and bright green light, like a piece of over-cooked pork liver, the color was complex and ugly. She was a little tired; she was aroused by this and became angry again. She couldn't think of an idea and was very confused. She couldn't just go back and feel confused, so she had to find a solution immediately. An ill-conceived idea is better than no idea at all. She never gives in in front of anyone! Well, let's do it simply, good or bad depends on this hammer! "It's okay that everything has been explained clearly today, let's just leave it like this. What do you think? I want to hear it! This is your own illness, don't say I have any intention of being angry with you!"

The card players seemed to hear the father and daughter quarreling, but they couldn't bear to look at anything else. In order to resist their voices, everyone smashed the cards louder and shouted red, touch... Xiangzi understood what was going on and kept his head down to sweep the floor.

"Are you just so angry with me?" The old man's eyes were widened. "You're making me so angry that you're going to cheat on me? Don't make any plans, I still have to live for a few more years!" "Don't make idle offers, what are you going to do?" Huniu's heart was pounding, but her mouth was very hard. "What should I do? Didn't I tell you before, there is him without me, there is me without him! I can't just be a cheap rickshaw puller!"

Xiangzi threw away the broom, straightened up, looked at Liu Si, and asked: "Who are you talking about?" Liu Si laughed wildly: "Haha, you kid, are you going to rebel? Who are you talking about? Get out of here right now! I look good on you, and I'll give you a face. If you dare to disturb Tai Sui, you won't even ask me what I do! Get out! Don't ever teach me to see you here, you're here to get some advantage, ah?"

The old man's voice was too loud, so he summoned several coachmen to watch the fun. The people playing cards thought that Mr. Liu was quarreling with the coachman again, so they still refused to look up.

Xiangzi didn't have a convenient mouth. He wanted to say a lot, but not a word came to his tongue. He stood there blankly, swallowing with his neck straightened. "Get out of here! Get out of here! Are you here looking for an advantage? I didn't have you when I took out the bad things, huh!" The old man was a little scared just to scare Xiangzi. He didn't hate Xiangzi as much as he hated his daughter. Even when he was angry, he still felt that Xiangzi was indeed an honest man.

"Okay, I'm leaving!" Xiangzi had nothing to say, so he had to leave here quickly; no matter what, he couldn't fight with them in their bickering.

The coachmen were watching the excitement and saw Mr. Liu scolding Xiangzi. They still remembered the scene in the morning and felt very happy. When they heard that the old man was chasing Xiangzi out, they turned to him again - Xiangzi had suffered so much and had to cross the river and demolish the bridge. Someone rushed over and asked, "What's the matter, Xiangzi?" Xiangzi shook his head.

"Xiangzi, please wait and leave!" Huniu felt a flash in her heart and saw clearly: her plan is of little use. It's better to hurry than to catch Xiangzi quickly. Otherwise, the chickens will fly and the eggs will be beaten! "It's our business, two grasshoppers are tied with a rope, neither of us can run away! Just wait, wait until I explain it clearly!" She turned to the old man and said, "Just say it, I already have it, Xiangzi's! I will go wherever he goes! Are you going to give me to him? Or are you going to kick us both out together? What do you want me to say?"

Huniu didn't expect that things would happen so quickly and that she would take out her last trick so early. Fourth Master Liu never expected that things would reach this stage. However, now that things have come to this, he cannot give in, especially in front of everyone. "You really have the nerve to speak out. Even my old face is burning for you!" He slapped himself in the mouth. "Bah! So shameless!"

The people playing cards stopped, feeling that something was wrong, but they were confused, didn't know what was going on, and couldn't talk to each other; some stood up, and some looked at their cards blankly.

After saying the words, Hu Niu was happy: "I am shameless? Don't teach me to talk about you to others. What shit have you never done? This is the first time for me, and it's all your fault: the male should marry, the female should marry, you are sixty-nine, and your life is in vain! This is not in front of the public," she pointed around, "It's best that we have figured it out, we are clear-sighted! You can do something else for this wedding shed!"

"Me?" Mr. Liu's face turned from red to white, and he showed off all his bachelor energy: "I set a fire to burn the shed, but I can't use it for you!" "Okay!" Huniu's lips trembled, and her voice was very unpleasant, "I pack up my bed and leave, how much will you give me?"

"The money is mine, and I give it to whomever I want!" The old man felt a little sad when he heard that his daughter was leaving, but he was cruel to fight for this tone.

"Your money? I've been helping you for these years; without me, think about it, it would be weird if all your money was given to wild bitches. Let's follow our conscience!" Her eyes found Xiangzi again, "You say it!"

Xiangzi stood there upright, without a word to say.
