No matter who you are, no matter who you are.

fifteen

When it comes to using force, Xiangzi cannot hit an old man, nor can he hit a girl. His power has no use. To act rogue, you can only think about it, but you cannot act it out. As for Huniu, she could just stamp her feet and run away. Because of the current situation, since she had a falling out with her father and was willing to go with him, no one knew what was going on in her heart. On the surface, she died for Xiangzi. In front of everyone, he couldn't help but show some heroic spirit. He had nothing to say, so he could only stand there and wait for the truth to come to light; at least he had to do this to act like a man.

The Liu family's father and daughter could only stare at each other, having nothing to say; Xiangzi was speechless. The coachmen, no matter who they were talking to, seemed to find it difficult to interrupt. The people playing cards could not stop talking, and the silence was already embarrassing. However, everyone could only say a few perfunctory words to persuade both parties not to be too angry and to say slowly that the matter would not be difficult to deal with. They can only say this, they cannot solve anything, and they don’t want to solve anything. Seeing that neither side is willing to give in, then it is difficult for an upright official to stop family affairs, so he should run away if he has the chance.

Before everyone could escape, Miss Tiger caught Mr. Feng from the Tianshun Coal Plant: "Mr. Feng, don't you have room in your shop? Let Xiangzi stay for two days first. Our things will be done quickly, and we can't occupy your place for a long time. Xiangzi, go with Mr. Feng and see you tomorrow to discuss our affairs. Let me tell you, when I go back to the door, I still have to sit in a sedan chair to leave this door! Mr. Feng, I will leave him to you, and I will ask you for someone tomorrow!"

Mr. Feng took a deep breath, unwilling to take this responsibility. Xiangzi was eager to leave here and said: "I can't run away!"

The tiger girl glared at the old man, returned to her room, cried at the top of her voice, and locked the door from the inside.

The Feng gentlemen persuaded Fourth Master Liu to come in, and the old man showed off his outfield energy again and asked everyone not to leave. He still had to drink a few cups: "Don't worry, everyone, from now on she is her and I am me, and there will be no more quarrels. If you leave her, just pretend that I have never had such a girl. I have spent my whole life in the outfield, and I have lost my face to her! Go back twenty years, I Kill them both alive! Now let her go. Don't give her a penny! Just give her a try and you'll know who is better, dad or a wild man!" Everyone said a few words in a hurry to avoid right and wrong.

Xiangzi went to Tianshun Coal Factory.

Things went really quickly. Huniu rented two small north rooms in a large courtyard in Maojiawan. She immediately searched for them, and the paper poster was so confused that everything fell to the ground. She asked Mr. Feng to write some happy words and paste them in the house. After the house was decorated, she went to ask for a sedan: a sedan with stars in the sky, sixteen rattles, no golden lamps, no butlers. After everything was agreed upon, she dressed herself in a red silk sedan gown; she had done it before the year, so she didn't have to go through any needlework. The happy day is set on the sixth day of the Lunar New Year, which is a good day and does not require taboos. She did it all by herself and told Xiangzi to buy new ones from head to toe: "This will happen once in a lifetime!"

Xiangzi only has five yuan in his hand!

Huniu looked at it again: "What? Where is the more than thirty yuan I gave you?"

Xiangzi couldn't help but tell the truth and told her everything about Cao Zhai. She blinked and said suspiciously: "Okay, I don't have time to argue with you, let's just follow our conscience! I'll give you these fifteen dollars! If you don't dress like a new person by the end of the day, be careful!"

On the sixth day of the lunar month, Huniu got on the sedan chair. I didn’t say a word to my father, there was no escort from brothers, no congratulations from relatives and friends; only those gongs and drums sounded lively on the streets after the New Year, and the sedan chair steadily passed through the Xi’an Gate and the Xisi Archway, which also aroused some envy and some feelings among the people wearing new clothes-especially the shop assistants.

Xiangzi was wearing new clothes bought from Tianqiao, with a red face and a small satin hat worth three cents. He seemed to have forgotten himself, and looked at everything and listened to everything stupidly. He seemed to not even recognize himself. He moved from a coal shop to a new house decorated in snow-white, and he didn't know what was going on: before, things were just like in a coal factory, with piles of black; now he walked into the new house in a daze, and it was so white that it dazzled the eyes, with a few blood-red happy words stuck on it. He felt a kind of ridicule, a kind of white, vague and suffocating feeling. In the room, there are Huniu's original tables, chairs and bed; the stove and dish table are new; and there is a duster with colorful chicken feathers stuck in the corner of the room. He knew the tables and chairs, but he felt unfamiliar with the stove, dish table, and feather duster. The combination of old and new objects reminds him of the past and worries about the future. Everything is at the mercy of others, and he himself looks like something old and something new at the same time, a decoration, something strange; he doesn't recognize himself. He couldn't think of crying, he couldn't think of laughing. His big hands and feet were moving around in this small and warm room, like a big rabbit in a small wooden cage. His eyes were red, looking outside and inside. He only had legs that could fly, but he couldn't run out! Huniu was wearing a red coat, her face was smeared with white powder and rouge, her eyes were glancing at him. He didn't dare to look at her. She is also a strange thing that is both old and new at the same time, a girl and a woman, a woman and a man, a human and some ferocious beast! This beast, wearing a red coat, has caught him and is preparing to take care of him carefully. Anyone can deal with him. This beast is particularly powerful. It wants to guard him all the time, glare at him, laugh at him, and hug him tightly, sucking out all his strength. He couldn't escape. He took off the little satin hat and stared blankly at the red knot on the hat until he was dazzled - when he turned his face, the wall was covered with red dots, whirling and beating, and in the middle there was a bigger, red, tiger girl with an ugly smile on her face! On the eve of the wedding, Xiangzi realized that Huniu was not pregnant. Like a magician, she explained to him: "If I hadn't wronged you so much, how could you have nodded so firmly! I stuffed a pillow in the waistband of my trousers! Haha, haha!" She laughed until she shed tears: "You silly thing! Forget it, I deserve you anyway; who are you and who am I? I had a quarrel with my father and followed you, why don't you thank God?" The next day, Xiangzi went out very early. Most of the shops have opened for business, but some are still closed. The Spring Festival couplets on the door are still red, but the yellow hanging money has been blown to pieces by the wind. The street is very calm, there are many foreign cars, and the rickshaw drivers seem to be more energetic than usual. Almost all of them are wearing new pairs of shoes, and there is a piece of red paper stuck on the back of the car. Xiangzi envied these rickshaw drivers and felt that they seemed to be celebrating the New Year, while he had been suffocating in a gourd for several days; they were all keeping to themselves, but he had no livelihood and was wandering on the street. He was not content with idleness, but if he planned to think about tomorrow, he would have to discuss it with Huniu, his wife; he was thinking about his wife, such a wife! ——Begging for food in hand. It's useless to grow so tall and have so much strength. He had to serve his wife first, that thing with a red coat and tiger fangs; a thing that sucked human essence and blood; he was no longer a human being, but just a piece of meat. He lost himself and was only struggling in her teeth, like a mouse caught in a cat's mouth. He didn't want to discuss with her, he had to leave; he had made up his mind to leave her without saying goodbye. There's nothing shameful about this, she's a monster who can do tricks with him with pillows! He was so worried that he not only wanted to tear the new clothes into pieces, but also wanted to wash himself in clean water from the inside out. He felt that there was something unclean and disgusting stuck to his whole body, which made him feel disgusted in his heart. He wants to never see her face again!

Where to go? He has no destination. On weekdays, when pulling a cart, his legs followed other people's words. Today, his legs were free and he was at a loss. Following the Xisi Archway to the south, he exited the Xuanwu Gate: the road was so straight, and his heart would never turn. After leaving the city gate and heading south, he saw a bathhouse. He decided to take a shower.

He took off his clothes and looked at his body, feeling very ashamed. When he went down into the pool, the hot water made his whole body numb. He closed his eyes, and his body was numb and numb, as if some accumulated filth was radiating out. He almost didn't dare to touch himself, his heart was empty and beads of sweat were streaming down his head. It wasn't until his breathing became short that he lazily climbed up, his body flushed like a newborn baby. He didn't seem to dare to just walk out and put on a big towel. He still felt ugly. Although beads of sweat fell down, he still felt that he was unclean - the filth in his heart seemed to never be washed away: in the eyes of Fourth Master Liu and in the eyes of everyone who knew him, he would always be a woman-stealer!

Before the sweat had completely fallen, he hurriedly put on his clothes and ran out. He was afraid that everyone would see his nakedness! After leaving the bathhouse, he felt relaxed in the cool breeze. The street was much livelier than before. The clear sky brings some brilliance to everyone's face. Xiangzi's heart was still worried, and he didn't know where to go. Going south, east, and south again, he ran to the overpass. After the New Year, at around nine o'clock, the shopkeeper's apprentices had already finished their breakfast and arrived here. Various stalls and performing arts venues were set up early and occupied. When Xiangzi arrived, a circle of people had gathered around the place, and gongs and drums were playing inside. He wasn't in the mood to see anything, and he couldn't laugh anymore.

On weekdays, the cross talkers, bear performers, jugglers, treasure counting performers, yangko singers, drum calligraphers, and acrobatics practitioners here can all provide him with some real happiness and make him open his mouth to smile. He couldn't bear to leave Peiping, and Tianqiao was half the reason. Every time he looked at the mat tents on the overpass and the people in the circle, he remembered many funny and lovely things. Now he was too lazy to push forward, and he was no longer part of the laughter on the bridge. He avoided the crowd and walked to a quiet place, feeling reluctant to leave! No, he couldn't leave this lively and lovely place, couldn't leave the overpass, couldn't leave Peiping. Walk? There is no way to go! He still had to go back to her—to her! ——Go to discuss. He couldn't leave, nor could he be idle. He had to take a step back and think, just like everyone had to take a step back and think when they were helpless. You have suffered all kinds of grievances, why should you be serious about this point alone? He couldn't correct everything in the past, so he had to go down the road.

He stood still, listening to the chaotic sounds of people and the sound of gongs and drums; looking at the people, cars and horses coming and going, he suddenly thought of the two huts. The sound in my ears seemed to have disappeared, and the people in front of me seemed to have disappeared. Only the two white, warm huts with red happy characters on them stood squarely in front of me. Although they only stayed for one night, they were very familiar and intimate. Even the woman in the red coat seemed not to be given up casually. Standing on the overpass, he had nothing and was nothing; in those two huts, he had everything. Go back, only by going back can you find a solution. Everything for tomorrow is in that cabin. It's useless to be ashamed, afraid, or sad; if you want to live, you have to find a place where you can find a way.

He walked back angrily and entered the house, probably just after eleven o'clock. Huniu had already prepared lunch: boiled steamed buns, boiled cabbage and meatballs, a plate of tiger skin jelly, and a plate of pickled radish. Everything else has been laid out, only the cabbage is still simmering on the fire, emitting some wonderful fragrance. She had taken off her red coat and put on her usual cotton trousers and jacket. On her head was a small red flower made of velvet, with a small gold paper ingot on the flower. Xiangzi glanced at her, she didn't look like a bride. Every move she makes is like a daughter-in-law who has been with her for many years, deft, sophisticated, and a little self-satisfied. Although she didn't look like a bride, she still made him feel something new. She cooked the food and tidied the house. The fragrance and heating in the house were all things he had never experienced before. No matter what she did, he felt like he had a family. A home always has its lovely features. He didn't know how to get better.

"Where are you? You!" she asked as she went to serve the cabbage. "Go take a shower." He took off his robe.

"Ah! When you go out next time, say something! Don't wave your hands and walk away so carelessly!" He was speechless.

"Can you hum? No, I'll teach you!"

He snorted, there was nothing he could do! He knew that he would marry a yaksha, but this yaksha could cook, clean the house, scold him and help him, but it would be useless to teach him anything! He ate the steamed buns. The food was indeed more delicious and hotter than usual, but it didn't taste good and he couldn't feel the pleasure of devouring it in his mouth as he chewed it. He couldn't sweat after eating.

After eating, he lay on the kang, resting his head on his palms and looking at the roof of the shed. "Hey! Help me brush this guy off! I'm not someone's slave girl!" she shouted from the outer room.

Lazy, he stood up, glanced at her, and walked over to help. He is usually very diligent, but now he is holding his breath to do things. When he was at the car factory, he often helped her. Now the more he looks at her, the more he hates her. He has never hated anyone as much as he hates her. He can't say why. I'm angry, but I don't want to let it out. I keep it in my heart. Since I can't break up with her, there's no point in arguing with her. Wandering around the hut, he felt that his whole life was a grievance.

After packing her things, she glanced around and sighed. A smile followed. "how?"

"What?" Xiangzi squatted by the stove and warmed his hands. His hands were not cold, so he had no choice but to warm them because there was no place to put them. The two cabins did feel like home, but he didn't know where to put his feet.

"Take me out to have fun? Go to Baiyun Temple? No, it's late; go for a walk on the street?" She wanted to fully enjoy the happiness of her wedding. Although getting married is not a good idea, it is good to be so carefree. It is just a good time to spend more time with my husband and have fun for a few days. In her parents' home, she has no shortage of food, clothing, and change; she just doesn't have a close man. Now, she wants to make up for this shortcoming, and she wants to show off in the streets, at temple fairs, and play with Xiangzi.

Xiangzi refused to go. First, he felt that it was shameful to take his wife around the world. Second, he thought that a wife who came like this could only be hidden at home. This was not a decent thing, and the less she showed off in front of everyone, the better. Also, when you go out, you can't help but meet acquaintances. All the rickshaw pullers in the western half of the city don't know Huniu and Xiangzi. He can't invite everyone to whisper behind his back.

"Can we discuss it?" He was still squatting there.

"Is there anything to discuss?" She came over and stood next to the stove. He took his hand off, put it on his lap, and stared blankly at the flames. After being stunned for a long time, he said: "I can't be so idle!"

"Suffering!" She laughed. "If you don't pull a cart for a day, your body will feel itchy, right? Look at the old man, he has been playing for a lifetime and still drives a car factory when he is old. He doesn't pull a cart, he doesn't work hard, and he makes his living based on his heart. You have to learn a little bit, who is old after pulling a cart for a lifetime? Let's play for a few days first, and we won't just be busy in these few days, why are we running for our lives? I don't plan to quarrel with you these two days, and you don't want to be angry with me!"

"Let's discuss it first!" Xiangzi decided not to give in. Since you can't stomp away, you have to find a way to do something. You have to stand at one end first, and you can't swing back and forth like a swing.

"Okay, tell me!" She moved a stool and sat by the stove. "How much money do you have?" he asked.

"Really? I knew you were going to ask this! You're not marrying a wife, you're marrying for the money, right?"

Xiangzi seemed to be choked by a breath of wind and swallowed several times. Old man Liu and the driver of Renren Factory all thought that he was greedy for money, so he hooked up with Huniu; now, she said so herself! My own car and my own money were thrown away for no reason, and now I am weighed down by a few dollars from my wife; I have to go down my spine to eat! He wished he could wrap his hands around her neck and choke her! pinch! pinch! Until she rolled her eyes! He strangled everything to death and then wiped his own neck. They are not human and must die; he himself is not human and must die; no one needs to think about living!

Xiangzi stood up and wanted to go out for a walk again; he shouldn't have come back just now. Seeing that Xiangzi's expression was wrong, she softened a little: "Okay, let me tell you. I have a total of about five hundred yuan in my hand. Even the sedan chair and rented house - three shares[1]

, paste the shed, make clothes, buy things, and bring them to you. I spent a small amount of one hundred and returned them to the bag, leaving about four hundred yuan. I'm telling you, you don't have to worry. Let's give it a happy life. You, as an adult, sweating while pulling a cart, deserve a few days of fun; as for me, as an old girl, I deserve a few days of fun. When we almost run out of money, we'll beg the old man to go. As for me, if I hadn't fallen out with him that day, I would never have left. Now my anger is gone, dad is dad after all. As for him, he only has a daughter like me, and you are the person he likes. If we give in and make amends to him, there will probably be nothing difficult for him to do. How ready is this! If he has money, let's accept it in a decent manner. There is nothing unreasonable about it; it's better than you working as livestock for others! In two days, you go there first; he may not see you. If you don't see him once, you will go a second time; if you give him face, he will have to change his mind. Then I'll go and give him a few nice words, maybe we can all move back. As soon as we moved back, the caretaker puffed up his chest and no one dared to look at us sideways; if we endured here forever, we would always be a pair of black people, don't you think? "

Xiangzi never thought of this. Ever since Huniu came to Cao's house to look for him, he thought he had married her, used her money to buy a car, and drove it himself. Although it was not respectable to use his wife's money, his relationship with her was a kind of unspoken relationship, so there was nothing he could do about it. He didn't expect that Hu Niu had such a trick. Pulling his long face down, of course this is indeed an idea, but Xiangzi is not that kind of person. After thinking about it, he seemed to understand something: if he had money, he could teach others to take it away for free, and he had nowhere to complain if he had grievances. When someone gives you money, you have to accept it; after accepting it, you can no longer treat yourself as a person. You have no ambition and no strength, so you have to be someone else's slave: a plaything of your own wife and a slave of your father-in-law. A person seems to be nothing at all, just a bird. If he goes to hunt for food, he will fall into the net. If you eat other people's grain and rice, you have to stay in a cage and sing for others, and you can be sold at any time!

He refused to go to Fourth Master Liu. The relationship with Hu Niu is that the flesh is in the flesh; with Liu Si, it has nothing to do with it. She has already suffered a lot from her, so she can no longer sue her father! "I don't want to be idle!" He only said this to save himself from talking and arguing.

"Are you going to suffer from this?" she said, tapping and teasing. "If you don't like to be idle, go and do some business."

"I can't! I can't make any money! I can pull a cart, and I love pulling a cart!" The tendons in Xiangzi's head jumped.

"Let me tell you, you are not allowed to pull the cart! I won't allow you to get on my bed with a stinking sweat all over your body! You have your own ideas, and I have mine. Let's see, who can be more awkward than the other! You married a wife, but you didn't pay a penny for the money I spent. Think about it, who of us should listen to whom?"

Xiangzi was speechless again.

[1]

Three shares, three months' rent is paid in the first month of renting a house.

sixteen

When the Lantern Festival arrived, Xiangzi couldn't bear it any longer.

Hu Niu was very happy. She was busy cooking Yuanxiao and making dumplings, visiting temples during the day and lantern viewing at night. She didn't allow Xiangzi to have any ideas, but she always wanted to eat him, so she tried her best to buy him something fresh to eat. There are seven or eight families in the courtyard, most of whom live in one room; some families of all ages live in one room. Some of these people pull carts, some do small business, some work as patrolmen, and some work as servants. Everyone has their own business, and no one has time. Even the children carry small baskets and go to make porridge in the morning and collect coal cores in the afternoon. Only the little ones played or fought in the yard with their buttocks turned red from the cold. The dirt, dirt, and water from the furnace were all dumped in the courtyard, and no one bothered to clean it. The middle of the courtyard was filled with ice. The older children came back from collecting coal cores and used it as an ice rink, clamoring for ice skating. Those who suffer the most are the elderly and women. The old people have no food and clothing, lying on the cold kang, waiting for the young ones to earn some money so they can have a bowl of porridge. The young ones who work hard may earn money, but they may come back empty-handed, and they will lose their temper and quarrel everywhere. The old people had to swallow their tears as water when they had an empty stomach. Those women have to take care of both the old and the young, and they also have to deal with the young men who make money. Even when they were pregnant, they had to go about their business as usual, eating only steamed buns and sweet potato porridge; no, not only did they have to work as usual, but they also had to make porridge and do some work. Fortunately, both young and old were full and lay down. They had to hold a small kerosene lamp for others to wash, work, and mend. The house was so small and the walls were so broken. The cold wind got in through the cracks in the wall on one side and kept going out on the other side, taking away all the warmth. They only have some rags hanging on their bodies, and their stomachs are filled with a bowl or half a bowl of porridge, or they may have a six- or seven-month-old fetus. They have to work, and they have to feed the young and old first. They are all sick, and have lost their hair before they are thirty, but they cannot be idle for a moment, from illness to death; after death, the coffin must be collected from "good people". Those girls, sixteen or seventeen years old, had no pants and could only huddle around a piece of junk in the house - a natural prison - to help their mother with work and catch up on work. To go to the hut, they had to make sure there was no one in the courtyard before they dared to run out like thieves; all winter, they had not seen the sun or the blue sky. The ugly ones will inherit everything from their mothers in the future; the decent-looking ones, even they know, will be sold by their parents sooner or later to "enjoy their happiness"!

Just in such a courtyard, Huniu felt very proud. She was the only one who had food and clothing, didn't have to worry, and could walk around. She went in and out with her face held high. Not only did she feel her superiority, but she was also afraid that others would mess with her, so she ignored the miserable people. Most of the people who come here to do small business sell cheap things, such as bone scrapings, frozen cabbage, raw bean juice, and donkey and horse meat. They all come here to find their caregivers. Since Huniu moved in, those selling sheep head meat, smoked fish, hard noodle pastry, braised and fried tofu have also been shouting in front of the door. She held the bowl and raised her face as she carried these snacks into the room. The children looked at her with their iron-like fingers in their mouths, as if she were some kind of princess. She is here to enjoy, she cannot, will not, and does not want to see the suffering of others.

Xiangzi first disliked her behavior. He was born in a poor family and knew what poverty meant. He didn't want to eat those odds and ends, but it was a pity for the money. Secondly, what made him even more embarrassed was that he figured out something: she didn't allow him to pull the cart, but raising him with good food and food every day was like fattening a cow so that it could be milked! He completely became her thing. He has seen: a thin old female dog on the street, and when running errands, she also chooses a fat male dog. Thinking of this, he not only hated this kind of life, but also worried about himself. He knows how a hard-working man should protect his body. The body is everything. If he continues to live like this, he will one day become a skeleton with a dry skeleton, still the same size, and his chest will be completely empty. He shivered. If he was going to die, he would have to pull the cart right away, go out for a run, run for a day, and fall asleep when he came back, not knowing what was going on. If he didn't eat her good food, he wouldn't serve her and play with her. He decided to do this and couldn't give in anymore; if she was willing to pay for the car, fine; if she didn't want to, he would rent a car. Without saying a word, he made up his mind and went to rent a car. On the seventeenth day, he started to pull a car and rented it for "a full day". After two long transactions, he felt something wrong that he had never experienced before. His calves were tight and his hip bones were sore. He knew where the source of his illness was, but to comfort himself, he thought that it might be because he hadn't pulled a cart for more than 20 days and his legs were numb. After running a few times and stretching his legs, he might be fine.

Another deal was made, this time for a baby carriage, four of them went together. After picking up the handlebars, everyone let a tall man in his forties walk in front. The tall man smiled and admitted the truth. He knew that the three cars were "better" than him. He worked hard. Although he knew he couldn't outrun the three young men behind him, he refused to rely on his old age. After running for more than a mile, he was praised from behind: "What's the matter, do you need more energy? You really can't leave!" He gasped and replied: "I'm following your buddies in the car, is it okay if I'm slow?!" He was indeed not running slowly, and even Xiangzi had to exert 70% to 80% of his energy to keep up with him. His running style is not good-looking: He is tall and cannot bend down, and his waist and back seem to be a solid board, so his whole body has to be thrown forward; when his body is forward, his hands are obviously far behind; it is not like running, but like pulling something forward. His waist was rigid, so his hips had to move; his feet were almost stretched on the ground, twisting forward harder. The twisting was really slow, but you could tell by looking at it that he was exerting great effort. When he reached a roundabout place, he adjusted his body and turned hard, and everyone was sweating for him; he always seemed to just drive forward, regardless of whether the car could pass or not.

When he did, his sweat dripped down from the tip of his nose, ears and lips. After putting down the car, he straightened up quickly and grinned. When I took the money, my hands were shaking so much that I couldn't hold anything.

They are considered friends after walking together in a car, so the four of them put the car together. The Xiangzis wiped their sweat and continued to joke. The tall man wandered alone for a long time, coughed a lot, and spit out a lot of white foam. Then he seemed to have calmed down a little and began to talk to them: "It's over! I still have that heart. My waist and legs are not strong at all! No matter how I lift my waist, I can't lift my legs. I'm so worried!"

"You took those two steps just now, you must be too slow!" A short young man in his twenties took over: "Unyielding Heart, the three of us are great enough, who didn't sweat?" The tall man sighed a little proudly, but also as if he was ashamed.

"Let's just say that your running method is so close that I really need to teach you how to do it[1]

Yes, do you believe it? "Another young man said. "I'm old, I'm not joking. The tall man smiled and shook his head: "It doesn't matter how old you are, buddy!" Let me tell you the truth, those who work in our industry should not get married, seriously! Seeing that everyone was passing their ears, he lowered his voice a little: "Once we have a family, we will not be idle day and night. Let's finish playing!" Look at my waist, it’s straightened out, there’s no softness at all! It’s better not to run too fast, you’ll cough as soon as you grit your teeth, and your heart will feel spicy with wormwood! Don't talk about it, anyone who works in our profession will be a bachelor for the rest of his life! Even the damn little birds are in pairs, we are not allowed to have a family! Another thing I said is that after getting married, I will have one child a year, and now I have five! All with their mouths open waiting to eat! The fare is big, the food is expensive, and the business is hard, what can we do! It's better to be a bachelor for the rest of your life, live in a white house when you are wrong, get big bayberry sores, and accept your fate! When a person dies, he dies! Once a family of this thing is formed, even the older ones and the younger ones, several of them, will not be able to close their eyes even if they die! Do you think so? " he asked Xiangzi.

Xiangzi nodded but said nothing.

This time, a seat came. The short man negotiated the price first, but he gave in and called the tall man: "Big brother, go ahead! There are five children in this thing!"

The tall man smiled: "Okay, I'll run again! Ordinarily we can't do this! Come on, I'll bring back a lot of cakes! See you later, buddies!" Seeing the tall man walking away, the short man said to himself: "I can't even touch a daughter-in-law for the rest of my fucking life! There are four or five girls in his arms at the door of his fucking house!"

"Don't mention it for now," another young man took over. "You see, those who do this business should be careful. The tall man is right. Just put it this way, why do you want to start a family? Can you show it as a toy? No! Okay, this is the building! All day long, you are eating wormwood, and you are struggling. Even a good young man has to climb down!" Hearing this, Xiangzi pulled up the car and accosted him: "Go south, there is no business here."

"See you later!" the two young ones said together.

Xiangzi didn't seem to hear. I was kicking my legs while walking, and my hip shaft was indeed a little sore! I wanted to stop driving the car, but I simply didn’t have the courage to go home. The one at home is not a wife, but a blood-sucking goblin!

The sky was slowly getting longer, and he wandered around two or three more times, and it was just five o'clock. He handed over the car and spent some time in the teahouse. After drinking two pots of tea, he felt hungry and decided to eat outside and go home. After eating twelve taels of meat pie and a bowl of red bean and millet porridge, I slowly walked home while burping loudly. He must have known that there was a thunder waiting for him at home, but he was very calm; he made up his mind: he would not quarrel with her, he would not make trouble with her, he would fall asleep, and he would still come out to pull the cart tomorrow, whatever she liked!

As soon as he entered the house, Huniu was sitting in the outer room. She glanced at him, her face so heavy that she was about to drip. Xiangzi planned to join forces with Xini, so he pulled his long face and greeted her. But he was not used to doing this kind of thing, so he lowered his head and walked into the back room. She said nothing, and the hut was as quiet as an ancient cave in the mountains. The coughs, conversations, and children's cries of the neighbors in the courtyard were all very real and seemed to be far away, just like hearing distant sounds on the mountain.

Neither of them wanted to speak first. They lay down one after another with their mouths closed, like a pair of giant turtles that never made a sound. After waking up, Huniu spoke, her voice half annoyed and half smiling: "What have you been doing? You've been gone all day!"

"Going to pull the cart!" He said as if he was asleep, as if there was something stuck in his throat. "Oh! Stop sweating and feel itchy, you bitch! If you don't come back to eat the food I cooked for you, why don't you go around the world to make yourself comfortable? Don't offend me. My father is a bachelor, and I can do anything! If you dare to go out again tomorrow, I will hang myself for you. If I can tell you, I can do it!" "I can't be idle!"

"You won't go to the old man?"

"Don't go!"

"How arrogant!"

Xiangzi was really angry. He couldn't help but say what was in his heart. He couldn't bear it anymore: "Pull the car, buy your own car, and if anyone stops me, I will leave and never come back!" "Well--" This sound was spinning in her nose, very long and tortuous. In this voice, she expressed pride and contempt for Xiangzi, but there was also a detour in her heart. She knew that Xiangzi was a tough guy, albeit an honest one. The words of a tough guy are never spoken in jest. It was so easy to catch him, so we can't let him go casually. He is an ideal person: honest, thrifty, and strong; given her appearance and age, it is really difficult to get such a treasure again. Being able to be both strong and soft is the key, and she had to irritate him: "I also know that you want to be strong, but you also have to know that I really love you. If you don't want to go to the old man, this is what I do: I will go to him. She is his daughter anyway, so it doesn't matter if she is embarrassed."

"If the old man wants us, I have to pull the cart!" Xiangzi said it home.

Huniu was speechless for a long time. She didn't expect Xiangzi to be so smart. Although his words were so simple, it was clear that he would no longer fall for her trap, and he was not a stupid ass. Therefore, she felt that it was more and more interesting. She had to put in some effort to win over this grown-up or big thing who would get upset when he got anxious. She couldn't push too hard, it wasn't easy to find such a big thing. She had to loosen and tighten him so that he could never escape from her grasp. "Well, you love to pull a cart, but I can't. You have to swear not to pull a chartered cart, and you have to come back every day; you see, if I don't see you for a day, I will panic! Promise me, you will come back early every night!"

Xiangzi remembered what the tall man said during the day! I stared into the darkness with my eyes open, and saw a group of people pulling carts, doing small business, and working hard. Their backs couldn't collapse and they were pulling their legs. He will be like that in the future. But it was not easy for him to quarrel with her anymore. As long as he could pull the cart, he had already won. "I always break up the meeting!" He agreed.

Although she said that, she was not very enthusiastic about going to Fourth Master Liu. Father and daughter naturally quarrel often on weekdays, but the situation is different now. They can't just talk about it for a while, because she is no longer a member of the Liu family. A married woman is always somewhat estranged from her parents. She didn't dare go back to the court. What if the old man really turns his back on him and refuses to recognize him? She will take matters into her own hands. If he refuses to let go of his property, there is nothing she can do. Even if there were people mediating on the side, when there was no other choice, they could only persuade her to come back. She had her own home.

Xiangzi went to pull the cart as usual, and she walked around the house alone. She wanted to get dressed and go to her father several times, but she thought about it but was too lazy to move her hands. She was in trouble. For your own comfort and happiness, you have to go back; for your own dignity, it is right not to go. If the old man calmed down, she would just drag Xiangzi to Renhe Factory, and she would naturally teach him to have something to do without having to pull a cart, and she would be able to securely take over his father's business. Her heart lit up. What if the old man is stubborn to the end? She was disgraced, no, not only was she disgraced, but she also had to become a coachman's wife; she, hum! There is no difference from the group of women in the courtyard. Her heart suddenly became dark. She almost regretted marrying Xiangzi. No matter how strong he was, if her father didn't nod, he would be a cart puller all his life. Thinking of this, she even wanted to go back to her parents' home alone and break up with Xiangzi. She couldn't lose everything for him. Then I thought about it, and the joy of following Xiangzi was beyond words. She sat on the bed, dazed and vague, thinking about the happiness after marriage; her whole body was like a big red flower, blooming in the sunshine with a warm fragrance. No, I can’t bear to leave Xiangzi. No matter if he pulls a cart or begs for food, you will always have to follow him. Look, look at those women in the courtyard, if they can bear it, she can bear it. After all, she didn't want to go to Liu's house.

Xiangzi, since he left Renhe Factory, refused to walk on Xi'anmen Street again. In the past two days when he was driving a truck, he would always rush to the east city when he went out, so that the west city would be full of people and factory cars, making it awkward to meet them. On this day, after collecting the car, he deliberately passed by the factory gate for no other reason than to take a look. Huniu's words were still in his mind, as if he wanted to test whether he had the courage to return to the factory. If Huniu could make an agreement with the old man, he would first try to dare to walk on this street before returning to the factory. Pulling his hat down, he slipped away to the factory, lest he be seen by acquaintances. Seeing the light of the car door from a distance, he felt very sad for some reason. I remembered the time when I first came here, the temptation of Huniu, and the scene on my birthday night. These are all very clear, like some pictures floating in front of my eyes. Between these pictures, there are others, clearly and briefly sandwiched between these pictures: Western Mountain, Camel, Cao's House, Detective... They are all distinct, terrifying, and connected together. These pictures were so clear that he felt a little dazed in his heart, almost as if he was really looking at a few pictures and forgot that he was in them. When he thought about his relationship with them, his mind became confused. They suddenly rotated up and down, left and right, in a messy and confusing manner. He could not remember why he should suffer these tortures and grievances. The time these scenes took up seemed to be very long, but also seemed to be very short. He couldn't tell how old he was. He just felt that he was much older than when he first came to Renhe Factory. At that time, he was full of hope; now, he is full of worry. He didn't know why, but the pictures never deceived him.

In front of him were the people and the factory. He stood on the other side of the street and stared blankly at the extremely bright electric light. As I looked at it, my heart suddenly moved. The four golden characters under the lamp - Renhe and Depot - have changed! He was illiterate, but he remembered what the first character looked like: it looked like two sticks joined together, neither a fork nor a triangle, such a simple and strange character. Find the word based on the sound, which is probably "人". This "person" changed his appearance and became "benevolence" - a word even stranger than "person". He couldn't think of any reason. Looking at the east and west rooms—the two rooms he could never forget—there were no lights on.

He was so impatient that he lowered his head and walked home. As I walked, I wondered, could it be that the people and the factory had fallen out? He had to inquire slowly, without saying anything to his wife first. Back home, Huniu was eating melon seeds in the house to relieve her boredom. "It's so late again!" There was no anger on her face. "Let me tell you, I can't stand it! You go out for the whole day. I don't even dare to move out of my house. I'm a poor man in the yard for fear of losing my things. I don't even have a place to say a word all day long. No, I'm not a stupid person. You have to think of something, this won't work!" Xiangzi said nothing.

"You're talking! Why do you want to tease people? Do you have a mouth? Do you have a mouth?" Her words became faster and crisper as she spoke, sounding like a cannon. Xiangzi still had nothing to say.

"That's great," she was really anxious, but she also looked a little helpless. Her face was neither crying nor smiling, she was so anxious that she couldn't do her best. "Let's buy two cars and rent them out. Is it okay for you to eat at home? Is that okay?" "Two cars cost thirty cents a day, which is not enough for food! Rent one and I will pull one myself, so that's fine!" Xiangzi spoke slowly but naturally; when he heard about buying a car, he forgot everything.

"Isn't that the same thing? You're still homeless!"

"That's okay," Xiangzi's idea seemed to follow the problem of the car, "rent one out and rent it for the whole day. I'll drive that one myself for half a day and rent it out for another half day. If I rent it during the day, I'll go out early in the morning and come back at three o'clock; if I rent it late, I'll go out at three o'clock and come back at night. That's good!" She nodded. "Let me think about it. If I don't have any better ideas, I'll just do it."

Xiangzi was very happy. If this idea can be realized, he will be in his own car again. Although my wife bought it for me, as I slowly saved up money, I was able to buy another car myself. It was only then that he realized that Hu Niu had some benefits. He actually smiled at her, an innocent and heartfelt smile, as if he had written off all the previous hardships and changed into a new world with a smile. It was as easy and happy as changing a piece of clothing!

Learning Resource Sharing Seventeen

Xiangzi slowly found out about Renhe and the factory: Mr. Liu sold some of the cars and gave the rest to a famous car owner in Xicheng. Xiangzi could guess that the old man was old and he couldn't continue the business without his daughter to help him, so he simply closed it and took the money to enjoy himself. Where has he gone? Xiangzi didn't find out.

He couldn't tell whether he should like the news or not. Due to his own ambition and arrogance, Fourth Master Liu was determined to abandon his daughter, so Huniu's plan was completely in vain; he could honestly pull a cart to earn a living, without relying on anyone. Judging from Fourth Master Liu's small fortune, it's really a pity; who knows how Old Man Liu managed to get the money out, as he and Huniu didn't even get a single copper in their hands.

However, now that the matter has come to this, he has not thought much about it, let alone been tempted by it. This is what he thinks. Anyway, his strength is his own, and if he is willing to work hard to make money, eating will not be a problem. He told Huniu simply without emotion at all. She was moved. After hearing this, she immediately saw her future clearly - it was over! It's all over! I have no choice but to be the wife of a coachman for the rest of my life! She can never escape from this courtyard! She thought that her father would marry another wife, but she never expected that she would leave so tremblingly. If the old man really marries a little wife, Hu Niu will fight for the property, and maybe she can contact her stepmother and get some benefits... There are many ideas, as long as the old man keeps running the car factory. I never expected that the old man would be so determined and vicious, turning all his property into cash and hiding it secretly! She originally thought that her falling out with him was just a ploy and she would make peace soon. She knew that the relationship between the people and the factory was dependent on her. Who would have thought that the old man would let go of the factory? !

There is news of spring, and the buds on the branches are obviously red and fat. But in this large courtyard, spring does not arrive on the branches first, and there is not a single flower or tree here. Here, the spring breeze first blew up some small pockmarks on the ice in the courtyard, blew out some fishy smell from the filthy soil, and blew the scraps and shredded paper to the corner of the wall, creating a small whirlwind. People in the courtyard are in trouble all the time. Only now did the old people dare to come out to bask in the warmth; only now did the young girls lose some of the soot on their noses, revealing some reddish-yellow skin; Only now did the women dare to drive their children to the courtyard to play without feeling ashamed; Only now did the children dare to pull a piece of torn paper as a kite, and run around the courtyard casually, without cracking a few cuts in their little black hands from the cold. However, the porridge factory stopped making pots, the people giving relief stopped giving out rice, and the doers of good deeds stopped giving out money; it seemed that the poor people were handed over to the spring breeze and spring light! It is when spring wheat is just as green as grass and old grain is in short supply, the price of grain and rice rises as usual. The days are getting longer, and even the elderly cannot lie down early to deceive their hungry stomachs with dreams. Spring has arrived in the human world, and it has only increased difficulties in this large courtyard. Elderly lice--particularly powerful--sometimes crawl outside the cotton pads of the elderly or children to enjoy a little bit of spring!

Huniu looked at the melting ice in the courtyard and the tattered clothes, smelled the complex and slightly hot smell, and listened to the lamentations of the old people and the cries of the children, and her heart felt cold. In the winter, people hid in their houses, and dirty things were frozen on the ice; now, people come out, and things show their original shape. Even the broken brick walls are falling down, as if they are ready to collapse when it rains. The courtyard is full of green flowers, with poor flowers blooming, and they are several times uglier than in winter. Huh, just at this moment, she felt that she would live here forever; her little money would eventually be spent, and Xiangzi was just a cart driver!

Asking Xiangzi to look after the house, she went to Nanyuan to find her aunt and inquire about the old man. The aunt said that the fourth master did come to her house once, probably on the twelfth day of the first lunar month, firstly to thank her, and secondly to tell her that he planned to go to Tianjin or Shanghai for fun. He said: I have lived my whole life without ever leaving the capital, so I can’t be considered a hero after all. While I still have some breath, I go to various places to see things. Besides, he himself has no shame in wandering around the city anymore because his daughter has disgraced him. The aunt's report was just this, and her judgment was even simpler: the old man might have really gone out, or he might just be talking about it, and he was hiding in a secluded place; who knows!

When she got home, she threw herself on the kang and started crying. She cried for a long time without any hypocrisy or cunning, until her eyeballs were swollen.

After crying, she wiped her tears and said to Xiangzi: "Okay, you are so arrogant! I have to follow you! I bet my money on the wrong place. Just marry the chicken and follow the chicken, don't say anything. I'll give you a hundred yuan, and you can buy a car!"

Here, she made a conscious decision: she originally wanted to buy two cars, one for Xiangzi to pull and one to rent. Now she changed her mind and just bought one and asked Xiangzi to pull it; she still kept the rest of the money in her own hands. The money is in her hands, and the power is also in her, but she refuses to spend it all; what if Xiangzi changes his mind after spending all the money to buy a car? This cannot be ignored! Besides, Old Man Liu left like this, which made her feel that nothing was reliable. No one could know for sure what would happen tomorrow. It was best to have fun and have some money in hand. She could eat whatever she liked. She was always used to eating snacks. Use what Xiangzi earns - he is a first-class coachman - to live on, and then have the little money of your own to supplement your pocket, and focus on the immediate pleasure first. One day the money will be spent, but people will not live forever! Marrying a cart driver—even though it was a last resort—had already wronged me. I could no longer ask him for money every day with my hands down, but I didn’t have a single copper in my pocket. This decision made her a little happier. Although she knew that the future would be terrible, she would not fall head over heels immediately at the moment; it was like walking towards sunset, the distance was already dark, but there was still some light in front of her, so she should take advantage of the light and take a few more steps.

Xiangzi didn't argue with her, just buy one. As long as it's your own car, you can earn sixty or seventy cents a day, enough to chew grain. Not only did he not argue, he also felt a little happy. All the hard work I endured in the past was just to buy a car. Now that I can buy it again, what else can I say? Of course, if a car is used to feed two people, there will be no money left over; the car may become old, and there is no preparation to buy a new car. Danger! However, since buying a car is so difficult, you should be satisfied if you can buy it now, so why think so far ahead!

Erqiangzi in the courtyard is about to sell a car. Er Qiangzi sold his nineteen-year-old daughter Xiao Fuzi to a soldier last summer. Sold for two hundred yuan. After Xiaofuzi left, Erqiangzi became quite wealthy for a while. He redeemed all his pawns and made some new clothes. The whole family was dressed neatly. Erqiang's wife was the shortest and ugliest woman in the whole hospital, with a protruding forehead, big cheeks, no hair on her head, old teeth exposed, and her face was covered with freckles, which made her look disgusting. She also had red eyelids and put on a new blue shirt while crying for her daughter. Er Qiangzi had always had a bad temper. After selling his daughter, he often drank several glasses of wine. After drinking, tears would form in his eyes, and he was very easy to find trouble. Although Sister-in-law Erqiang had a new coat and had enough to eat, her joy was not worth the pain, and she was beaten almost twice as often as before. Er Qiangzi is over forty and plans not to pull carts anymore. So I bought a pair of baskets and a grocery pick, including melons, fruits, pears, peanuts and cigarettes. The goods were very complete. After two months of business, there was a thick pile of accounts. Not only was the loss made, but the loss was a lot. If he is used to pulling a cart, he will not know how to deal with business; pulling a cart is a hit-and-miss thing, success will be achieved and failure will bring him down; he will not know how to deal with the hardships of doing small business. The man who pulled the cart knew how to get things on credit, so he was reluctant to let his acquaintances owe money; if he owed money, it would be difficult to get it back. In this way, the good-looking master couldn't get him, and everyone who dealt with him was greedy for credit and refused to give him credit, so he had no choice but to lose money. He feels sad when he loses money; when he feels sad, he drinks more. When he was drunk, he often quarreled with the patrolmen outside and was murderous at home with his wife and children. I offended the patrolman and beat my wife, all because of alcohol. When he woke up from the wine, he felt very regretful and miserable. When he thought about it again, he had to pay for this money in exchange for his daughter, and he was paying for it in vain, and he was drinking and beating people. He felt that he was not a human being. At this time, he could sleep for a day and leave his worries to Meng.

He decided to give up the business and went to pull the car instead, and he couldn't let all the money go to waste. He bought a car. When he was drunk, he was unreasonable. In his sobriety, he was above all respectable. Because he loves dignity, he often puts on airs of poverty and has a plan for everything. He bought a new car and was dressed neatly. He felt that he was a high-class coachman. He had to drink good tea and pull a decent seat. He could show off his car and his white trousers at the entrance of the car and chat with everyone, never bothering to do business. Sometimes he slaps the car with a new blue cloth duster, sometimes he stamps his new white-soled double-faced shoes, sometimes he looks at the tip of his nose and stands by the car with a smile, waiting for others to praise his car, and then he starts talking and talks endlessly. He can spend a day or two "soaking" like this. When he got into a good seat, his legs couldn't run without the strength of his car and clothes! This made him very sad. Whenever I feel sad, I think of my daughter, so I have to go drink. In this way, all his money was put away, leaving only the car.

Around the beginning of winter, he got drunk again. As soon as they entered the house, the two sons—one thirteen and one eleven years old—wanted to hide outside. This trick overturned him and gave each of them a kick. Erqiang's wife said something, and he ran towards her and kicked her in the stomach. She lay on the ground without making a sound for a long time. The two children were anxious. One picked up the coal shovel and the other picked up the rolling pin, and fought for their lives with their father. The three of them fought together and stepped on Erqiang's wife several times. The neighbors came over and finally pushed Er Qiangzi down on the kang. The two children hugged their mother and cried. Erqiang's wife woke up, but she still couldn't get off the ground. On the third day of the twelfth lunar month, she stopped breathing and was wearing the blue shirt she made when selling her daughter. Erqiang's wife's mother-in-law didn't agree, so she had to go to court. After much persuasion from her friends, her parents gave in, and Erqiangzi agreed to send her away properly and give her family fifteen yuan. He took the car out and deposited sixty yuan. Next year, he wanted to sell the car, but he had no hope of redeeming it himself. When he was drunk, he wanted to sell his son, but no one wanted him. He also looked for Xiao Fuzi's husband. They didn't recognize him as a father-in-law at all, so there was no need to say anything else.

Xiangzi knew the history of this car and didn't like it very much. There were too many cars, so why buy just this one? This was an unlucky car. This car was bought in exchange for his daughter, but he only sold it because he beat his wife to death! Huniu didn't think so. She wanted to spend her early eighties to buy it. It was cheap! The car has only been driven for half a year, and even the color of the belt has not changed much, and it was authentically made by Dechengjia, a famous factory in Xicheng. Why don’t you pay fifty or sixty yuan to buy a 70% new car? She couldn't bear the bargain. She also knew that soon after the New Year, money was tight everywhere, and Erqiangzi would not sell it for a big price, but she was eager to use the money. She went to see the car in person, negotiated the price with Er Qiangzi, and paid the money; Xiangzi had to wait for the car to be pulled, saying nothing, and it was inconvenient to say anything, because the money was not his own. After buying the car, he looked at it carefully and found that it was indeed very strong. But he always felt a little awkward. What made him most unhappy was the black-painted car body, which was paired with a white copper body. When Er Qiangzi drove the car, it was black and white, which looked very beautiful. Xiangzi always felt that this was a bit depressing, like wearing a filial piety. He really wanted to change his condom to something earthy yellow or moon-white, or something that would make it less sober. But he didn't discuss it with Huniu, so as not to invite her to gossip again.

When the car was pulled out, everyone paid special attention, and some people actually called it "the little widow." Xiangzi felt unhappy. He tried his best not to think about it, but the car followed him all day long, and his hair was cooing, as if something was going to go wrong at some point. Sometimes I suddenly think of Er Qiangzi and his encounter with Er Qiangzi. It seems that he is not pulling a car, but a coffin. In this car, he often saw some ghost figures, as if they were ghosts.

However, nothing happened since he got into the car, although he was feeling uneasy in his heart. The weather is getting warmer and warmer, and you can wear single pants and a jacket without having to wear cotton clothes. There is not much spring in Peiping. The sky is getting so long that it almost makes people impatient, and everyone feels sleepy. Xiangzi went out early in the morning, and by four or five o'clock, he felt that he had exerted enough effort. The sun is still high. He didn't want to run anymore, but he also refused to put the car away, and he yawned long and lazily hesitantly.

The days are so long. If Xiangzi feels tired and bored, Huniu will be even more lonely at home. In winter, she could keep warm by the stove and listen to the sound of the wind outside. Although she was depressed, she still had a little bit of "it's better not to go out" to masturbate. Now that the stove has been moved to the eaves, there is literally nothing to do in the house. The courtyard was so dirty and smelly, there was not even a blade of green grass. When I went to the street, I didn't worry about the neighbors. Even if I went to buy something, I had to go straight there, and I didn't dare to wander around for a while. She was like a bee trapped in the room, staring at the sunshine outside but unable to fly out. She could not talk to the women in the courtyard. What they said was that the parents were short-lived, and she was used to being wild and toneless. She didn't like to talk about it, and she didn't like to listen to it either. Their grievances come from the pain in life, and every little thing can lead to tears; her grievances come from dissatisfaction with life. She has no tears to shed, but wants to scold someone and vent her sullenness. She and they can't understand each other, so it's best to do their own thing without talking to each other[1]

.

It wasn't until the middle of April that she found a companion. Erqiangzi's daughter Xiaofuzi is back. Xiaofuzi’s “person”[2]

It's an officer. He set up a very simple home everywhere, spent 1,200 on a young girl, and bought a large plank and two chairs, and then he could live happily. When the army was transferred elsewhere, he walked away and put the people and the planks where they were. Spending so much, 1,200 yuan, you won't suffer a loss in a year and a half. Just talking about small things such as washing clothes, cooking, etc. If you hire a servant, wouldn't it cost ten or eight yuan to eat and earn a month? If you marry a girl like this, she will be a servant, you can sleep with her, and you will be sure to be clean and not sick. I'm so happy that I can cut her a large floral blouse for a lot of money. If she's not happy, there's nothing she can do if she has to squat at home without seeing anything. When he left for work, he didn't regret the planks and one or two chairs at all, because she had to find a way to pay the two months' rent that was owed. Selling the planks and other things might not be enough to pay off the debt.

Xiao Fuzi sold the shop floor and paid the rent, and returned home wearing only a floral foreign cloth shirt and a pair of silver earrings.

After Er Qiangzi sold the car, in addition to paying back the deposit and interest, he still had about twenty yuan left. Sometimes he feels that he has lost his wife in middle age and is very pitiful. When others don't pity him, he drinks a glass of wine and good food by himself to feel sorry for himself and masturbate. At this time, he seemed to have a grudge against money and spent all his money. Sometimes he thought that he should work harder to pull the cart and raise the two boys properly, so that he would have some hope for the future. When he thought about his son, he bought a lot of food for them both. Seeing them gobbling up the food, he said to himself with tears in his eyes: "Motherless child! Poor child! Dad has to work hard, and it is the child who is running! I am not surrendered. It doesn't matter whether I am full or not. I have to let the child eat enough first! Eat! Don't forget me when you grow up!" At this time, he also spent a lot of money. Slowly, all the twenty yuan was paid out.

When he ran out of money and caught up with him drinking and losing his temper, he would not care what the children ate for a day or two. The children had no choice but to think of ways to get a few coins and buy something to eat. They would work as deacons for people who were doing official and white things, and would pick up some scraps of copper and scrap paper from the carts. Sometimes they could buy a few sesame cakes, sometimes they could only buy a pound of wheat stubble and white potatoes, and even swallowed the belts and whiskers. Sometimes only two of them had a big copper, so they had to buy peanuts or iron broad beans. Although they couldn't stave off hunger, they could chew them for a while.

Xiao Fuzi came back, and they met their relatives. Each of them hugged one of her legs. They had nothing to say and only smiled at her through tears. My mother is gone, my sister is my mother!

Er Qiangzi had no expression for his daughter's return. When she came back, there was an extra person to eat. However, seeing his two sons so happy, he had to admit that there should be a woman in the family to cook and wash clothes for everyone. It's not easy for him to say anything, just go wherever he goes.

Xiao Fuzi is not ugly. Although he was very thin before, he has gained some weight and become taller since joining that officer. He has a round face and well-proportioned eyebrows. There is nothing particularly outstanding about him, but he is solid and not ugly. The upper lip is very short. Whether you want to be angry or smile, you open your lips first, revealing some very white and neat teeth. That officer just loved her teeth. Showing these teeth, she looked a little stupid and thoughtless, and at the same time she seemed a little coquettish. This air made her - like all girls who are poor but not ugly - like flowers and plants, which are selected for sale in the market if they have the slightest fragrance or color.

Huniu always ignored the people in the courtyard, but she regarded Xiao Fuzi as a friend. Firstly, Xiao Fuzi looked pretty, secondly she had a floral robe, and thirdly Huniu thought that since she was married to an officer, she had seen the world, so she was willing to associate with her. It is not easy for women to make friends, but they are quick to make friends; within a few days, the two of them had become close friends. Huniu loves to eat snacks. Whenever she gets some melon seeds or other things, she always calls Xiao Fuzi over and eats them while laughing. While joking, Xiao Fuzi showed his white teeth stupidly and told many things that Huniu had never heard of. She did not enjoy the blessings of being with the officer, but the officer was happy and took her back to the restaurant and to see a show, so she had something to say and said it to make Hu Niu envious. There are many things she can't say: for her, this is ravage; for Huniu, this is enjoyment. Huniu begged her, saying that she was embarrassed to say it, but also embarrassed to refuse. She has seen the erotic palace, but Huniu has never seen it. Huniu heard things like this once and liked to hear them a second time. She regarded Xiao Fuzi as the cutest, most enviable, and worthy of envy. After hearing that, and looking at her appearance, age, and husband, she felt that her life was too wronged. She has never had youth, and there is no hope for the future. Now, Xiangzi is as dead as a brick! The more she was dissatisfied with Xiangzi, the more she loved Xiaofuzi. Although Xiaofuzi was so poor and pitiful, in her eyes, he was a man who had experienced blessings and had seen battle formations. Even if he died immediately, he would not be unjust. In her opinion, Xiaofuzi represents the enjoyment that a woman should have.

Huniu didn't seem to notice Xiao Fuzi's hardship. Xiao Fuzi didn't bring anything back, but she had to take care of her two brothers no matter how tough her father was. Where would she get the money to prepare meals for them?

Er Qiangzi got drunk and had an idea: "If you really care about your brothers, you can find a way to make money to support them! They are all pointing at me. I have to work as a livestock for others all the time. I have to eat first; can I run on an empty stomach? Teach me to fall to death. What do you think of Coke? If you are idle, you are also idle. If you have something ready-made, why don't you sell it?"

Looking at his father who was as hungry as a drunk cat, at himself, and at his two younger brothers who were as hungry as mice, Xiao Fuzi was left crying. Tears couldn't move her father, and tears couldn't feed her brother. She had to come up with something more real. In order to feed her brothers, she had to sell her own meat. Hugging her little brother, her tears fell on his hair, and he said: "Sister, I'm hungry!" Sister! My sister is a piece of meat and I have to give it to my brother!

Instead of comforting Xiao Fuzi, Huniu was willing to help her: Huniu was willing to use some capital to teach her to dress up neatly, and then return the money to her. Huniu was willing to lend her a place because her own room was too dirty, and Huniu's house had Xiangzi at least, and since there were two rooms, everyone had a place to turn around. Xiangzi will not come back during the day, so Huniu is happy to help her friends, and she can see more and understand more, what she lacks and what she wants to do but cannot do. Every time Xiao Fuzi used the room, Huniu made a condition that she must give her two cents. Friends are friends, things are things. For Xiao Fuzi's sake, she has to clean up the house well. She has to work and spend some money. Shouldn't she spend money on buying brooms and dustpans? Two cents is by no means too much, because we are friends, so we can meet like this.

Xiao Fuzi showed some teeth and tears fell into his stomach.

Xiangzi didn't know anything, but he couldn't sleep well again. Huniu "fulfilled" Xiao Fuzi, and she also wanted to find the lost youth in Xiangzi.
