eighteen

In June, there was almost no sound in the courtyard during the day. The children spent the early hours carrying broken baskets to pick up whatever they could find; by nine o'clock, the poisonous sun was already burning their skinny backs, so they had no choice but to bring back what they could find and eat whatever food the adults could give them. Then, if the older ones can find the smallest capital in the world, they will buy and pick up some ice cores to sell. If you can't find this little capital, you can go out of the city to take a bath in the moat, steal a few pieces of coal at the station, or catch some dragonflies and cicadas to sell to the children of wealthy families. The younger ones didn't dare to run far away, so they all went to the area with trees outside the door to pick up locust insects and dig "King Kong" [1]

Go play or something. The children all went out, and so did the men. The women were all naked in the house, and no one wanted to come out; not because they were afraid of looking bad, but because the ground in the courtyard was already burning their feet in the sun.

It wasn't until the sun was about to set that the men and children came back one after another. At this time, there were wall shadows and some cool breeze in the courtyard, and the house was surrounded by the heat of the day, like fire cages; everyone was sitting in the courtyard, waiting for the women to cook. At this moment, the courtyard was very lively, like a market without goods. Everyone had been suffering from the heat for a day, their eyes were red, and they were not in a good temper. They were hungry, and they all looked anxious and pale. If someone says something wrong, some will beat their children, and some will beat their wives; even if they can't beat them, they will scold them. This commotion continued until everyone had finished their meal. Some children lie down in the courtyard and fall asleep, while others go to the street to have sex[2]

. After the adults had eaten, their tempers became much calmer, and the talkative ones only gathered in groups and talked about the hard work of the day. Those who have no food to eat must have nowhere to pawn or sell—even if they have something to pawn or sell—because it’s getting dark. No matter how hot the room is, the man will just lie on the kang and remain silent, maybe yelling loudly. With tears in her eyes, the woman tried to accommodate everyone, but she encountered many obstacles before she was able to borrow a twenty-dollar piece of paper. Holding this precious note, she went out to get some mixed noodles and a pot of porridge for everyone to eat.

Huniu and Xiaofuzi are not in this order of life. Hu Niu is pregnant, this time it’s true. Xiangzi goes out early in the morning, and she always doesn't get up until eight or nine o'clock; it is a traditional erroneous belief that you should not exercise too much during pregnancy. Huniu not only believes in this, but also uses it to show some identity: everyone has to get up early to operate, but she can lie down as leisurely as she likes. At night, she took a small bench and sat outside the street gate in a windy place. She didn't come in until almost everyone in the courtyard was asleep. She didn't bother to chat with everyone.

Xiao Fuzi also got up late, but she had other reasons. She was afraid that the men in the yard would look at her sideways, so she didn't dare to leave the house until they were all gone. During the day, she either looks for Hu Niu or goes out for a walk, because her advertisement is herself. At night, in order to avoid the attention of people in the courtyard, she went out to walk around the streets, and after asking everyone to lie down, she sneaked in secretly.

Among men, Xiangzi and Erqiangzi are exceptions. Xiangzi was afraid of entering the courtyard, and even more afraid of going inside. The poor comments from everyone in the courtyard made him panic, and he wanted to find a quiet place to sit alone. Inside the house, he felt more and more that Hu Niu looked like a tigress. It was so hot and suffocating in the hut, and with the tiger in the mix, he seemed to be out of breath as soon as he entered. A few days ago, he had no choice but to come back early in order to avoid Huniu making noises with him. Recently, with Xiao Fuzi as her company, she doesn't control him much anymore, so he comes back later.

As for Er Qiangzi, he rarely comes home these days. He knew his daughter's business and was too embarrassed to enter that street gate. But he couldn't stop her, he knew he didn't have the strength to feed his children. He had no choice but not to come back, out of sight and out of mind. Sometimes he hates his daughter. If Xiao Fuzi were a boy, I guarantee he wouldn't have to make a fool of himself like this; since she was a girl, why did she come to him! Sometimes he pities his daughter, who sells herself to support her two younger brothers! Hate it or hurt it, he can't help it. When he got drunk and had no money in his hand, he no longer hated her and felt sorry for her. He came back and asked her for money. At this time, he sees his daughter as someone who can make money, and since he is a father, it is legitimate for him to ask for money from her. At this time, he also thought about his dignity: Didn't everyone look down on Xiao Fuzi? Her father didn't spare her. He forced her to take the money and cursed her, as if he was scolding everyone - Er Qiangzi was not wrong, Xiao Fuzi was born shameless.

He was noisy, but Xiao Fuzi didn't even express his anger. Huniu, on the other hand, half scolded and half persuaded him to get rid of him. Naturally, he had to take some money from his hand. This kind of money only allowed him to drink again, because if he saw them sober, he would jump into the river or hang himself.

On June 15th, it was crazy hot. As soon as the sun came up, the ground seemed to be on fire. Some gray air that looks like clouds but not clouds, like mist but not mist floats low in the air, making people feel suffocated. There was no wind at all. Xiangzi looked at the gray-red sky in the courtyard, and planned to stay late - not go out until four in the afternoon; if he couldn't make any money, he could stay late until dawn: no matter what, it would be better at night than during the day.

Huniu urged him to go out, fearing that he would be a hindrance at home, in case Xiaofuzi brought a guest. "Why do you think it's better to be at home? Even the walls in the house are hot at noon!"

Without saying a word, he drank a gourd of cold water and walked out.

The willow trees on the street seemed to be sick, with leaves hung with a layer of dust and curled up on the branches; the branches were too lazy to move, hanging down listlessly. There was not a single drop of water on the road, which was dry and glowing white. The dust on the sidewalk flew up high and connected with the gray air in the sky, forming a vicious formation of gray sand that burned the faces of passers-by. It's dry everywhere, it's hot everywhere, and it's stuffy everywhere. The whole old city is like a well-fired brick kiln, making people breathless. Dogs lay on the ground sticking out their red tongues, the nostrils of mules and horses dilated extremely wide, vendors did not dare to shout, and the asphalt road melted away; even the bronze plaques in front of the shops seemed to be melted by the sun. The street was unusually quiet, except for the irritating monotonous tinkling sounds coming from the copper and iron shops. The people pulling the carts knew that they would have nothing to eat if they were not active, and they were too lazy to do business: some put the carts in a shady place, set up the shed, and sat on the carts to take a nap; some went into small teahouses to drink tea; some did not pull out the carts at all, but came to the street to see if there was any possibility of getting out of the carts. Those who were in charge of the business, even the most beautiful young men, were willing to be embarrassed and did not dare to run anymore. They only lowered their heads and walked slowly. Every well platform has become their savior. No matter how many steps they have taken, they will run to the well when they see it. If they can't catch up with the newly drawn water, they will fill the trough with the donkeys and horses. Others, because of heat stroke or fever, would fall to the ground while walking and never get up.

Even Xiangzi is a little timid! After pulling the empty cart for a few steps, he felt that he was surrounded by heat from his face to his feet, and even the backs of his hands were sweating. However, when he saw Zuo'er, he still wanted to pull away, thinking that maybe there would be some wind while running. He made a deal and pulled up the car, only to realize that the weather was so severe that no one was allowed to work. As soon as I ran, I couldn't breathe, and my lips were burnt. Even though I was not thirsty, I wanted to drink water when I saw it. If I don't run, the poisonous flower's handles and back will be cracked by the sun. He managed to pull it into place, and his pants and jacket were completely wrapped around him. Pick up the banana fan and fan it. The wind is hot. He had already drank a lot of cold water, but he ran to the teahouse again. After drinking two pots of hot tea, he felt calmer. As soon as the tea enters the mouth, sweat comes out of the body, as if the body is already empty and will not store any more water. He didn't dare to move anymore.

After sitting for a long time, he felt tired. Not daring to go out and having nothing to do, he felt as if the weather was deliberately trying to get in his way. No, he couldn't give in. He has been driving a car for more than one day, and this is not the first time in summer. He can't just "soak" for a day in vain. I wanted to go out, but I was too lazy to move my legs, and my body was very soft, as if I hadn't had a good bath. Although I was sweating a lot, my heart was not happy yet. After sitting for a while, he couldn't sit still anymore. Sitting would make him sweat anyway, so he might as well go out and give it a try.

As soon as I came out, I realized my mistake. The layer of gray in the sky has dissipated, and it is no longer depressing, but the sunshine is also much stronger: no one dares to look up to see where the sun is, only to feel that it is dazzling everywhere, in the sky, on the roof, on the walls, on the ground, it is all bright white, with a little red in it; from top to bottom, the whole thing is like a huge fire mirror, and every ray of light is like the focus of the fire mirror, making things ignite. In this white light, every color is dazzling, every sound is unpleasant, and every smell is mixed with the fishy stench evaporated from the ground. There seemed to be no people on the street, and the road seemed to have suddenly widened a lot. It was empty and not cool at all, and the white flowers were scary. Xiangzi didn't know how to recover. He lowered his head, pulled the cart, and walked forward very slowly. He had no idea or purpose. He was groggy, with a layer of sticky sweat on his body and a rancid smell. After walking for a while, the soles of my feet and my shoes and socks were stuck together, as if I were stepping on a piece of wet mud, which made me feel very sad. I didn't want to drink any more water at first, but when I saw the well, I couldn't help but go over and take another sip, not to quench my thirst, but to enjoy the coolness of the well water. From my mouth to my stomach, I suddenly felt cold, and the pores on my body suddenly shrank, and I shivered, which was very comfortable. After drinking, he burped repeatedly, and the water was rising!

Walking for a while, sitting for a while, he was still too lazy to do business. Until noon, he still couldn't feel hungry. I want to eat something as usual, but the sight of food makes me sick. The stomach is almost filled with all kinds of water, and sometimes there will be a slight sound in the stomach, like a mule drinking water, and the stomach will make a light sound.

Comparing winter with summer, Xiangzi always thinks winter is more terrible. He didn't expect that summer would be so uncomfortable. After spending more than one summer in the city, he couldn't remember it being this hot. Is it because the weather is hotter than in previous years, or is my body weak? Thinking of this, he suddenly became less groggy, and his heart seemed to feel cold. My body, yes, my body is not good anymore! He was scared, but there was nothing he could do. He couldn't drive away Huniu. He was going to become Er Qiangzi, the tall man he met that time, and Xiao Ma'er's grandfather. Xiangzi is finished!

At one o'clock in the afternoon, he made another deal. It was the hottest time of the day, the hottest day of the summer, but he decided to go for a run. He didn't care how hot it was under the sun: if he didn't feel any better after doing the job, it would prove that his body was not broken; if he couldn't pull off the deal, then there was nothing else to say, and he might as well have fallen dead on the burning ground!

After taking a few steps, he felt a bit of cool breeze, just like a bit of cool air coming in through the crack of the door in an extremely hot room. He couldn't believe himself; looking at the willow branches by the road, they did move slightly. Suddenly there were more people on the street, and people in the shops rushed out, holding cattail leaf fans to cover their heads, looking around: "There's a cool breeze! There's a cool breeze! The cool breeze is coming down!" Everyone almost jumped up and shouted. The willow tree beside the road suddenly turned into an angelic shape, conveying the message from heaven: "The willow branches are moving! God, please enjoy more cool breeze!"

It's still hot, but I feel much calmer. The cool breeze, even a little bit, gives people a lot of hope. After a few gusts of cool breeze passed, the sunlight was no longer so strong. It was brighter and darker, as if there was a piece of flying sand floating on it. The wind suddenly picked up, and the willow branches that had not moved for a long time seemed to have suddenly received something gratifying, and they swayed freely, and the branches seemed to have grown out. A gust of wind passed, the sky darkened, and all the dust flew into the air. Some dust fell, and ink-like dark clouds appeared on the northern horizon. Xiangzi stopped sweating. He glanced to the north, stopped the car, and put on the raincloth. He knew that the summer rain would come at a moment's notice and would not take any time.

As soon as the raincloth was put on, there was another gust of wind, and black clouds seemed to be rolling in, covering half of the sky. The hot air on the ground mixed with the cool wind, mixed with the smelly dry soil, which seemed to be cool and hot at the same time; half of the sky in the south was sunny and sunny, and half of the sky in the north was covered with dark clouds, as if some disaster was coming, and everything was panicked. The rickshaw driver was in a hurry to put on the raincloth, the shopkeepers were busy folding the covers, the hawkers were hurriedly packing up their stalls, and the travelers were rushing forward. Another gust of wind. When the wind passed, the street banners, stalls, and pedestrians seemed to have been swept away by the wind, and they were all gone, leaving only the willow branches dancing wildly in the wind.

The clouds had not yet covered the sky, but it was already very dark on the ground. It was as if the extremely bright and hot sunny afternoon had suddenly turned into a dark night. The wind carried the rain stars, as if they were searching for something on the ground, bumping around here and there. There was a red flash in the distance to the north, as if a black cloud had been lifted away, revealing a large area of ​​blood. The wind has died down, but it is blowing sharply and makes people shiver. After a gust of wind like this passed by, everything seemed to be different for some reason. Even the willow trees were waiting for something in confusion. Another flash, right above the head, bright white raindrops fell immediately, kicking up a lot of dust very hard, with a slight hint of rain in the soil. Heavy raindrops hit Xiangzi's back, and he shivered twice. The raindrops stopped and dark clouds spread across the sky. Another gust of wind came, stronger than the previous one. The willow branches flew sideways, the dust moved in all directions, and the rain fell; wind, soil, and rain were all mixed together, and they were all gray and cold horizontally and vertically. Everything was wrapped in it, and it was not clear which was the tree, which was the ground, which was the cloud. Everything was chaotic, noisy, and confused in all directions. The wind passed, leaving only straight rain channels, falling wildly, and one couldn't see clearly, just one piece. After a while, countless arrows shot up from the ground, and thousands of waterfalls fell from the houses. Within a few minutes, the sky and the earth were inseparable. The river in the sky fell down, and the river on the ground flowed across it, turning into a gray and yellow, sometimes white and bright, water world.

Xiangzi's clothes were already soaked, and there was not a single dry spot on his body; through his straw hat, his hair was completely wet. The water on the ground reached over his feet, making it difficult to walk; the rain above hit his head and back, swept across his face, and wrapped around his crotch. He couldn't raise his head, open his eyes, breathe, or step. He seemed to be standing still in the water. He didn't know where the road was, and he didn't know what was around him. He could only feel the bone-chilling water being poured on every part of his body. He didn't know anything anymore. He only felt a little warm in his heart and heard the sound of rain in his ears. He wanted to put the car down, but he didn't know where to put it. He wanted to run, but the water wrapped around his legs. He was just half-dead, half-alive, lowering his head and dragging forward step by step. The rider seemed to have died in the car, silently letting the driver struggle for his life in the water.

The rain subsided a little, Xiangzi straightened his back slightly and exhaled: "Sir, please stay away before leaving!"

"Come on! What's wrong with you leaving me here?" The man in the car shouted, stamping his feet.

Xiangzi really wanted to put the car down and find a place to take shelter. However, looking at his body, water was already flowing down his body. He knew that he would tremble into a ball if he stood still. He gritted his teeth and ran through the water no matter how high or low it was. As soon as he ran not far away, it became dark for a while, then lightened up, and the rain once again fascinated his eyes.

When I got there, I didn't even give the bus driver a single copper. Xiangzi didn't say anything. He couldn't care less about his life.

The rain stayed for a while, then started falling again, much lighter than before. Xiangzi ran home angrily. Holding the fire for a while, he shivered like a leaf in the wind and rain. Huniu made him a bowl of ginger syrup, and he drank it all in one go holding the bowl like a fool. After drinking, he got out of bed and knew nothing more. He seemed to be asleep, with the sound of rain in his ears.

By about four o'clock, the black clouds began to show signs of fatigue, flickering weakly and not quite red. After a while, the clouds in the west split, and the black cloud peaks were edged with golden, and some white air rushed under the clouds; Shendu went to the south, pulling a few thunders that were not very loud. After staying for a while, the sun peeked out of the clouds in the west, turning the rain-stained leaves into golden green. A pair of seven-color rainbows hung in the east sky, with two ends stuck in the dark clouds, and a patch of blue sky on the back of the bridge. The rainbow dissipated soon, and there was no longer a black cloud in the sky. The washed blue sky and everything seemed to be a new, cool, and beautiful world just born out of the darkness. Even the puddles in the courtyard were visited by several dragonflies of various colors.

However, except for the children chasing the dragonflies with their bare feet, the people in the courtyard did not care to appreciate the sunny day after the rain. A piece of the back eaves of Xiao Fuzi's house collapsed, and the three sisters were busy lifting up the Kang mat to block the hole. The courtyard wall collapsed in several places, and everyone had no time to take care of it. They just cleaned up their houses: some steps were too short, and water had poured into the house. Everyone was scrambling to get water out with dustpans and broken bowls. Some knocked down the gables and tried to fill them up. Some roofs leaked like a watering can, soaking everything. They were busy carrying it out and putting it next to the stove to bake or put it on the window sill to dry in the sun. When it was raining, everyone hid in the house that could collapse at any time and bury them alive, leaving their lives to God; after the rain, they calculated and packed up the losses; although after the heavy rain, a pound of grain might lose half a copper, but their losses could not be compensated by this. They spend money on the house, but no one will ever come to repair the house; unless it collapses to the point where no one can live in it anymore, one or two plasterers will come and use some plain mud and broken bricks to loosely block it up - preparing it to collapse again. When the rent couldn't be paid, the whole family was kicked out and their belongings were confiscated. The house is broken, and people can be killed by the house, but no one cares about it. Rain falls on the rich as well as on the poor; on the righteous as well as on the unjust. In fact, the rain is not fair because it falls in a world without justice.

She groped all over her body, scraped together a dozen copper coins, and gave them to her brother. My younger brother would never dare to get close to his father on weekdays, but seeing his father being beaten to the ground today, he felt a little bolder. "Here you go, let's go!"

Er Qiangzi took the money with eyes and stood up while chattering: "Leave it alone with you girls! If you offend the master, I'll kill you all with a knife!" When he was about to reach the street gate, he shouted, "Xiangzi! Leave it alone.[1]

, see you outside! "

After Er Qiangzi left, Xiangzi and Xiao Fuzi entered the house together.

"I can't help it!" She said to herself, which summed up all her difficulties and contained infinite hope - if Xiangzi was willing to marry her, she would have a way.

Xiangzi, after this scene, saw many dark shadows on her body. He still likes her, but he can't bear the responsibility of raising her two younger brothers and a drunk father! He didn't dare to think that once Huniu died, he would be free; Huniu also had the benefits of Huniu, at least she helped him a lot financially. He didn't dare to think that Xiao Fuzi would eat him, but it was absolutely true that her whole family would not be able to earn a living. To love or not to love, the poor have to decide based on money. "Love" is only born in rich families.

He started to pack his things.

"Are you going to move away?" Xiao Fuzi's lips turned completely white.

"Move away!" He was cruel. In a world without justice, poor people rely on cruelty to maintain their personal freedom, which is a very small freedom.

After looking at him, she lowered her head and walked out. She doesn't hate it, nor is she angry, just desperate.

Huniu's jewelry and better clothes were brought into the coffin. All that was left were some shabby clothes, a few wooden utensils, and some pots, pots, and spoons. Xiangzi picked out a few of the better ones and put them aside; the rest, including clothes and utensils, were sold. He called a "drum player" and sold it for more than ten yuan. He was in a hurry to move out and get rid of these things, so he didn't have the heart to find a few more people to slowly negotiate the price. The "drum player" packed away his things, leaving only his bedding and a few selected clothes in the room, which were placed on the kang without seats. The room was completely empty, and he felt a little happier, as if he had been freed from many entanglements, and he could fly far away from now on. However, after a while, he remembered those things again. The table has been moved away, but there are still some traces left on the table legs - piles of fine soil, forming several small squares against the base of the wall. Looking at these marks, he thought of things and people, as if they were all gone in a dream. No matter whether things are good or bad, no matter whether people are good or bad, without them, there will be no place for the heart. He sat on the edge of the kang and took out another "yellow lion".

Along with the cigarette, he brought out a broken piece of money. Intentionally or unintentionally, he took out all the money; for the past two days, he never bothered to do the calculation. He took out a pile of foreign money, dime notes, copper notes, copper coins, everything. The pile is not small. When I counted it, it was less than twenty yuan. After collecting more than ten yuan from selling things, his total property is only more than thirty yuan.

Putting the money on the bricks, he stared at them, not knowing whether it was better to cry or laugh. There was no one or anything in the house, only himself and a pile of old, moldy money. What is this for?

With a long sigh, he reluctantly put the money in his arms, then picked up the bedding and a few clothes and went to find Xiao Fuzi.

"You can keep these clothes and wear them! Keep the bedding for a while. I'll go find a good car dealer first and then pick it up." Not daring to look at Xiao Fuzi, he lowered his head and said all this in one breath.

She said nothing and only agreed twice.

Xiangzi found the depot and came back to get her bedding. He saw that her eyes were swollen from crying. He didn't know what to say, but he tried his best to come up with these two sentences: "Just wait! When I'm done, I'll come! I'll definitely come!"

She nodded and said nothing.

Xiangzi only rested for one day, and then went to pull the cart as usual. He wasn't as eager to do business as he was before, but he wasn't deliberately lazy either, he was just fooling around day by day without getting tired of it. After living like this for a month, he felt very peaceful in his heart. His face was a little plumper, but not as red as before; his face was yellow, not obviously strong, nor was he thin. The eyes are very bright, but there is no expression. They are always so bright and seem to be quite energetic, but they seem to have seen nothing. His look was like a tree after a storm, standing quietly in the sunshine, not daring to move at all. He didn't like talking before, and now he doesn't like talking even more. The day was already very warm, and the willow branches were already covered with young leaves. Sometimes he would put his car toward the sun, lowering his head and talking to himself with his mouth moving slightly. Sometimes he would lie on his back to receive the sunshine and take a nap. Except when he had to speak, he rarely talked to others.

I have become addicted to cigarettes. As soon as he sat in the car, his big hands went under the breast pads. He lit a cigarette, inhaled and exhaled very slowly, looked up at the smoke rings, stared blankly, and then nodded, as if he understood something.

When he pulled up the cart, he could run faster than the average cart driver, but he no longer ran as hard as he could. He was particularly careful when going around corners and going up and down slopes. Almost overly cautious. Someone wanted to race with him. No matter what kind of teasing provoked him, he lowered his head and said nothing, and still ran neither fast nor slow. He seemed to have seen through what pulling a cart was all about and no longer wanted any glory or praise from it.

In the factory, he made friends; although he didn't like to talk much, the silent geese also liked to fly in groups. If he doesn't make friends, his loneliness may not be bearable. As soon as he took out his cigarette box, he passed it around to everyone. Sometimes people see that there is only one stick left in his box and are embarrassed to reach out, so he simply says: "Buy more!" When he catches everyone gambling, he does not hide aside like before, but also comes to take a look, and sometimes places a bet, regardless of winning or losing, as if just to show everyone that he is very gregarious and understands that everyone should be happy after working hard for a few days. He would accompany them when they drank; he would not drink much, but he would spend his own money to buy some food and wine for everyone to eat. Things that he looked down upon in the past now seemed interesting to him—since his own path was blocked, he had no choice but to admit that others were doing the right thing. If there is a scandal among friends, he originally did not know how to deal with people, but now he will pay forty copper coins, or he will be a "public discussion son"[2]

. Not only did he pay the money, he also went to pay homage or celebrate in person, because he understood that these things were not just for wasting money, but some favors that must be fulfilled. Here people are really crying or laughing, not just making noise.

He didn't dare to touch the more than thirty yuan. After getting a piece of white cloth, he clumsily took a big needle and sewed the money into it, always keeping it close to the flesh. I don’t want to spend it, and I don’t want to buy another car. I just keep it with me as a preparation - who knows what disasters there may be in the future! Illness and unexpected disasters can come to you at any time, so you must be prepared. People are not made of iron, he understood.

It was approaching the beginning of autumn, and he took up the monthly subscription again. This time, it was easier than anything he had done in the house before; otherwise, he would not have accepted this task. He now knows how to choose a job, and has suitable monthly subscription skills; otherwise, there is nothing he can do to break up the meeting, and he is not as angry as before to go to the house. He knew that his body should be taken care of. As a coachman, if he tried to fight for his life - like he did before - he would only lose his life without getting any benefits. Experience makes people know how to be smoother, because there is only one life!

This time he worked near the Lama Temple. The owner's surname is Xia, he is in his fifties, he is knowledgeable about books and etiquette. He has a wife and twelve children at home. I recently married a concubine, and I didn't dare to let my family know about it, so I deliberately chose a secluded place to create a small family. In this small family near the Lama Temple, there are only Mr. Xia and his newly married concubine; in addition there is a maid and a coachman - Xiangzi. Xiangzi liked this very much. Let's talk about the courtyard first. There are only six rooms in the courtyard. Mr. Xia lives in three of them, one is in the kitchen, and the remaining two are used as servants. The yard is very small. There is a small, half-sized jujube tree leaning against the south wall. There are more than a dozen half-red dates hanging on the top of the tree. When Xiangzi swept the yard, he would sweep from one end to the other with two or three brooms, which was very easy. There were no flowers and plants to water, and he wanted to tidy up the jujube tree, but he knew how willful the jujube tree was, and it was unregulated, so it was inconvenient to do so.

There aren't many other jobs. Mr. Xia went to the Yamen for work in the morning and came back at five o'clock in the afternoon. Xiangzi only had to drop him off and pick him up. When he got home, Mr. Xia stopped going out, as if he was taking refuge. Mrs. Xia went out very often, but she always came back around four o'clock so that Xiangzi could pick up Mr. Xia. After picking him up, Xiangzi's work for the day would be finished. Besides, the places Mrs. Xia went to were just Dong'an Market and Zhongshan Park. After arriving, she still had a lot of time to rest. Xiangzi did all this as a joke.

Mr. Xia's hand was very tight, and he would not let go of a small coin easily. When he went in and out, he looked away, as if there was no one or anything on the street. The wife had a loose hand and went out to buy things every three days. If it was food and it didn't taste good, she would give it to the servant. If it was supplies, when it was time to buy new ones, she would give the old ones to the servant first so that she could negotiate with Mr. Xia for money. Mr. Xia's mission in life seems to be to dedicate all his energy and money to his concubine; in addition, he has no life or enjoyment. His money had to be borrowed from his concubine, and he would not spend it himself, let alone give it to others. It is said that his first wife lived in Baoding with her twelve children, and sometimes she could not get even a small amount of money from him for four or five months in a row.

Xiangzi hated this Mr. Xia: he was always hunched over, with his neck hunched, coming in and out like a thief, looking at his toes, never making a sound, not spending money, not smiling, even sitting in the car like a skinny monkey; but occasionally he would say one or two words, which would be extremely unpopular, as if everyone was a bastard, and he was the only one who was a gentleman who knew the rules and etiquette. Xiangzi doesn't like such people. But he regards "things" as "things". As long as he makes money every month, what else does he care about? ! Besides, my wife is very open-minded, and she often gets some food and daily necessities. Forget it, just think of it as pulling an unreasonable monkey. Regarding that wife, Xiangzi only regarded her as a woman who would give her some change and did not like her very much. She is much more beautiful than Xiao Fuzi, and she is moistened with powder and perfume, and wrapped in silk and satin, which is not what Xiao Fuzi can match. However, although she was beautiful and well-dressed, for some reason he thought of Huniu when he saw her; there was always something about her that looked like Huniu, not the clothes or her appearance, but some attitude or demeanor that Xiangzi couldn't find the right words to describe. He just felt that she and Huniu were, to use the word he could think of, the same thing. She is very young, twenty-two or three years old at most, but her demeanor is very sophisticated, never like a newly married woman, just like Hu Niu who has never had the shyness and gentleness of a young girl. She had a perm, wore high heels, and the clothes were cut just right to help her twist in an angular way. Even Xiangzi could see that although she was dressed so fashionably, she didn't have the magnanimity of ordinary ladies. But it's not like she was born a prostitute. Xiangzi couldn't figure out what was going on with her. He only thought she was a bit scary, as scary as Hu Niu. However, Huniu is not as young as she is, not as beautiful as she is; so Xiangzi is even more afraid of her, as if she carries with her all the power and viciousness of women he has ever experienced. He couldn't bear to look at her.

After spending some time here, he became more and more afraid of her. When Xiangzi took Mr. Xia out, he never saw how much money he spent; however, Mr. Xia also sometimes went shopping - to buy medicine at a large pharmacy. Xiangzi didn't know what kind of medicine he bought; however, whenever he bought medicine, the couple seemed to like it very much, and even Mr. After two or three days of being energetic, Mr. As soon as he saw Mr. Xia sitting in the car like a ghost, Xiangzi knew it was time to go to the pharmacy again. He didn't like Mr. Xia, but every time he went to the pharmacy, he couldn't help but feel sorry for the old skinny monkey. When Mr. Xia returned home with the medicine bag, Xiangzi thought of Huniu and couldn't explain how uncomfortable he felt. He didn't want to hold a grudge against the dead ghost, but looking at himself and Mr. Xia, he couldn't help but resent her. In any case, his body was not as strong as before, and Hu Niu should bear most of the responsibility.

He wanted to quit his job. However, it seemed outrageous to quit his job for such an irrelevant thing; while smoking the "Yellow Lion", he said to himself, "Why bother with other people's business?!"

Let this issue go, we won’t settle it for now, we’ll talk about it later.

When chrysanthemums were out of market, Mrs. Xia bought four pots of flowers, but the maid Yang Ma threw one of the pots, and they started to quarrel. Yang's mother came from the countryside and thought that flowers and plants were not important things at all. However, since she hit other people's objects, no matter how unimportant they were, she was always careless, so she didn't dare to say anything. When Mrs. The country girl was anxious and didn't know how to talk about measurements. She shook her bottom and yelled out the most rude curses. Mrs. Xia jumped around and cursed for a while, telling Ma Yang to pack up and get out immediately.

Xiangzi never came to dissuade him. His words could not dissuade a fight, let alone a fight between two women. When he heard Yang Ma scolding Mrs. At the same time, he also saw that if Yang Ma blew it, he would have to follow suit; Mrs. Xia probably wouldn't keep a servant who knew her history. After Yang Ma left, he waited to be dismissed; calculating that it would be time for him to pack up his bed when the new maid arrived. But he didn't worry about this. His experience enabled him to calmly go to work and resign without any emotion.

However, after Yang Ma left, Mrs. Xia was very polite to Xiangzi. Without a maid, she had to go to the kitchen and cook by herself. She gave Xiangzi money and taught him to go out and buy groceries. After buying it, she asked him to peel what should be peeled and what should be washed. While he peeled and washed vegetables, she cut meat and cooked rice, finding something to say to him while doing the work. She was wearing a pink sanitary robe, a pair of green trousers underneath, and a pair of white satin embroidered slippers on her feet. Xiangzi lowered his head and worked clumsily, not daring to look at her, but he wanted to look at her. The smell of her perfume often flowed into his nose strongly, as if telling him that he must look at her, like a fragrant flower that attracts bees and butterflies.

Xiangzi knew the power of women and the benefits of women; a tiger girl is enough to make anyone afraid of women and reluctant to part with them. What's more, Mrs. Xia is far beyond what Huniu can match. Xiangzi couldn't help but glance at her. If she was as scary as Huniu, she was many times more admirable than Huniu.

Two years ago, Xiangzi would never have dared to look at her like this. Now, he doesn't care much about it: First, he has been seduced by women, and he can no longer control himself. Secondly, he has gradually fallen into the trap of a "carriage driver": what ordinary coachmen think is right, he now sees as right; since his own efforts and self-denial have failed, everyone's behavior must be justified, and he has to be a "carriage driver", whether he is willing or not; being different will not work. So, picking up an advantage is what ordinary poor people think is justifiable. Why didn't Xiangzi see an advantage? He glanced at this bitch, yes, she was just a bitch! If she was willing, Xiangzi couldn't refuse. He couldn't believe she could be so mean, but what if? If she didn't move, of course Xiangzi wouldn't move; if she showed some interest first, he would have no idea. Has she shown some interest? Otherwise, why didn't you let Yang Ma go and hire someone immediately, and just ask Xiangzi to help with the cooking? Why do you wear so much perfume in the kitchen? Xiangzi didn't dare to decide or hope for anything, but he still wanted to decide something or hope for something. He seemed to be having an unreal dream. He knew it was a dream, but he was willing to continue having it. There was a kind of heat in life that forced him to admit that he was worthless, and in this worthless thing was hidden the greatest joy—perhaps the greatest distress, who cares about it!

A little hope, a little courage; a little courage stirred up great heat; a fire burned in his heart. There is nothing mean here, neither he nor she is mean, their desires are equal!

A little fear awakened his reason; a little reason extinguished the fire in his heart; he almost wanted to escape immediately. There is only suffering here, and those who take this road will surely make a fool of themselves!

Suddenly hope, suddenly fear, he felt like malaria in his heart. This was even more sad than when he met Hu Niu; at that time, he didn't know anything, just like a little bee that came out for the first time and landed on a spider web; now, he knows how to be careful and how bold to be, he inexplicably wants to flow down, and is clearly afraid of falling!

He did not look down upon this concubine, this prostitute, this beauty. She was everything and nothing. If there was something he could do to help himself, he thought, Mr. With a husband like him, she could do nothing wrong. With a master like him, it doesn't matter what he - Xiangzi - does. He became bolder.

However, she didn't care whether he looked at her or not. After she could cook, she ate alone in the kitchen; after finishing the meal, she called Xiangzi: "You go ahead and eat. You have to get rid of the guy after eating. When you pick up the husband in the second half of the day, buy the food for the night so as not to go out again. Tomorrow is Sunday and the husband is at home. I will go out to find an old lady. Do you have any acquaintances who can recommend one? Old ladies are really hard to find! Okay, eat first, don't let it get cold!"

She said it very generously and naturally. That pink sanitary robe suddenly—in Xiangzi's eyes—seemed much plainer. On the contrary, he was a little disappointed, and felt ashamed from the disappointment. He realized that he was no longer a strong person, not only a weak person, but also a bad person! After eating two bowls of rice in a daze, he felt very bored. After washing my dick, I sat down in my room and sucked in countless "Yellow Lions" in one breath!

When he went to pick up Mr. Xia in the afternoon, he hated this old skinny monkey very much for some reason. He really wanted to let go of Huanhuan and throw this old guy half to death. Only then did he realize that when he was pulling a cart in front of a house, the master's third concubine and the eldest young master didn't know why the eldest master almost poisoned him to death after the master found out. He had previously thought that the eldest young master was too young and ignorant, but now he understood why the master deserved to die. However, he did not want to kill anyone. He only felt that Mr. Xia was annoying and hateful, and there was no way to punish him. He deliberately moved the handlebar up and down, shaking the old monkey a few times. The old monkey didn't say anything, but Xiangzi felt a little uncomfortable. He had never done such a thing, and he couldn't forgive himself even if he did it occasionally for a reason. Regret made him a little indifferent to everything, so why did he deliberately make himself uncomfortable? No matter how you say it, you are a coachman and you just do good things for others. What's the use of thinking about anything else?

He calmed down and forgot about this fruitless matter; occasionally thinking about it again, he felt a little ridiculous.

The next day, Mrs. Xia went out to find the maid. I'll go out for a while and bring back a trial worker. Xiangzi gave up, but no matter what he thought in his heart, it didn't make him happy.

After lunch on Monday, Mrs. Xia sent away the old woman who was doing the job trial because she thought she was too unclean. Then, she asked Xiangzi to buy a pound of chestnuts.

After returning from buying a catty of ripe chestnuts, Xiangzi called out outside the door.

"Bring it in," she said from inside the house.

Xiangzi went in. She was applying powder in front of the mirror. She was still wearing the pink sanitary robe, but she had changed into a pair of light green underwear. Seeing Xiangzi coming in from the mirror, she quickly turned around and smiled at him. Xiangzi suddenly saw Huniu in this smile, a young and beautiful Huniu. He was dead. His courage, hope, fear, and caution were all gone, leaving only a puff of hot breath, which could be big or small, supporting his whole body. This tone made him advance as he advanced and retreat as he retreated. He no longer had any ideas.

The next night, he packed up his belongings and returned to the factory.

The thing that he was most afraid of and shameful about was now revealed to everyone like a haha ​​- he couldn't pee!

Everyone scrambled to tell him what medicine to buy or which doctor to go to. No one felt this was shameful, they all sympathetically gave him advice, and recounted their own experiences with a blush on their faces. Several young people have bought this disease with money, several middle-aged people have suffered from this symptom in vain, several people who have received monthly subscriptions have had experiences of different weights but of the same nature, several people who have received monthly subscriptions have not personally experienced this, and there are other stories about the owners that are worth telling. Xiangzi's illness made them all open their hearts and talk to him about their confidants. He had forgotten his shame, but he was not proud of it either. He endured this illness calmly, which was no different than suffering from a little cold or a little heat. When it hurt, he regretted it a little; when he felt comfortable for a while, he remembered that sweetness again. No matter what, he is not in a hurry; life experience has taught him to underestimate life, so what's the use of being anxious.

Such a little medicine, such a folk remedy, cost him more than ten dollars; the root of the disease was not eliminated. So-so, he thought he was cured and stopped taking medicine. On cloudy days or during seasonal changes, his joints would hurt, so he would take some medicine temporarily or push through without taking it seriously. If life is miserable to the end, what does the body mean? If you think about it, even a fly would be having fun in a manure pit, let alone such a big living person.

After the illness passed, he almost became a different person. His stature is still so tall, but the righteousness is gone. His shoulders are deliberately relaxed forward, his mouth is drooped, and there is a cigarette between his lips. Sometimes I put half a cigarette between my ears, not because of the convenience of the place, but just for fun[1]

. He still doesn't like to talk much, but when he wants to open his mouth, he still manages to be playful. Even if he doesn't speak smoothly and neatly, he still has so much energy. When the mind is relaxed, the body and expression become careless.

However, compared with ordinary coachmen, he is not very bad. When he sat alone, he thought of his former self, and he still wanted to be strong and was unwilling to just slip away. Although there is no use in being strong, it is not wise to destroy yourself. At this time, he thought about buying a car again. Of my more than thirty yuan, more than ten yuan have been spent on medical treatment, which is a waste! But with a base of about twenty yuan, he has more hope than others who have a completely empty gun. Thinking of this, he really wanted to throw away the half-finished box of "Yellow Lion", stop smoking and drinking, and grit his teeth to save money. From saving money, I thought of buying a car, from buying a car, I thought of Xiao Fuzi. He felt a little sorry for her. Ever since he came out of the courtyard, he never went to see her. Not only did he not get better, but he also got a lot of internal diseases!

When he met his friends, he still smoked cigarettes and drank some wine when he had the chance, completely forgetting about Xiao Fuzi. With his friends, he doesn't take the initiative to do anything, but if others want to do something, he has to accompany them. The hard work and grievances of the day can be temporarily forgotten only by talking and having fun with them. The immediate comfort drives away noble aspirations. He is willing to be happy for a while and then sleep in darkness; who doesn't like this, since life is so boring, painful and hopeless! The sores of life can only be numbed for a while by the poison of tobacco, alcohol and women. Fight poison with poison, and the poison will one day return to the heart. Who doesn't know this, but who can have a better idea to replace this? !

The more you refuse to work hard, the more you feel sorry for yourself. In the past, he was not afraid of anything, but now he will find peace and ease: he will not get out of the car when it is windy or rainy; his body is a little sore, and he will rest for two or three days. Self-pity is selfish. He refuses to lend the little money he has to others, just to keep it for himself in rainy days. Cigarettes and alcohol can be used by others, but money cannot be lent out. I am more delicate and pitiful than everyone else. The more leisure you have, the lazier you become, and you feel bored when you have nothing to do, so you need some entertainment or good food from time to time. When he thought that he shouldn't waste time and money like this, he always had a ready-made sentence in his heart, a sentence that was forged by how much experience he had: "We should have been stronger back then, was there any benefit?" No one can refute this sentence, no one can explain it; then, who can stop Xiangzi from going down? !

Laziness can make people angry. Xiangzi now knows how to glare at people. He decided not to deal with the car seat, the patrolman, or anyone else. When he worked hard, he never got justice. Now, he knows how precious his sweat is. If he can spare a drop, he will lose a drop. Don't even think about anyone trying to take advantage of him. He casually put the car down, too lazy to move, regardless of whether that was the right place to put the car. The patrolman came to intervene, but he moved his mouth without moving his body, and would stop for as long as he could. When he arrived, he saw that the car had to be moved away, and his mouth couldn't be idle, he would curse. If the patrolman refused to be scolded, then it would be okay to give him a fight. Fortunately, Xiangzi knew that he was strong, so he beat the patrolman first, and then he would not suffer any loss in going to jail. During the fight, he felt his strength and ability again, and threw all his strength into the flesh of others. He saw the light, and the sun seemed to shine especially brightly. Saving his strength to prepare for a fight, he had never even thought about it before, but now it has become a reality, and it is something that can make him happy for a while; thinking about it, how funny it is!

Not to mention being a bare-handed patrolman, he was not afraid of the cars that were roaming the streets. The car was coming, picking up all the dust on the ground. Xiangzi did not hide, no matter how loud the car's horn was, no matter how anxious the passengers were. The car had no choice but to slow down. It slowed down, and Xiangzi also avoided it, eating less dust. If the car comes from behind, he will also use this trick. He figured it out clearly. The car didn't dare to hurt people anyway, so why did he get away so early that he could teach it to pick up all the dust? Patrol officers are specially designed to clear the way for cars, lest they run too fast and bring up not much dust. Xiangzi is not a patrol officer, so cars are not allowed to run rampant. In the eyes of the patrol officers, Xiangzi is the first-class "prick head", but they dare not offend the "prick head". The laziness of a miserable person is the natural result of trying hard but failing, and the mischievousness of a miserable man contains some axioms.

He was absolutely rude about the car seat. He was pulled wherever he was told, without taking more than one step. Talk about "up" at the entrance of the alley, but teach him to pull him "inside" the entrance of the alley. That won't happen! Zuo'er stared, Xiangzi's eyes widened even more. He knew how afraid the gentlemen in western-style clothes were of getting their clothes dirty, and he also knew how tyrannical and stingy the gentlemen in western-style clothes—most of them—were. Okay, he had already prepared it; after all, he just went over and grabbed the sleeves of their fifty or sixty yuan suits, and at least gave them a big black handprint! To give them such a handprint, they still have to pay money. They know how strong that big hand is, and it has squeezed their thin arms until it hurts.

He wasn't running too slowly, but he couldn't speed up in vain. As soon as the seat was pressed, his big feet touched the ground: "Hurry up, how much does it cost?" He was not polite, what he was selling was blood and sweat. He no longer wished to reward them with a few more rewards for their kindness. You get what you pay for, so you had to make it clear before you put in any effort.

He no longer cherishes the car so much. Since the heart of buying a car has become cold, I am indifferent to other people's cars. A cart is just a cart. If you pull it, you can earn grain and cart share, and that's all. If you don't pull it, you don't have to pay the cart share. So as long as you have enough money to eat for a day, there is no need to pull it out. The relationship between people and cars is nothing more than this. Of course, he was unwilling to damage someone else's car intentionally, but he couldn't protect it with extra care. Sometimes he was unintentionally bruised by another driver. He would not jump around and make a fuss with others, but would calmly drag him back to the factory. If he should pay fifty cents, he would pay two cents and finish the job. If the factory owner doesn't agree, that's easy to handle. The final solution will be a fight. If the factory owner is willing to fight, Xiangzi will accompany him!

Experience is the fertilizer of life. The kind of experience you have will determine the kind of person you become. You cannot grow peonies in the desert. Xiangzi was completely in the wrong rut. He was neither better nor worse than other drivers, he was just a driver like a driver. In this way, he felt more comfortable than before, and others liked him; the old crow had a black side, and he didn't want to be a white-haired boy alone.

Winter is coming again, and the yellow wind blowing from the desert can freeze many people to death in one night. Listening to the wind, Xiangzi buried his head in the quilt and did not dare to get up again. It wasn't until the wind stopped the howling of wolves that he got up helplessly and couldn't decide whether to go out or rest for the day. He was too lazy to reach for the cold handlebars, fearing the choking and nauseating wind. The strong wind was afraid of sunset, and it was not until after four o'clock that the wind completely stopped, and some reddish sunset showed through the dusky sky. He steeled himself and pulled the car out. He held his hands and pressed his head against the handlebar with his chest, shaking slowly and listlessly, with half a cigarette in his mouth. After a while, it got dark, and he wanted to close two deals quickly so that he could close the car early. He didn't bother to light the lamps until the patrolmen along the road urged him four or five times before lighting them.

In front of the Drum Tower, he grabbed a seat under the lamp and headed towards the east city. He didn't even take off his cotton robe, just trotting along in a daze. He knew it wasn't decent, but if it wasn't decent, it wasn't decent. Who would give a few more cents to a decent person? This is not pulling a cart, but a gangster; even though his head is sweaty, he still refuses to take off his long clothes, so he makes do with whatever he can. Entering a small alley, a dog probably saw that the man pulling a cart through his long clothes didn't like it, so he followed him and bit him. He stopped the car, held the cloth duster upside down, and chased the dog desperately. After driving the dog away, he waited for a while to see if it dared to come back. The dog didn't dare to come back, so Xiangzi said happily: "Damn it! I thought I was afraid of you!" "Who do you think you are as a cart puller? Let me ask you!" The person in the car asked angrily.

Xiangzi's heart skipped a beat. The voice sounded familiar. It was very dark in the alley. Although the car lights were bright, they were all below and he couldn't see clearly who was in the car. The people in the car were wearing large hoods and their mouths and noses were covered in large scarves, with only two eyes exposed. Xiangzi is guessing. The person in the car spoke again: "Aren't you Xiangzi?"

Xiangzi understood that the person in the car was Mr. Liu! He banged, his whole body felt hot, and he didn't know what to do.

"Where is my daughter?"

"Dead!" Xiangzi stood there blankly, not knowing whether it was himself or another person who said these two words.

"What? Dead?"

"Dead!"

"If I fall into your fucking hands, I'm still alive?!"

Xiangzi suddenly found himself: "Come down! Come down! You are too old, you can't help me beat you; come down!"

Fourth Master Liu walked down with trembling hands. "Where is it buried? I ask you!" "I don't care!" Xiangzi picked up the car and drove away.

He walked far away and looked back. The old man - like a big black shadow - was still standing there.

The air-conditioning at night bit his face. He didn't feel cold, but felt happy. The street lamps glowed coldly, and Xiangzi felt comfortable and warm in his heart. He was sweating all over and felt thirsty and wanted to drink water. Only then did he realize that he had reached the back door. . No matter how unpalatable they are, swallow them. After eating, he wiped his mouth with the back of his hand. Where to go?

There was no hesitation, confusion, he seemed to take out his whole heart in one breath. After he finished speaking, he felt sweat on his head, and his heart felt empty, feeling empty and comfortable, as empty and comfortable as cold sweat after passing out.

Xiangzi nodded; having finished speaking, he seemed unwilling to open his mouth again. "Do you still have to pull the car?"

Xiangzi nodded again. He doesn't do anything else.

I sold my car to Mr. Zuo. If you want to come, you have to rent one. Okay?"

Xiangzi left Cao's house around eleven o'clock, which is the most lovely time of the day in winter. It was a particularly beautiful day, with no clouds in the blue sky. The sun shone down from the dry and cool air, making people feel some refreshing warmth. Looking at the woods, there was no movement at all; looking further north, you could see some watery land outside Wansheng Garden, with only a few broken cattails and reeds left on the uneven ground. , showing that he is no amateur. Why is it so quiet now? Isn't there no business here in winter?

Xiangzi sat on the edge of the kang, because he couldn't straighten his neck while standing. He was very fond of meeting her. He often heard people say that there was a "white-faced pocket" in the white house, and it must be her. The nickname "White Face Pocket" comes from her two big breasts. Xiangzi asked her straight to the point if she had seen a little lucky child, but she didn't know. Xiangzi described Xiao Fuzi's appearance, and she remembered: "Yes, there is such a person! She is not old, so she can show some white teeth. Yes, we call her Xiao Tenderrou."

"Which room is she in?" Xiangzi's eyes suddenly opened with murderous intent. "She? She's dead!" "White Face Pocket" pointed outwards, "She was hanged in the woods!"

"how?"

"After the little tender meat came here, she became very popular. But she couldn't stand it a little, and her body was quite thin. One day, when I was lighting the lamp, I still remember it really, because I was sitting at the door with two or three other girls. Alas, at this time, a wanderer came and ran into her room; she didn't like to sit with us at the door. When we first arrived, She was beaten for this when she got famous, and everyone let her stay alone in the house. Fortunately, she didn't go to see anyone else. After dinner, the guests left and ran to the woods. We didn't see anything, and no one came to the house to see her. I saw a man lying naked in the room, sleeping soundly. It turned out that he was drunk. She took off the guest's clothes, put on her own clothes, and escaped. She was so clever that she would not have been able to escape if it was dark. She disguised herself as a man and fooled everyone. Huh, as soon as she entered the tree, Lin, she was hanging there. When she was taken off, she was dead, but her tongue didn't spit out much, and her face wasn't ugly. She was still cute when she died! There was nothing going on in the woods at night, how kind it was for her not to come out to scare people..." Xiangzi walked out without waiting for her to finish. We walked to a cemetery with some pine trees planted in all directions, and there were more than a dozen grave heads among the trees. The sunlight was already very weak, and it was even dimmer in the pine forest. He sat on the ground with some hay and pine flowers. There was no sound, only the long and mournful screams of a few mountain magpies on the trees. This was definitely not Xiao Fuzi's grave, he knew, but his tears fell one after another. There is nothing left, even Xiao Fuzi has fallen into the ground! He wanted to be strong, and Xiao Fuzi wanted to be strong. He only had some useless tears left, and she was already a hanger! Once you get the chance to win a seat, you will be buried in a pile of random deaths. This is the end of a life of hard work!

Returning to the depot, he slept for two days. I never want to go to Cao's house. I don't even need to send a letter. Mr. Cao can't save Xiangzi's life. After sleeping for two days, he pulled the car out. His heart was completely blank. He no longer thought about anything or hoped for anything. He only came out to suffer for his stomach. When his stomach was full, he went to sleep. What else should he think about or hope for? Seeing a bone-thin dog waiting to eat some skins and whiskers next to the sweet potato pick, he realized that he was just like this dog, spending the whole day just to pick up some sweet potato skins and whiskers. It's all about making do with life, there's no need to think about anything else.

Man has lifted himself out of the beasts, but up to now he has driven his own kind into the beasts. Xiangzi was still in the cultural city, but he turned into a beast. Through no fault of his own at all. He stopped thinking, so even if he killed someone, he would not bear any responsibility. He no longer had hope, so he fell in a daze, into the bottomless pit. He eats, he drinks, he whores, he gambles, he is lazy, he is cunning, because he has no heart, his heart has been taken away. All that was left of him was the tall frame of flesh, waiting to fester and prepare to go to the post of random death.

Winter has passed, and the spring sunshine is nature's clothing for everyone. He sold all the cotton-padded clothes and rolls. He wants to eat well and drink well, and he does not need to save winter clothes, nor is he prepared to see winter again. He can be happy today, but die tomorrow! It doesn’t matter if it’s winter or not! Unfortunately, I'm still alive in the winter, so let's talk about it later. Originally, when he thought, he thought about a lifetime; now, he only cares about the present. Experience told him that tomorrow is just a continuation of today, and tomorrow will inherit today's grievances. He felt very happy after selling his cotton-padded clothes. What could he do with cash? Why wait for the winter when a gust of wind would choke people to death?

Slowly, he wanted to sell not only clothes, but also everything. Anything that was not needed for the time being was sold immediately. He likes to see his things turn into money and be spent by himself; if he spends it himself, it will not fall into the hands of others. This is the safest way. Sell ​​things and buy them when you need them; if you don't have the money to buy them, just don't use them. It doesn’t matter if you don’t wash your face or brush your teeth. It not only saves money, but also saves trouble. Who do you show your decency to? Wearing rags and eating pancakes and rolls with sauce and meat in their stomachs, this is true! There are good things in the belly, and even if it is dead, it will still have some oil and water, so it will not look like a starving mouse. Xiangzi, such a decent Xiangzi, turned into a thin and dirty low-class coachman. He doesn't wash his face, body, or clothes, and sometimes doesn't shave his hair for more than a month.

He doesn’t care much about his car, it’s either new or old, as long as it’s small. After the deal was brought in, with a little bit of sweetness, he threw it out halfway. If the person in the car didn't agree, he would stare and start a fight. It wouldn't be a big deal if he stayed in the police district for two days! Pulling the cart alone, he walked very slowly, feeling sorry for his own sweat. When he gets on the stroller, if he is happy, he is willing to run just to leave others behind. At this time, he is also very good at causing trouble. He can cut across other cars, make hard turns on purpose, twist the car behind him awkwardly, and give the car in front of him a cold blow. At first he thought that pulling a cart was dragging someone's life, and if he was not careful, he would be in danger of falling to death. Now, he deliberately wants to be bad; it doesn't matter if he falls to death, everyone deserves to die! He regained his taciturnity. Silently, he ate, he drank, and he broke.

Speech is how human beings exchange opinions and convey feelings. If he has no opinions and no hope, what is the use of talking? Except for bargaining, he keeps his mouth shut all day long; his mouth seems to be specially prepared for eating, drinking tea and smoking. Even when he was drunk, he would not make a sound. He would sit in a secluded place and cry. Almost every time he got drunk, he would go to the woods where Xiao Fuzi hung himself and cry; after crying, he would stay in the white house. When I woke up from drinking, my hands were cleansed by money and I was sick. He doesn't regret it; if he does regret it sometimes, he regrets why he was so strong, so cautious, and so honest in the first place. Everything that should be regretted is over, and there is nothing to regret now.

Now, if he can take advantage, that's what he will do. Smoking one more cigarette, spending a fake copper when buying things, drinking soy juice and eating a few more pickles, pulling a cart with less effort and earning one or two more coppers all made him feel satisfied. He took advantage and others suffered a loss. Yes, this is a kind of revenge! Slowly expanding this, he also learned to borrow money from his friends, but he still didn't want to pay it back; when pressed, he could act rogue. In the first year of junior high school, no one doubted him at all. They all knew that he was a decent and trustworthy person, so he borrowed money as soon as he opened his mouth. He used the remnants of this personality to borrow things everywhere, picking them up for free, and spending them as soon as he got them. When someone wants a debt, he will act very pitiful and beg for mercy; if that doesn't work, he will borrow another twenty cents, pay off the debt of fifteen cents, and drink the remaining five cents first. After going back and forth, he couldn't even borrow a copper, so he started to cheat money. He would visit all the houses he had been in before, whether it was the owner or the servant. When he met, he would make up a lie and defraud a few money. If he had no money, he would beg for some rags, and when he got the clothes, he would immediately turn into money, and the money would immediately turn into tobacco and alcohol. He lowered his head and thought about bad ideas. If he thought of a good idea, he could make more money than pulling a car for a day; he would save energy and make money, which he felt was very good value. He even went to see Cao Zhai's Gao Ma. He was waiting from a distance for Gao Ma to come out to buy things. When he saw her coming out, he rushed over in one step and called her "Sister-in-law Gao" in a very moving voice. "Oh! I'm scared to death! Who do I think you are? Xiangzi! Why are you like this?" Gao Ma's eyes widened, as if she saw a monster.

"Don't mention it!" Xiangzi lowered his head.

"Didn't you make an agreement with your husband? Why didn't you look back? I asked Lao Cheng about you, and he said he didn't see you. Where have you been? Both my husband and my wife are worried!"

"I was ill for a long time and almost died! Talk to your husband and help me, and then come back to work when I feel better!" Xiangzi said the words he had already compiled, simple and touching.

"Sir is not at home, would you please come in and meet my wife?"

"Don't worry! I'm like this! Tell me about it!"

Gao Ma took out two dollars for him: "My wife gave it to you, please take some medicine quickly!"

"Yes! Thank you, madam!" Xiangzi took the money and thought about where to develop it. As soon as Gao Ma turned around, he ran to the overpass and played for a whole day.

After slowly cleaning all the doors of the house, he tried it a second time, but this time it was no longer very effective. He saw that this road could not be long, he had to think of another idea, and he had to think of an idea that could make money easier than pulling a cart. Before, his only hope was to pull a cart; now, he hates pulling a cart. Naturally, he couldn't completely cut off the relationship with the car for the time being, but as long as there was a way to temporarily cope with three meals, he refused to touch the handlebars. His body is lazy and his ears are sharp. When there is news, he will run to the front. Whether it's a citizens' group or a petition group, he does it all if someone pays for it. Whether it was three cents or two cents, he was willing to spend a day flying flags and walking around with the crowd. He felt that this was better than pulling a cart anyway. He didn't make much money, but he didn't have to work hard. Holding a small flag, he lowered his head, held a cigarette in his mouth, and followed everyone with a half-smile without saying a word. When he has to shout a few times, he will open his mouth wide and be completely silent. He cherishes his voice. He didn't want to use force for anything, because he had tried his best before without any benefit. During this kind of flag-raising and shouting, if he encounters any danger, he will be the first to run away, and he will run very fast. He can destroy his life in his own hands and never sacrifice anything for anyone else. Those who work hard for the individual also know how to destroy the individual. These are the two ends of individualism.

Beng'er means ability and future.
